\displaystyle V^{*}_{\mathbf{R}\mathbf{k}^{\prime},m^{\prime}a^{\prime}}V_{\mathbf{R}\mathbf{k},ma}f_{\mathbf{R},m}^{\dagger}f_{\mathbf{R},m^{\prime}}\gamma_{\mathbf{k},a}\gamma_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},a^{\prime}}^{\dagger}\frac{1}{U(n_{\mathbf{R}}^{f}-1)+E_{f}-\epsilon_{\mathbf{k},a}+V_{c}(-2N_{c}-1)-E_{c}+W/N_{M}(N_{c}-N_{f}+1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle V^{*}_{\mathbf{R}\mathbf{k}^{\prime},m^{\prime}a^{\prime}}V_{\mathbf{R}\mathbf{k},ma}f_{\mathbf{R},m}^{\dagger}f_{\mathbf{R},m^{\prime}}(\delta_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}\delta_{a,a^{\prime}}-\gamma_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},a^{\prime}}^{\dagger}\gamma_{\mathbf{k},a})\frac{1}{U(n_{\mathbf{R}}^{f}-1)+E_{f}-\epsilon_{\mathbf{k},a}+V_{c}(-2N_{c}-1)-E_{c}+W/N_{M}(N_{c}-N_{f}+1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-V^{*}_{\mathbf{R}\mathbf{k}^{\prime},m^{\prime}a^{\prime}}V_{\mathbf{R}\mathbf{k},ma}f_{\mathbf{R},m}^{\dagger}f_{\mathbf{R},m^{\prime}}\gamma_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},a^{\prime}}^{\dagger}\gamma_{\mathbf{k},a}\frac{1}{U(n_{\mathbf{R}}^{f}-1)+E_{f}-\epsilon_{\mathbf{k},a}+V_{c}(-2N_{c}-1)-E_{c}+W/N_{M}(N_{c}-N_{f}+1)}

of Eq.

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-V_{\mathbf{R}\mathbf{k}^{\prime},m^{\prime}a^{\prime}}V^{*}_{\mathbf{R}\mathbf{k},ma}f_{\mathbf{R},m^{\prime}}^{\dagger}f_{\mathbf{R},m}\gamma^{\dagger}_{\mathbf{k},a}\gamma_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},a^{\prime}}\frac{1}{U(n_{\mathbf{R}}^{f}-1)+E_{f}-\epsilon_{\mathbf{k},a}+V_{c}(-2N_{c}-1)-E_{c}+W/N_{M}(N_{c}-N_{f}+1)}

\displaystyle+\delta_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}\delta_{a,a^{\prime}}V_{\mathbf{R}\mathbf{k},m^{\prime}a}V^{*}_{\mathbf{R}\mathbf{k},ma}f_{\mathbf{R},m}^{\dagger}f_{\mathbf{R},m^{\prime}}\frac{1}{U(n_{\mathbf{R}}^{f}-1)+E_{f}-\epsilon_{\mathbf{k},a}+V_{c}(-2N_{c}-1)-E_{c}+W/N_{M}(N_{c}-N_{f}+1)}

(S178)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-V_{\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}^{\prime},m^{\prime}a^{\prime}}^{*}V_{\mathbf{R}\mathbf{k},ma}\gamma^{\dagger}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},a^{\prime}}f_{\mathbf{R},m^{\prime}}\frac{1}{U(n_{\mathbf{R}}^{f}-1)+E_{f}-\epsilon_{\mathbf{k},a}+V_{c}(-2N_{c}-1)-E_{c}+W/N_{M}(N_{c}-N_{f}+1)}f_{\mathbf{R},m}^{\dagger}\gamma_{\mathbf{k},a}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-V_{\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}^{\prime},m^{\prime}a^{\prime}}^{*}V_{\mathbf{R}\mathbf{k},ma}\gamma^{\dagger}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},a^{\prime}}f_{\mathbf{R},m^{\prime}}f_{\mathbf{R},m}^{\dagger}\gamma_{\mathbf{k},a}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle V_{\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}^{\prime},m^{\prime}a^{\prime}}^{*}V_{\mathbf{R}\mathbf{k},ma}f_{\mathbf{R},m}^{\dagger}f_{\mathbf{R},m^{\prime}}\gamma^{\dagger}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},a^{\prime}}\gamma_{\mathbf{k},a}\frac{1}{U(n_{\mathbf{R}}^{f})+E_{f}-\epsilon_{\mathbf{k},a}+V_{c}(-2N_{c}+1)-E_{c}+W/N_{M}(N_{c}-N_{f}-1)}

\displaystyle-\delta_{m,m^{\prime}}V_{\mathbf{R}^{\prime}\mathbf{k}^{\prime},ma^{\prime}}^{*}V_{\mathbf{R}\mathbf{k},ma}\gamma^{\dagger}_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},a^{\prime}}\gamma_{\mathbf{k},a}\frac{1}{U(n_{\mathbf{R}}^{f})+E_{f}-\epsilon_{\mathbf{k},a}+V_{c}(-2N_{c}+1)-E_{c}+W/N_{M}(N_{c}-N_{f}-1)}

(S180)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}P_{L}(S{\hat{H}}_{1}+{\hat{H}}_{1}S^{\dagger})P_{L}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{U(n_{\mathbf{R}}^{f})+E_{f}-\epsilon_{\mathbf{k},a}+V_{c}(-2N_{c}+1)-E_{c}+W/N_{M}(N_{c}-N_{f}-1)}\bigg]P_{L}

(S181)

Since we fix the filling of
f
at each site, we can replace
n_{\mathbf{R}}^{f}
with
\nu_{f}+4
. We also replace
N_{c}
by

N_{c}=\sum_{\mathbf{k},a}\gamma_{\mathbf{k},a}^{\dagger}\gamma_{\mathbf{k},a}=\sum_{\mathbf{k},a}c_{\mathbf{k},a}^{\dagger}c_{\mathbf{k},a}=N_{M}\hat{\nu}_{c}+\sum_{\mathbf{k}}8\,.

Based on this, the pre-factors that appear in the effective Hamiltonian can be written as

\displaystyle\frac{1}{D_{1,\mathbf{k},a}[\hat{\nu}_{c},\nu_{f}]}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{U(n_{\mathbf{R}}^{f}-1)+E_{f}-\epsilon_{\mathbf{k},a}+V_{c}(-2N_{c}-1)-E_{c}+W/N_{M}(N_{c}-N_{f}+1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{U(\nu_{f}+3)+E_{f}-\epsilon_{\mathbf{k},a}+V_{c}(-2N_{M}\hat{\nu_{c}}-16\sum_{\mathbf{k}}1-1)-E_{c}+W(-\nu_{f}-4+\hat{\nu}_{c}+8\sum_{\mathbf{k}}1/N_{M}+1/N_{M})}

(S182)

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{D_{2,\mathbf{k},a}[\hat{\nu}_{c},\nu_{f}]}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{U(n_{\mathbf{R}}^{f})+E_{f}-\epsilon_{\mathbf{k},a}+V_{c}(-2N_{c}+1)-E_{c}+W/N_{M}(N_{c}-N_{f}-1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{U(\nu_{f}+4)+E_{f}-\epsilon_{\mathbf{k},a}+V_{c}(-2N_{M}\hat{\nu_{c}}-16\sum_{\mathbf{k}}1+1)-E_{c}+W(-\nu_{f}-4+\hat{\nu}_{c}+8\sum_{\mathbf{k}}1/N_{M}-1/N_{M})}P_{L}

(S183)

Using explicit formula of
E_{f},V_{c},E_{c}

in Eq.
S163
, the denominators becomes

\displaystyle D_{1,\mathbf{k},a}[\hat{\nu}_{c},\nu_{f}]=

\displaystyle U(\nu_{f}+3)-\frac{7U}{2}-\frac{8W}{N_{M}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}}1-\epsilon_{\mathbf{k},a}+\frac{V_{0}}{2\Omega_{0}N_{M}}(-2N_{M}\hat{\nu_{c}}-16\sum_{\mathbf{k}}1-1)-(-4W-\frac{8V_{0}}{\Omega_{0}N_{M}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}}1)

\displaystyle-W(\nu_{f}-\hat{\nu}_{c}-4+8\sum_{\mathbf{k}}1+1/N_{M})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(U-W)\nu_{f}+(\frac{-V_{0}}{\Omega_{0}}+W)\hat{\nu}_{c}-\epsilon_{\mathbf{k},a}-\frac{U}{2}+\frac{1}{2N_{M}}(-\frac{V_{0}}{2\Omega_{0}}-W)

and

\displaystyle D_{2,\mathbf{k},a}[\hat{\nu_{c}},\nu_{f}]=

\displaystyle=U(\nu_{f}+4)-\frac{7U}{2}-\frac{8W}{N_{M}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}}1-\epsilon_{\mathbf{k},a}+V_{c}(-2N_{M}\hat{\nu_{c}}-16\sum_{\mathbf{k}}1+1)-(-4W-\frac{8V_{0}}{\Omega_{0}N_{M}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}}1)

\displaystyle-W(\nu_{f}-\hat{\nu}_{c}+4-\sum_{\mathbf{k}}1/N_{M}-1/N_{M})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(U-W)\nu_{f}+(\frac{-V_{0}}{\Omega_{0}}+W)\hat{\nu}_{c}-\epsilon_{\mathbf{k},a}+\frac{U}{2}+\frac{1}{2N_{M}}(\frac{V_{0}}{2\Omega_{0}}-W)

We comment on the value of denominators:

•

We drop the term at the order of
1/N_{M}
which is negligibly small.

•

In the original model, there
is
a damping term, which suppresses the hybridization between
f
fermions and
c
-fermions with large momentum. Then the dominant hybridization comes from the
c
electron with momentum near
\Gamma_{M}
. These conduction electrons have small
|\epsilon_{\mathbf{k},a}|
, so we drop this term in the denominators. (A similar approximation has also been taken in the standard Kondo model.).

•

We also replace the operator
\hat{\nu}_{c}
by a real number
\nu_{c}
which represents the average filling of conduction electrons.

Taking above approximations, we can replace
D_{1/2,\mathbf{k},a}[\hat{\nu}_{c},\nu_{f}]
with real numbers
D_{1/2,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}

\displaystyle D_{1,\mathbf{k},a}[\nu_{c},\nu_{f}]\approx D_{1,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}=(U-W)\nu_{f}-\frac{U}{2}+(\frac{-V_{0}}{\Omega_{0}}+W){\nu}_{c}

\displaystyle D_{2,\mathbf{k},a}[\nu_{c},\nu_{f}]\approx D_{2,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}=(U-W)\nu_{f}+\frac{U}{2}+(\frac{-V_{0}}{\Omega_{0}}+W){\nu}_{c}

(S184)

and we define
D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}
as

\displaystyle D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}=\bigg[-\frac{1}{D_{1,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{1}{D_{2,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg]^{-1}

(S185)

We give the value at
\nu_{c}=0
,
U=58
meV,
W=48
meV

\nu_{f}

The final formula of
the
effective Hamiltonian is

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}_{eff}

\displaystyle={\hat{H}}_{0}+\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},m,a,m^{\prime},a^{\prime}}\frac{e^{i(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{k}^{\prime})\mathbf{R}}}{N_{M}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\frac{V_{\mathbf{R}\mathbf{k}^{\prime},m^{\prime}a^{\prime}}^{*}V_{\mathbf{R}\mathbf{k},ma}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}}f_{\mathbf{R},m}^{\dagger}f_{\mathbf{R},m^{\prime}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\gamma_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},a^{\prime}}^{\dagger}\gamma_{\mathbf{k},a}}

\displaystyle+\bigg[\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},m^{\prime},a}\frac{V_{\mathbf{R}\mathbf{k},m^{\prime}a^{\prime}}^{*}V_{\mathbf{R}\mathbf{k},ma}}{N_{M}}f_{\mathbf{R},m}^{\dagger}f_{\mathbf{R},m^{\prime}}\frac{1}{D_{1,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}-\sum_{\mathbf{k},m,a,a^{\prime}}V_{\mathbf{R}\mathbf{k}^{\prime},m^{\prime}a^{\prime}}^{*}V_{\mathbf{R}\mathbf{k},ma}\gamma_{\mathbf{k},a}^{\dagger}\gamma_{\mathbf{k},a^{\prime}}\frac{1}{D_{2,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg]\,.

(S186)

Since, we only consider the state with fixed fillings of
f
at each site, we drop the projection operator
P_{L}
. However, we need to remember that our Hilbert space is spanned by the states with the fixed filling of
f
-electrons
\nu_{f}
.

We now transform everything from band basis
\gamma_{\mathbf{k},m}
to conduction electron basis
c_{\mathbf{k},m}
. Using
\gamma_{\mathbf{k},a}=\sum_{a}U_{\mathbf{k},ba}^{*}c_{\mathbf{k},b}
and
V_{\mathbf{R}\mathbf{k},mn}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{N_{M}}}\sum_{m^{\prime}}e^{i\mathbf{k}\mathbf{R}}H^{fc}_{mm^{\prime}}(\mathbf{k})U_{\mathbf{k},m^{\prime}n}

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}_{eff}

\displaystyle={\hat{H}}_{0}+\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},m,a,m^{\prime},a^{\prime}}\frac{e^{i(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{k}^{\prime})\mathbf{R}}}{N_{M}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\frac{H^{fc,*}_{m^{\prime}b^{\prime}}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime})H^{fc}_{mb}(\mathbf{k})}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}}f_{\mathbf{R},m}^{\dagger}f_{\mathbf{R},m^{\prime}}c_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},b^{\prime}}^{\dagger}c_{\mathbf{k},b}

\displaystyle+\bigg[\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},,m,m^{\prime},b}\frac{H^{fc,*}_{m^{\prime}b}(\mathbf{k})H^{fc}_{mb}(\mathbf{k})}{N_{M}}f_{\mathbf{R},m}^{\dagger}f_{\mathbf{R},m^{\prime}}\frac{1}{D_{1,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}-\sum_{\mathbf{k},m,b,b^{\prime}}H^{fc}_{ma^{\prime}}(\mathbf{k})H_{ma}^{fc,*}(\mathbf{k})c_{\mathbf{k},b}^{\dagger}c_{\mathbf{k},b^{\prime}}\frac{1}{D_{2,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg]\,.

(S187)

We next go to the original labeling.

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}_{eff}

\displaystyle=\hat{H}_{0}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\eta,\eta^{\prime},s,s^{\prime}\alpha,\alpha}\sum_{a,a^{\prime}\in\{1,2\}}\frac{e^{i(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{k}^{\prime})\mathbf{R}}}{N_{M}}H^{(fc,\eta),*}_{\alpha^{\prime}a^{\prime}}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime})H^{(fc,\eta^{\prime})}_{\alpha a}(\mathbf{k}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\frac{F(|\mathbf{k}|)^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}}\bigg[f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}c_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}^{\dagger}c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[\sum_{\mathbf{k},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime},\eta,s}\sum_{a\in\{1,2\}}\frac{F(|\mathbf{k}|)^{2}H^{(fc,\eta),*}_{\alpha^{\prime}a}(\mathbf{k})H^{(fc,\eta)}_{\alpha a}(\mathbf{k})}{N_{M}D_{1,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\sum_{\mathbf{R}}}f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha^{\prime}\eta s}

\displaystyle-\sum_{\mathbf{k},\alpha,\eta,s}\sum_{a,a^{\prime}\in\{1,2\}}\frac{F(|\mathbf{k}|)^{2}H^{(fc,\eta)}_{\alpha a^{\prime}}(\mathbf{k})H_{\alpha a}^{(fc,\eta),*}}{D_{2,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}(\mathbf{k})c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}^{\dagger}c_{\mathbf{k},a^{\prime}\eta s}\bigg]\,.

(S188)

where

\displaystyle F(|\mathbf{k}|)=e^{-\lambda^{2}|\mathbf{k}|^{2}/2}

(S189)

is the damping factor of
f
-
c
hybridization (Eq.
S3
).

With normal ordering, we have

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}_{eff}={\hat{H}}_{0}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\eta,\eta^{\prime},s,s^{\prime}\alpha,\alpha}\sum_{a,a^{\prime}\in\{1,2\}}\frac{e^{i(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{k}^{\prime})\mathbf{R}}}{N_{M}}H^{(fc,\eta),*}_{\alpha^{\prime}a^{\prime}}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime})H^{(fc,\eta^{\prime})}_{\alpha a}(\mathbf{k}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\frac{F(|\mathbf{k}|)^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}}\bigg[:f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}::c_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}^{\dagger}c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}:\bigg]

\displaystyle+\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\eta,\eta^{\prime},s,s^{\prime}\alpha,\alpha}\sum_{a,a^{\prime}\in\{1,2\}}\frac{e^{i(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{k}^{\prime})\mathbf{R}}}{N_{M}}H^{(fc,\eta),*}_{\alpha^{\prime}a^{\prime}}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime})H^{(fc,\eta^{\prime})}_{\alpha a}(\mathbf{k}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\frac{|F(|\mathbf{k}|)|^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}}\bigg[\frac{|F(|\mathbf{k}|)|^{2}}{2}\delta_{\alpha,\alpha^{\prime}}\delta_{\eta,\eta^{\prime}}\delta_{s,s^{\prime}}:c_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}^{\dagger}c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}:\bigg]

\displaystyle+\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\eta,\eta^{\prime},s,s^{\prime}\alpha,\alpha}\sum_{a,a^{\prime}\in\{1,2\}}\frac{e^{i(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{k}^{\prime})\mathbf{R}}}{N_{M}}H^{(fc,\eta),*}_{\alpha^{\prime}a^{\prime}}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime})H^{(fc,\eta^{\prime})}_{\alpha a}(\mathbf{k}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\frac{|F(|\mathbf{k}|)|^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}}\bigg[:f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}:\delta_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}\delta_{a,a^{\prime}}\delta_{\eta,\eta^{\prime}}\delta_{s,s^{\prime}}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime},\eta,s}\sum_{a\in\{1,2\}}\frac{|F(|\mathbf{k}|)|^{2}H^{(fc,\eta),*}_{\alpha^{\prime}a}(\mathbf{k})H^{(fc,\eta)}_{\alpha a}(\mathbf{k})}{N_{M}D_{1,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}:f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha^{\prime}\eta s}:

\displaystyle-\sum_{\mathbf{k},\alpha,\eta,s}\sum_{a,a^{\prime}\in\{1,2\}}\frac{|F(|\mathbf{k}|)|^{2}H^{(fc,\eta)}_{\alpha a^{\prime}}(\mathbf{k})H_{\alpha a}^{(fc,\eta),*}(\mathbf{k})}{D_{2,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}:c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}^{\dagger}c_{\mathbf{k},a^{\prime}\eta s}:\bigg]

\displaystyle+\text{const}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}_{0}+\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\eta,\eta^{\prime},s,s^{\prime}\alpha,\alpha}\sum_{a,a^{\prime}\in\{1,2\}}\frac{e^{i(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{k}^{\prime})\mathbf{R}}}{N_{M}}H^{(fc,\eta),*}_{\alpha^{\prime}a^{\prime}}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime})H^{(fc,\eta^{\prime})}_{\alpha a}(\mathbf{k}){\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\frac{|F(|\mathbf{k}|)|^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}}\bigg[:f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}::c_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}^{\dagger}c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}:\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[\sum_{\mathbf{k},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime},\eta,s}\sum_{a\in\{1,2\}}\frac{|F(|\mathbf{k}|)|^{2}H^{(fc,\eta),*}_{\alpha^{\prime}a}(\mathbf{k})H^{(fc,\eta)}_{\alpha a}(\mathbf{k})}{2N_{M}}\bigg(\frac{1}{D_{1,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{1}{D_{2,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg)\sum_{\mathbf{R}}:f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha^{\prime}\eta s}:

\displaystyle-\sum_{\mathbf{k},\alpha,\eta,s}\sum_{a,a^{\prime}\in\{1,2\}}\frac{|F(|\mathbf{k}|)|^{2}H^{(fc,\eta)}_{\alpha a^{\prime}}(\mathbf{k})H_{\alpha a}^{(fc,\eta),*}(\mathbf{k})}{2}\bigg(\frac{1}{D_{1,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{1}{D_{2,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg):c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}^{\dagger}c_{\mathbf{k},a^{\prime}\eta s}:\bigg]+\text{const}

(S190)

S4.5
Effect of
{\hat{H}}_{J}
and
P_{H}{\hat{H}}_{fc}P_{H}

We have ignored the effect of
ferromagnetic exchange
coupling term
\hat{H}_{J}
and also part of the hybridization term
P_{H}{\hat{H}}_{fc}P_{H}
. We now add these terms to
{\hat{H}}_{0}

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}_{0}^{complete}={\hat{H}}_{0}+\lambda{\hat{H}}_{\lambda}

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}_{\lambda}={\hat{H}}_{J}+P_{H}{\hat{H}}_{fc}P_{H}

(S191)

with
\lambda=1
a dimensionless parameter
introduced to keep track of the
{\hat{H}}_{\lambda}
. We can perform SW transformation based on
\hat{H}^{complete}_{0}
and
\hat{H}_{1}
. The corresponding
S^{\prime}
operator needs to satisfy

\displaystyle[S^{\prime},\hat{H}^{complete}_{0}]=-\hat{H}_{1}\,.

(S192)

The analytical expression of
S^{\prime}
is complicated to find, but we can expand
S^{\prime}
in powers of
\lambda

\displaystyle S^{\prime}=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\lambda^{n}S_{n}\,.

Then we have

\displaystyle[S^{\prime},\hat{H}_{0}^{complete}]=-\hat{H}_{1}

\displaystyle\Leftrightarrow

\displaystyle[\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\lambda^{n}S_{n},\hat{H}_{0}+\lambda\hat{H}_{\lambda}]=-\hat{H}_{1}

\displaystyle\Leftrightarrow

\displaystyle[S_{0},\hat{H}_{0}]+\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\lambda^{n}}\bigg([S_{n},\hat{H}_{0}]+[S_{n-1},\hat{H}_{\lambda}]\bigg)=-\hat{H}_{1}.

We can find
S_{n}
iteratively by requiring

\displaystyle[S_{0},\hat{H}_{0}]=-\hat{H}_{1}

\displaystyle[S_{n},\hat{H}_{0}]=-[S_{n-1},\hat{H}_{\lambda}]\quad,\quad n\geq 1.

S_{0}=S
is the operator we derived previously
(Eq.
S168
)
.

The effective Hamiltonian after SW transformation now becomes

\displaystyle(\hat{H}_{0}+\hat{H}_{\lambda})+\frac{1}{2}([S^{\prime},\hat{H}_{1}])=\hat{H}_{0}+\lambda\hat{H}_{J}+\frac{1}{2}[S_{0},\hat{H}_{1}]+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\lambda^{n}[S_{n},\hat{H}_{1}]\,.

We only keep the leading-order (
\lambda^{0}
) contribution and the effective Hamiltonian is

\displaystyle\hat{H}^{\prime}_{eff}=\bigg(\hat{H}_{0}+\hat{H}_{J}+\frac{1}{2}[S,\hat{H}_{1}]\bigg)=\hat{H}_{eff}+{\hat{H}}_{J}

(S193)

where
{\hat{H}}_{eff}
is defined in Eq.
S190
.

We are only interested in the states in the low-energy space, where the filling of
f
at each site is
\nu_{f}
, so we drop
P_{H}{\hat{H}}_{fc}P_{H}
term.

S4.6
Effective Kondo model

We are now in the position to write down the effective Kondo model derived from SW transformation. Combining Eq.
S193
and Eq.
S190
, we have the following Kondo Hamiltonian

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}{\hat{H}}_{Kondo}={\hat{H}}_{eff}^{\prime}}=

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}_{0}+{\hat{H}}_{J}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\eta,\eta^{\prime},s,s^{\prime}\alpha,\alpha}\sum_{a,a^{\prime}\in\{1,2\}}\frac{e^{i(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{k}^{\prime})\mathbf{R}}}{N_{M}}H^{(fc,\eta),*}_{\alpha^{\prime}a^{\prime}}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime})H^{(fc,\eta^{\prime})}_{\alpha a}(\mathbf{k})\frac{F(|\mathbf{k}|)^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg[:f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}::c_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}^{\dagger}c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}:\bigg]

\displaystyle+\bigg[\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime},\eta,s}\sum_{a\in\{1,2\}}\frac{H^{(fc,\eta),*}_{\alpha^{\prime}a}(\mathbf{k})H^{(fc,\eta)}_{\alpha a}(\mathbf{k})F(|\mathbf{k}|)^{2}}{2N_{M}}\bigg(\frac{1}{D_{1,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{1}{D_{2,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg):f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha^{\prime}\eta s}:

\displaystyle-\sum_{\mathbf{k},\alpha,\eta,s}\sum_{a,a^{\prime}\in\{1,2\}}\frac{H^{(fc,\eta)}_{\alpha a^{\prime}}(\mathbf{k})H_{\alpha a}^{(fc,\eta),*}(\mathbf{k})F(|\mathbf{k}|)^{2}}{2}\bigg(\frac{1}{D_{1,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{1}{D_{2,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg):c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}^{\dagger}c_{\mathbf{k},a^{\prime}\eta s}:\bigg]

(S194)

We expand the hybridization matrix in powers of
\mathbf{k}
and only keep the zeroth-order and linear order terms:

\displaystyle H^{(fc,\eta)}(\mathbf{k})=\begin{bmatrix}\gamma+O(|\mathbf{k}|^{2})&v_{\star}^{\prime}(\eta k_{x}-ik_{y})+O(|\mathbf{k}|^{2})&0&0\\
v_{\star}^{\prime}(\eta k_{x}+ik_{y})+O(|\mathbf{k}|^{2})&\gamma+O(|\mathbf{k}|^{2})&0&0\end{bmatrix}

(S195)

The hybridization between
f
-fermions and
\Gamma_{1}\oplus\Gamma_{2}

c
-fermions is relatively weak and has been omitted.

Besides the four-fermion interactions term,
the
SW transformation introduces two additional fermion-bilinear term

as shown in Eq.
S194
. The first one is

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime},\eta,s}\sum_{a}\frac{F(|\mathbf{k}|)^{2}H^{(fc,\eta),*}_{\alpha^{\prime}a}(\mathbf{k})H^{(fc,\eta)}_{\alpha a}(\mathbf{k})}{2N_{M}}\bigg(\frac{1}{D_{1,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{1}{D_{2,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg):f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha^{\prime}\eta s}:

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\alpha,\eta,s}F(|\mathbf{k}|)^{2}\frac{\gamma^{2}+O(|\mathbf{k}|^{2})}{2N_{M}}\bigg(\frac{1}{D_{1,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{1}{D_{2,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg):f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha\eta s}:\approx\sum_{\mathbf{k}}F(|\mathbf{k}|)^{2}\frac{\gamma^{2}}{2}\bigg(\frac{1}{D_{1,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{1}{D_{2,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg)\nu_{f}

which is a constant. The second one corresponds to a conduction electron scattering term

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}_{cc}=

\displaystyle-\sum_{\mathbf{k},\eta,s}\sum_{a,a^{\prime}\in\{1,2\}}\frac{F(|\mathbf{k}|)^{2}H^{(fc,\eta)}_{\alpha a^{\prime}}(\mathbf{k})H_{\alpha a}^{(fc,\eta),*}(\mathbf{k})}{2}\bigg(\frac{1}{D_{1,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{1}{D_{2,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg):c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}^{\dagger}c_{\mathbf{k},a^{\prime}\eta s}:

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{\mathbf{k},\eta,s}\bigg(\frac{1}{D_{1,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{1}{D_{2,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg)F(|\mathbf{k}|)^{2}\sum_{a,a^{\prime}\in\{1,2\}}\Bigg[\begin{bmatrix}\gamma^{2}/2&\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(\eta k_{x}-ik_{y})\\
\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(\eta k_{x}+ik_{y})&\gamma^{2}/2\end{bmatrix}_{a,a^{\prime}}+O(|\mathbf{k}|^{2})\Bigg]:c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}^{\dagger}c_{\mathbf{k},a^{\prime}\eta s}:

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{k},\eta,s}\sum_{a,a^{\prime}\in\{1,2\}}[H_{cc}^{\eta}(\mathbf{k})]_{aa^{\prime}}:c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}^{\dagger}c_{\mathbf{k},a^{\prime}\eta s}:

(S196)

where
H_{cc}^{\eta}(\mathbf{k})=-\bigg(\frac{1}{D_{1,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{1}{D_{2,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg)F(|\mathbf{k}|)^{2}\bigg[\frac{\gamma^{2}}{2}\sigma_{0}+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(\eta k_{x}\sigma_{x}+k_{y}\sigma_{y})\bigg]

We define the four-fermion interaction induced by SW transformation in Eq.
S194
as Kondo interactions
{\hat{H}}_{K}
and rewrite it in a more compact form:

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}_{K}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime},a,a^{\prime},\eta,\eta^{\prime},s,s^{\prime}}e^{i(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{k}^{\prime})\mathbf{R}}\frac{H_{\alpha a}^{(fc,\eta)}(\mathbf{k})(H^{(fc,\eta^{\prime})}_{\alpha^{\prime}a^{\prime}}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}))^{*}}{N_{M}D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}:f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}::c_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}^{\dagger}c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}:

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime},a,a^{\prime},\eta,\eta^{\prime},s,s^{\prime}}e^{i(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{k}^{\prime})\mathbf{R}}F(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)\bigg[\frac{\gamma^{2}\delta_{\alpha^{\prime},a^{\prime}}\delta_{\alpha,a}}{N_{M}D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}\delta_{\alpha,a}[\eta^{\prime}k^{\prime}_{x}\sigma_{x}-k^{\prime}_{y}\sigma_{y}]_{\alpha^{\prime}a^{\prime}}}{N_{M}D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}\delta_{\alpha^{\prime},a^{\prime}}[\eta k_{x}\sigma_{x}+k_{y}\sigma_{y}]_{\alpha a}}{N_{M}D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}

\displaystyle+O(|\mathbf{k}|^{2},|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|^{2},|\mathbf{k}||\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)\bigg]:f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}::c_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}^{\dagger}c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}:

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime},a,a^{\prime},\eta,\eta^{\prime},s,s^{\prime}}e^{i(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{k}^{\prime})\mathbf{R}}F(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)\bigg[\frac{\gamma^{2}\delta_{\alpha^{\prime},a^{\prime}}\delta_{\alpha,a}}{N_{M}D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}\delta_{\alpha,a}[\eta^{\prime}k^{\prime}_{x}\sigma_{x}-k^{\prime}_{y}\sigma_{y}]_{\alpha^{\prime}a^{\prime}}}{N_{M}D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}\delta_{\alpha^{\prime},a^{\prime}}[\eta k_{x}\sigma_{x}+k_{y}\sigma_{y}]_{\alpha a}}{N_{M}D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg]

\displaystyle:f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}::c_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}^{\dagger}c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}:

(S197)

Using the U(4) momentum defined in Sec.
S1.2
and Eq.

, we rewrite
{\hat{H}}_{K}
as

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}_{K}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\alpha,\alpha^{\prime},a,a^{\prime},\eta,\eta^{\prime},s,s^{\prime}}e^{i(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{k}^{\prime})\mathbf{R}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}F(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)}\bigg[\frac{\gamma^{2}\delta_{\alpha^{\prime},a^{\prime}}\delta_{\alpha,a}}{N_{M}D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}\delta_{\alpha,a}[\eta^{\prime}k^{\prime}_{x}\sigma_{x}-k^{\prime}_{y}\sigma_{y}]_{\alpha^{\prime}a^{\prime}}}{N_{M}D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}\delta_{\alpha^{\prime},a^{\prime}}[\eta k_{x}\sigma_{x}+k_{y}\sigma_{y}]_{\alpha a}}{N_{M}D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg]

\displaystyle:f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}::c_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}^{\dagger}c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}:

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot\mathbf{R}}\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}F(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}N_{M}}\sum_{\mu\nu,\xi\xi^{\prime}}\bigg[\gamma^{2}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R})::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k}):

\displaystyle+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(k_{x}^{\prime}+i\xi^{\prime}k_{y}^{\prime}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R})::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},-\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k}):+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(k_{x}-i\xi k_{y}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R})::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},-\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k}):\bigg]

(S198)

where we have neglected the
(v_{\star}^{\prime}\gamma)^{2}
which is the second order in
\mathbf{k}
, and outsiede the scope of the Hamiltonian approximation in Eq.

.

We next use the real-space representation of

moments
as defined in Eq.

\displaystyle{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime})=\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{r}^{\prime}+i\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\cdot\mathbf{r}}{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{r},\mathbf{r}^{\prime})

Then
Eq.

becomes

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}_{K}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{r},\mathbf{r}^{\prime},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\frac{F(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}N_{M}^{2}}}e^{-i\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\cdot(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{r})+i\mathbf{k}\cdot(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{r}^{\prime})}\sum_{\mu\nu,\xi\xi^{\prime}}\bigg[\gamma^{2}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R})::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{r},\mathbf{r}^{\prime}):

\displaystyle+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(-i\partial_{r_{x}}+\xi^{\prime}\partial_{r_{y}}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R})::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},-\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{r},\mathbf{r}^{\prime}):+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(i\partial_{r^{\prime}_{x}}+\xi\partial_{r_{y}^{\prime}}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R})::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},-\xi)}(\mathbf{r},\mathbf{r}^{\prime}):\bigg]

(S199)

where the derivative is defined as

\displaystyle\partial_{r_{\alpha}}:\Sigma_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{r},\mathbf{r}^{\prime}):=\partial_{r_{\alpha}}\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}}e^{i\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{r}^{\prime}-i\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\cdot\mathbf{r}}:\Sigma_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})=\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}}(-ik^{\prime}_{\alpha}):\Sigma_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})e^{i\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{r}^{\prime}-i\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\cdot\mathbf{r}}

\displaystyle\partial_{r^{\prime}_{\alpha}}:\Sigma_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{r},\mathbf{r}^{\prime}):=\partial_{r^{\prime}_{\alpha}}\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}}e^{i\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{r}^{\prime}-i\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\cdot\mathbf{r}}:\Sigma_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})=\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}}(ik_{\alpha}):\Sigma_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})e^{i\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{r}^{\prime}-i\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\cdot\mathbf{r}}

The
\mathbf{k}
summation appearing in Eq.
S199
takes form of

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}}F(|\mathbf{k}|)e^{i\mathbf{k}\cdot(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{r}^{\prime})}\approx\frac{1}{A_{MBZ}}\int\exp(-\frac{1}{2}|\mathbf{k}|^{2}\lambda^{2})e^{i\mathbf{k}\cdot(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{r}^{\prime})}d^{2}\mathbf{k}=\frac{2\pi}{A_{MBZ}}\frac{e^{-|\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{r}^{\prime}|^{2}/\lambda^{2}/2}}{\lambda^{2}}\,.

For an arbitrary function of
\mathbf{r}
,
F(\mathbf{r})
, we notice

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{r}}F(\mathbf{r})\bigg(\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}}F(|\mathbf{k}|)e^{i\mathbf{k}\cdot(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{r}^{\prime})}\bigg)=

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{r}}F(\mathbf{r})\frac{2\pi}{A_{MBZ}}\frac{e^{-|\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{r}^{\prime}|^{2}/\lambda^{2}/2}}{\lambda^{2}}\approx\frac{1}{\Omega_{0}}\int_{\mathbf{r}}F(\mathbf{r})\frac{2\pi}{A_{MBZ}}\frac{e^{-|\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{r}^{\prime}|^{2}/\lambda^{2}/2}}{\lambda^{2}}

For small
\lambda=0.3375a_{M}
, we can approximate
F(\mathbf{r})
with its value at
\mathbf{R}
. Inserting above equations into Eq.
S199
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{r}}F(\mathbf{r})\bigg(\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}}F(|\mathbf{k}|)e^{i\mathbf{k}\cdot(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{r}^{\prime})}\bigg)\approx

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\Omega_{0}}F(\mathbf{R})\int_{\mathbf{r}}\frac{2\pi}{A_{MBZ}}\frac{e^{-|\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{r}^{\prime}|^{2}/\lambda^{2}/2}}{\lambda^{2}}=F(\mathbf{R})=\sum_{\mathbf{r}}\delta_{\mathbf{r},\mathbf{R}}F(\mathbf{r})

Finally, we have the following approximated real-space expression of
{\hat{H}}_{K}

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}_{K}\approx

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},,\xi,\xi^{\prime}}e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot\mathbf{R}}\frac{1}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}N_{M}}

\displaystyle\sum_{\mu\nu,\xi\xi^{\prime}}\bigg[\gamma^{2}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R}):+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(k_{x}^{\prime}-i\xi^{\prime}k_{y}^{\prime}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi-\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R}):+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(k_{x}+i\xi k_{y}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,-\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R}):\bigg]:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k}):

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{R},\xi,\xi^{\prime}}\frac{\gamma^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R})::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\mathbf{R}):

\displaystyle+\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{r},\xi,\xi^{\prime}}\frac{\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\delta_{\mathbf{r},\mathbf{R}}\bigg[:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi-\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R}):(i\partial_{r_{x}}+\xi^{\prime}\partial_{r_{y}}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\mathbf{r}):+:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,-\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R}):(-i\partial_{r_{x}}+\xi\partial_{r_{y}}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{r},\mathbf{R}):\bigg]

(S200)

In summary, we have the following two four-fermion interactions: Kondo coupling
{\hat{H}}_{K}
and
ferromagnetic exchange

{\hat{H}}_{J}
:

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}_{K}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{r},\mathbf{r}^{\prime},\xi,\xi^{\prime}}\frac{\delta_{\mathbf{r},\mathbf{R}}\delta_{\mathbf{r}^{\prime},\mathbf{R}}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\sum_{\mu\nu}\bigg[\gamma^{2}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R}):

\displaystyle+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi-\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R}):(i\partial_{r_{x}^{\prime}}+\xi^{\prime}\partial_{r_{y}^{\prime}})+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,-\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R}):(-i\partial_{r_{x}}+\xi\partial_{r_{y}})\bigg]:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},\xi)}(\mathbf{r},\mathbf{r}^{\prime}):

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}_{J}=

\displaystyle(-J)\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mu\nu,\xi}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R})::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime\prime},\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\mathbf{R})

(S201)

Here, we also provide the interactions in the momentum space (
\mathbf{k}
-space of
c
-electrons)

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}_{K}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot\mathbf{R}}\frac{{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}F(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}N_{M}}\sum_{\mu\nu,\xi\xi^{\prime}}\bigg[\gamma^{2}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R})::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k}):

\displaystyle+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(k_{x}^{\prime}+i\xi^{\prime}k_{y}^{\prime}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R})::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},-\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k}):+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(k_{x}-i\xi k_{y}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R})::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},-\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k}):\bigg]

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}_{J}=

\displaystyle-J\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\xi}\frac{e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot\mathbf{R}}}{N_{M}}\sum_{\mu\nu,\xi}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R})::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime\prime},\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k}):

(S202)

The one-body scattering term from SW transformation is given in Eq.
S196
, and is listed below

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}_{cc}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{k},\eta,s}\sum_{a,a^{\prime}\in\{1,2\}}[H_{cc}^{\eta}(\mathbf{k})]_{aa^{\prime}}:c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}^{\dagger}c_{\mathbf{k},a^{\prime}\eta s}:

\displaystyle H_{cc}^{\eta}(\mathbf{k})=

\displaystyle-\bigg(\frac{1}{D_{1,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{1}{D_{2,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg)F(|\mathbf{k}|)^{2}\bigg[\frac{\gamma^{2}}{2}\sigma_{0}+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(\eta k_{x}\sigma_{x}+k_{y}\sigma_{y})\bigg]

(S203)

The effective Kondo model now reads

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}_{Kondo}={\hat{H}}_{0}+{\hat{H}}_{K}+{\hat{H}}_{J}+{\hat{H}}_{cc}\,.

(S204)

Here, we also want to mention that the model is defined with respect to the Hilbert space with the filling of

at each site. We estimate the coupling strength here

S5
RKKY interactions at
M=0

Based on the Kondo model defined in Eq.
S204
, we derive the corresponding RKKY interactions by integrating out conduction electrons.

The action of the effective Hamiltonian
{\hat{H}}_{eff}
(Eq.
S204
) can be separated into

\displaystyle S=S_{0}+S_{1}

\displaystyle S_{0}=S_{f}+S_{c}\quad,\quad S_{1}=S_{K}+S_{J}+S_{cc}\,.

S_{f}
and
S_{c}
denote the effective action of
f
- and
c
- electrons that have been introduced in Eq.
S113
and Eq.
S114
and are also given below

\displaystyle S_{f}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\mathbf{R},\alpha\eta s}\int_{0}^{\beta}d\tau f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}(\tau)\partial_{\tau}f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha\eta s}(\tau)d\tau+i\sum_{\mathbf{R}}\int_{0}^{\beta}\lambda_{\mathbf{R}}(\tau)[\sum_{\alpha\eta s}f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}(\tau)f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha\eta s}(\tau)-4-\nu_{f})]d\tau

\displaystyle S_{c}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}\int_{0}^{\beta}d\tau c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}^{\dagger}(\tau)\partial_{\tau}c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}(\tau)d\tau+\int_{0}^{\beta}\sum_{\eta,s,a,a^{\prime},\mathbf{k}}\bigg(H^{(c,\eta)}_{a,a^{\prime}}+(-\mu+W\nu_{f}+\frac{V_{0}}{\Omega_{0}}\nu_{c})\delta_{a,a^{\prime}}\bigg)c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}^{\dagger}(\tau)c_{\mathbf{k},a^{\prime}\eta s}(\tau)d\tau

\displaystyle-\frac{V_{0}N_{M}}{2\Omega_{0}}\nu_{c}^{2}-(W\nu_{f}+\frac{V_{0}}{\Omega_{0}}\nu_{c})\sum_{\mathbf{k}}8

(S205)

where we have introduced Lagrangian multiplier
\lambda_{\mathbf{R}}(\tau)
to fix the filling of
f
-electrons.
{\hat{H}}_{U}
and chemical potential term of
f
-electrons are a constants after fixing the filling of
f
-electrons and have been omitted. We also treat
{\hat{H}}_{V}
via a mean-field approximation.

S_{K},S_{J},S_{cc}
are defined as

\displaystyle S_{K}=\int_{0}^{\beta}{\hat{H}}_{K}(\tau)d\tau,\quad S_{J}=\int_{0}^{\beta}{\hat{H}}_{J}(\tau)d\tau,\quad S_{cc}=\int_{0}^{\beta}{\hat{H}}_{cc}(\tau)d\tau\,.

(S206)

{\hat{H}}_{K}(\tau),{\hat{H}}_{J}(\tau),{\hat{H}}_{cc}(\tau)
take the same for as
{\hat{H}}_{K}

(Eq.

),
{\hat{H}}_{J}
(Eq.
S202
),
{\hat{H}}_{cc}
(Eq.

), but with

,

replaced by

, where
\tau
denotes imaginary time.

We now integrate out
the

c
-electrons in the partition functions and perform the cumulant expansion:
\langle e^{O}\rangle=\exp\bigg(\langle O\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\langle O^{2}\rangle-\frac{1}{2}\langle O\rangle^{2}+...\bigg)
.

\displaystyle Z=

\displaystyle\int D[f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}(\tau),f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha\eta s}(\tau),\lambda_{\mathbf{R}}(\tau)]\int D[c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}^{\dagger}(\tau),c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}(\tau)]e^{-S_{0}-S_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int D[f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}(\tau),f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha\eta s}(\tau),\lambda_{\mathbf{R}}(\tau)]e^{-S_{f}}\langle e^{-S_{1}}\rangle_{0}

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle\int D[f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}(\tau),f_{\mathbf{R},\alpha\eta s}(\tau),\lambda_{\mathbf{R}}(\tau)]\exp\bigg[-S_{f}-\langle S_{1}\rangle_{0}+\frac{1}{2}\langle S_{1}^{2}\rangle_{0}-\frac{1}{2}(\langle S_{1}\rangle_{0})^{2}+...\bigg]

where

\displaystyle\langle O\rangle_{0}:=\frac{1}{Z_{0}}\int D[c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}^{\dagger}(\tau),c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}(\tau)]Oe^{-S_{c}}\quad,\quad Z_{0}:=\int D[c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}^{\dagger}(\tau),c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}(\tau)]e^{-S_{c}}

The effective actions of
f
fermions then becomes

\displaystyle S_{eff}=S_{f}+\langle S_{K}+S_{J}+S_{cc}\rangle_{0}-\bigg[\frac{\langle S_{J}^{2}\rangle_{0,con}}{2}+\frac{\langle S_{K}^{2}\rangle_{0,con}}{2}+\langle S_{J}S_{K}\rangle_{0,con}+\frac{\langle S_{cc}^{2}\rangle_{0,con}}{2}+\langle(S_{K}+S_{J})S_{cc}\rangle_{0,con}\bigg]

(S207)

with
\langle AB\rangle_{0,con}=\langle AB\rangle_{0}-\langle A\rangle_{0}\langle B\rangle_{0}
.
We treat
S_{1}
as perturbation, since all terms in
S_{1}
are either generated from SW transformation that is proportional to
\gamma^{2},(v_{\star}^{\prime})^{2},\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}
, or coming from the ferromagnetic exchange coupling which is proportional to
J
. We derive the effective action
at
M=0
and integer filling
\nu=0,-1,-2
with
\nu_{f}=\nu,\nu_{c}=0
.

We first note that,
S_{cc}
(see Eq.
S206
and Eq.
S203
) does not contain any
f
electron operators, so
\langle S_{cc}\rangle_{0}
and
\langle S_{cc}^{2}\rangle_{0,con}
only give constant contributions.

As for the linear term:
\langle S_{K}+S_{J}\rangle_{0}
,
\langle S_{K}\rangle_{0}
gives

\displaystyle\langle S_{K}\rangle_{0}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\beta}\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},,\xi,\xi^{\prime}}e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot\mathbf{R}}\frac{1}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}N_{M}}\langle:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k},\tau):\rangle_{0}

\displaystyle\sum_{\mu\nu,\xi\xi^{\prime}}\bigg[\gamma^{2}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau):+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(k_{x}^{\prime}-i\xi^{\prime}k_{y}^{\prime}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi-\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau):+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(k_{x}+i\xi k_{y}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,-\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau):\bigg]d\tau

(S208)

We need to calculate
\langle:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k},\tau):\rangle_{0}
.
Due to the flat
U(4)
symmetry, only
\mu\nu=00
component can be non-zero
for the same reason proved around Eq.
S84
. The momentum conservation requires
\mathbf{k}^{\prime}=\mathbf{k}
. Due to imaginary-time translational symmetry, we only need to consider
\tau=0

\displaystyle\langle:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{k},0)\rangle=\delta_{\mu,0}\delta_{\nu,0}\langle\sum_{m}\psi_{\mathbf{k},m}^{c^{\prime},\xi,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{k},m}^{c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime}}\rangle_{0}=\delta_{\mu,0}\delta_{\nu,0}\delta_{\xi,\xi^{\prime}}\langle\sum_{m}\psi_{\mathbf{k},m}^{c^{\prime},\xi,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{k},m}^{c^{\prime},\xi}\rangle_{0}\,

(S209)

The above quantity is just the filling of conduction electrons in orbitals
1,2
with index
\xi
. At
\nu_{c}=0
, this becomes zero. Then
\langle S_{K}\rangle_{0}=0
.
Another linear term is

\langle S_{J}\rangle_{0}
. We have

\displaystyle\langle S_{J}\rangle_{0}=\int_{0}^{\beta}(-J)\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mu\nu,\xi}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\tau):\langle:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime\prime},\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\mathbf{R},\tau)\rangle_{0}d\tau

(S210)

which requires the calculation of

\langle:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime\prime},\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\rangle_{0}
.
Similarly, the only non-zero component corresponds to
\mu\nu=00
and
\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k}
. We have

\displaystyle\langle:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime\prime},\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{k},0)\rangle=\langle\sum_{m}\psi_{\mathbf{k},m}^{c^{\prime\prime},\xi,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{k},m}^{c^{\prime\prime},\xi}\rangle_{0}\,

(S211)

which is the filling of conduction electrons in orbitals
3,4
with index
\xi
. At
\nu_{c}=0
, this becomes zero. Then
\langle S_{J}\rangle_{0}=0
.

The remaining terms give the following spin-spin interaction

\displaystyle S_{RKKY}=-\bigg[\frac{\langle S_{J}^{2}\rangle_{0,con}}{2}+\frac{\langle S_{K}^{2}\rangle_{0,con}}{2}+\langle S_{J}S_{K}\rangle_{0,con}\bigg]

(S212)

The effective action becomes

\displaystyle S_{eff}=S_{f}+S_{RKKY}\,.

(S213)

Before calculating
S_{RKKY}
, here, we first introduce the following single-particle Green’s function at
M=0
, as also derived in Sec.
S9
,

\displaystyle G_{aa^{\prime},\eta}(\mathbf{k},\tau)=-\langle T_{\tau}c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}(\tau)c_{\mathbf{k},a^{\prime}\eta s}^{\dagger}(0)\rangle

(S214)

where
T_{\tau}
denotes time ordering. We further introduce
g_{0}(\mathbf{k},\tau)
and
g_{2,\eta}(\mathbf{k},\tau)
(Sec.
S9
, Eq.
S548
) as

\displaystyle g_{0}(\mathbf{k},\tau)=G_{11,\eta}(\mathbf{k},\tau)=G_{22,\eta}(\mathbf{k},\tau)=G_{33,\eta}(\mathbf{k},\tau)=G_{44,\eta}(\mathbf{k},\tau)

\displaystyle g_{2,\eta}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k})=G_{13,\eta}(\mathbf{k},\tau)=G^{*}_{24,\eta}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)=G^{*}_{31,\eta}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)=G_{42,\eta}(\mathbf{k},\tau)

(S215)

S5.1
-\langle(S_{K}+S_{J})S_{cc}\rangle_{0,con}

We now prove

-\langle(S_{K}+S_{J})S_{cc}\rangle_{0,con}
only produces a constant contribution. For
\langle S_{K}S_{cc}\rangle_{0,con}
, we have

\displaystyle\langle S_{K}S_{cc}\rangle_{0,con}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\mathbf{k}_{2},\xi,\xi^{\prime}}e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot\mathbf{R}}\sum_{\eta_{2}s_{2}}\sum_{a_{2},a_{2}^{\prime}\in 1,2}\sum_{\mu\nu}\frac{1}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}N_{M}}[H_{cc}^{\eta_{2}}(\mathbf{k}_{2})]_{a_{2}a_{2}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\bigg[\gamma^{2}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R}):+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(k_{x}^{\prime}-i\xi^{\prime}k_{y}^{\prime}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi-\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau_{1}):+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(k_{x}+i\xi k_{y}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,-\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau_{1}):\bigg]

\displaystyle\langle:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k},\tau_{1})::c_{\mathbf{k}_{2},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})c_{\mathbf{k}_{2},a_{2}^{\prime}\eta_{2}s_{2}}(\tau_{2}):\rangle_{0,con}d\tau_{1}d\tau_{2}

(S216)

We need to evaluate
\langle:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k},\tau_{1})::c_{\mathbf{k}_{2},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})c_{\mathbf{k}_{2},a_{2}^{\prime}\eta_{2}s_{2}}(\tau_{2}):\rangle_{0,con}
. For
\xi^{\prime}=\xi
, we have

\displaystyle\langle:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi,\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k},\tau_{1})::c_{\mathbf{k}_{2},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})c_{\mathbf{k}_{2},a_{2}^{\prime}\eta_{2}s_{2}}(\tau_{2}):\rangle_{0,con}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\eta,\eta^{\prime},s,s^{\prime}}\sum_{a,a^{\prime}\in\{1,2\}}\frac{1}{2}A_{a\eta s,a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}^{\mu\nu}\delta_{\xi,(-1)^{a+1}\eta}\delta_{\xi,(-1)^{a^{\prime}+1}\eta^{\prime}}\langle:c_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},a\eta s}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})c_{\mathbf{k},a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}(\tau_{1})::c_{\mathbf{k}_{2},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})c_{\mathbf{k}_{2},a_{2}^{\prime}\eta_{2}s_{2}}(\tau_{2}):\rangle_{0,con}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\eta,\eta^{\prime},s,s^{\prime}}\sum_{a,a^{\prime}\in\{1,2\}}\frac{1}{2}A_{a\eta s,a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}^{\mu\nu}\delta_{\xi,(-1)^{a+1}\eta}\delta_{\xi,(-1)^{a^{\prime}+1}\eta^{\prime}}\delta_{a,a_{2}^{\prime}}\delta_{s,s_{2}}\delta_{s^{\prime},s_{2}}\delta_{\eta,\eta_{2}}\delta_{\eta^{\prime},\eta_{2}}\delta_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}_{2}}\delta_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\mathbf{k}_{2}}

\displaystyle(-1)g_{0}(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2},\mathbf{k}_{2})g_{0}(\tau_{2}-\tau_{1},\mathbf{k}_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2\delta_{\mu,0}\delta_{\nu,0}g_{0}(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2},\mathbf{k}_{2})g_{0}(\tau_{2}-\tau_{1},\mathbf{k}_{2})\delta_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}_{2}}\delta_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\mathbf{k}_{2}}\delta_{a_{2},a_{2}^{\prime}}

(S217)

where we use Wick’s theorem and the definition of Green’s function in Eq.
S215
.
As for
\xi^{\prime}=-\xi=-1
, we have

\displaystyle\langle:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},-1,+1)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k},\tau_{1})::c_{\mathbf{k}_{2},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})c_{\mathbf{k}_{2},a_{2}^{\prime}\eta_{2}s_{2}}(\tau_{2}):\rangle_{0,con}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\eta,\eta^{\prime},s,s^{\prime}}\sum_{a,a^{\prime}\in\{1,2\}}\frac{1}{2}A_{a\eta s,a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}^{\mu\nu}\delta_{-1,(-1)^{a+1}\eta}\delta_{+1,(-1)^{a^{\prime}+1}\eta^{\prime}}\langle:\eta^{\prime}c_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},a\eta s}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})c_{\mathbf{k},a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}(\tau_{1})::c_{\mathbf{k}_{2},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})c_{\mathbf{k}_{2},a_{2}^{\prime}\eta_{2}s_{2}}(\tau_{2}):\rangle_{0,con}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{a,a^{\prime}\in\{1,2\}}\frac{1}{2}A_{a_{2}^{\prime}\eta_{2}s_{2},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}^{\mu\nu}\delta_{-1,(-1)^{a_{2}^{\prime}+1}\eta_{2}}\delta_{+1,(-1)^{a_{2}+1}\eta_{2}}\eta_{2}(-1)g_{0}(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2},\mathbf{k}_{2})g_{2}(\tau_{2}-\tau_{1},\mathbf{k}_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}g_{0}(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2},\mathbf{k}_{2})g_{2}(\tau_{2}-\tau_{1},\mathbf{k}_{2})\sum_{a,a^{\prime}\in\{1,2\}}\frac{1}{2}A_{a_{2}^{\prime}\eta_{2}s_{2},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}^{\mu\nu}\delta_{-1,(-1)^{a_{2}^{\prime}+1}\eta_{2}}\delta_{+1,(-1)^{a_{2}+1}\eta_{2}}\eta_{2}(-1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}g_{0}(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2},\mathbf{k}_{2})g_{2}(\tau_{2}-\tau_{1},\mathbf{k}_{2})\times 0}=0

(S218)

where we use Wick’s theorem and the definition of Green’s function in Eq.
S215
.
Similarly, for
\xi^{\prime}=-\xi=+1

\displaystyle\langle:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},+1,+-1)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k},\tau_{1})::c_{\mathbf{k}_{2},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})c_{\mathbf{k}_{2},a_{2}^{\prime}\eta_{2}s_{2}}(\tau_{2}):\rangle_{0,con}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\eta,\eta^{\prime},s,s^{\prime}}\sum_{a,a^{\prime}\in\{1,2\}}\frac{1}{2}A_{a\eta s,a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}^{\mu\nu}\delta_{1,(-1)^{a+1}\eta}\delta_{-1,(-1)^{a^{\prime}+1}\eta^{\prime}}\langle:\eta c_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},a\eta s}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})c_{\mathbf{k},a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}(\tau_{1})::c_{\mathbf{k}_{2},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})c_{\mathbf{k}_{2},a_{2}^{\prime}\eta_{2}s_{2}}(\tau_{2}):\rangle_{0,con}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{a,a^{\prime}\in\{1,2\}}\frac{1}{2}A_{a_{2}^{\prime}\eta_{2}s_{2},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}^{\mu\nu}\delta_{1,(-1)^{a_{2}^{\prime}+1}\eta_{2}}\delta_{-1,(-1)^{a_{2}+1}\eta_{2}}\eta(-1)g_{0}(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2},\mathbf{k}_{2})g_{2}(\tau_{2}-\tau_{1},\mathbf{k}_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}g_{0}(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2},\mathbf{k}_{2})g_{2}(\tau_{2}-\tau_{1},\mathbf{k}_{2})\sum_{a,a^{\prime}\in\{1,2\}}\frac{1}{2}A_{a_{2}^{\prime}\eta_{2}s_{2},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}^{\mu\nu}\delta_{1,(-1)^{a_{2}^{\prime}+1}\eta_{2}}\delta_{-1,(-1)^{a_{2}+1}\eta_{2}}\eta(-1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}g_{0}(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2},\mathbf{k}_{2})g_{2}(\tau_{2}-\tau_{1},\mathbf{k}_{2})\times 0}=0

(S219)

where we use Wick’s theorem and the definition of Green’s function in Eq.
S215

Combining Eq.
S216
, Eq.
S217
, Eq.
S218
and Eq.
S219
,

we have

\displaystyle\langle S_{K}S_{cc}\rangle_{0,con}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\mathbf{k}_{2}\xi}e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot\mathbf{R}}\sum_{\eta_{2}s_{2}}\sum_{a_{2},a_{2}^{\prime}\in 1,2}\sum_{\mu\nu}\frac{1}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}N_{M}}[H_{cc}^{\eta_{2}}(\mathbf{k}_{2})]_{a_{2}a_{2}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\bigg[\gamma^{2}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R}):+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(k_{x}^{\prime}-i\xi k_{y}^{\prime}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi-\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\tau_{1}):+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(k_{x}+i\xi k_{y}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,-\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\tau_{1}):\bigg]

\displaystyle\langle:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi,\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k},\tau_{1})::c_{\mathbf{k}_{2},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})c_{\mathbf{k}_{2},a_{2}^{\prime}\eta_{2}s_{2}}(\tau_{2}):\rangle_{0,con}d\tau_{1}d\tau_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\xi}\sum_{\eta_{2}s_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}\in 1,2}\sum_{\mu\nu}\frac{1}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}N_{M}}[H_{cc}^{\eta_{2}}(\mathbf{k}_{2})]_{a_{2}a_{2}}

\displaystyle\bigg[\gamma^{2}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{00}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R}):+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(k_{x}-i\xi k_{y}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{00}^{(f,\xi-\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\tau_{1}):+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(k_{x}+i\xi k_{y}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{00}^{(f,-\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\tau_{1}):\bigg]

\displaystyle(-2)g_{0}(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2},\mathbf{k})g_{0}(\tau_{2}-\tau_{1},\mathbf{k})d\tau_{1}d\tau_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bigg[\int_{0}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k}}\sum_{\eta_{2}s_{2}}\sum_{a_{2}\in 1,2}\sum_{\mu\nu}\frac{1}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}N_{M}}[H_{cc}^{\eta_{2}}(\mathbf{k}_{2})]_{a_{2}a_{2}}\gamma^{2}(-1)g_{0}(\tau_{1}-\tau_{2},\mathbf{k})g_{0}(\tau_{2}-\tau_{1},\mathbf{k})\,.

(S220)

We note that
g_{0}(\mathbf{k},\tau)
only depends on
|\mathbf{k}|
and
\tau
(Sec.
S9
, Eq.
S557
).
Then the terms proportional to
\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}
vanish after
\mathbf{k}
summation due to the linear-
k
factor.
We find
\langle S_{K}S_{cc}\rangle_{0,con}
is a constant.

For
\langle S_{J}S_{cc}\rangle_{0,con}
, we have

\displaystyle\langle S_{J}S_{cc}\rangle_{0,con}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\mathbf{k}_{2},\xi}e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot\mathbf{R}}\sum_{\eta_{2}s_{2}}\sum_{a_{2},a_{2}^{\prime}\in 1,2}\sum_{\mu\nu}\frac{-J}{N_{M}}[H_{cc}^{\eta_{2}}(\mathbf{k}_{2})]_{a_{2}a_{2}^{\prime}}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\tau):

\displaystyle\langle:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime\prime},\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k},\tau):c_{\mathbf{k}_{2},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})c_{\mathbf{k}_{2},a_{2}^{\prime}\eta_{2}s_{2}}(\tau_{2}):\rangle_{0,con}d\tau_{1}d\tau_{2}

(S221)

We need to evaluate
\langle:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime\prime},\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k},\tau):c_{\mathbf{k}_{2},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})c_{\mathbf{k}_{2},a_{2}^{\prime}\eta_{2}s_{2}}(\tau_{2}):\rangle_{0,con}
, which gives

\displaystyle\langle:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime\prime},\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k},\tau):c_{\mathbf{k}_{2},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})c_{\mathbf{k}_{2},a_{2}^{\prime}\eta_{2}s_{2}}(\tau_{2}):\rangle_{0,con}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\eta,\eta^{\prime},s,s^{\prime}}\sum_{a,a^{\prime}\in\{3,4\}}B^{\mu\nu}_{a\eta s,a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}\delta_{\xi,(-1)^{a+1}\eta}\delta_{\xi^{\prime},(-1)^{a^{\prime}+1}\eta^{\prime}}\langle:c_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},a\eta s}^{\dagger}(\tau_{1})c_{\mathbf{k},a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}(\tau_{1})::c_{\mathbf{k}_{2},a_{2}\eta_{2}s_{2}}^{\dagger}(\tau_{2})c_{\mathbf{k}_{2},a_{2}^{\prime}\eta_{2}s_{2}}(\tau_{2}):\rangle_{0,con}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{\eta,\eta^{\prime},s,s^{\prime}}\sum_{a,a^{\prime}\in\{1,2\}}B^{\mu\nu}_{a\eta s,a^{\prime}\eta^{\prime}s^{\prime}}\delta_{\xi,(-1)^{a+1}\eta}\delta_{\xi^{\prime},(-1)^{a^{\prime}+1}\eta^{\prime}}\delta_{\eta,\eta_{2}}\delta_{\eta^{\prime},\eta_{2}}\delta_{s,s_{2}}\delta_{s^{\prime},s_{2}}\delta_{a+2,a_{2}^{\prime}}\delta_{a^{\prime}+2,a_{2}}\delta_{a,a^{\prime}}

\displaystyle(-1)\delta_{\mathbf{k}_{2},\mathbf{k}}\delta_{\mathbf{k}_{2},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}|g_{2,\eta}(\mathbf{k}_{2},\tau_{1}-\tau_{2})|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{\mu,0}\delta_{\nu,0}(-\frac{1}{2})\delta_{\xi,(-1)^{a_{2}+1}\eta_{2}}\delta_{a_{2},a_{2}^{\prime}}\delta_{\mathbf{k}_{2},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}\delta_{\mathbf{k}_{2},\mathbf{k}}|g_{2,\eta}(\mathbf{k},\tau_{1}-\tau_{2})|^{2}

(S222)

where we find only
\mu=0,\nu=0
components can be non-zero, due to structures of
B^{\mu\nu}
(Eq.
S14
).
Then combine Eq.
S221
and Eq.
S222
, we find

\displaystyle\langle S_{J}S_{cc}\rangle_{0,con}=\bigg[\int_{0}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k}}\sum_{a_{2},\eta}\frac{-J}{N_{M}}[H_{cc}^{\eta}]_{a_{2},a_{2}}|g_{2,\eta}(\mathbf{k},\tau_{1}-\tau_{2})|^{2}(-\frac{1}{2})\tau_{1}d\tau_{2}\bigg]\nu_{f}

which is a constant.

S5.2
-\langle S_{K}S_{J}\rangle_{0,con}

We then calculate
-\langle S_{K}S_{J}\rangle_{0,con}
:

\displaystyle-\langle S_{K}S_{J}\rangle_{0,con}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{-\beta/2}^{\beta/2}\int_{-\beta/2}^{\beta/2}\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{R}_{2},\mathbf{r},\mathbf{r}^{\prime}}\frac{J}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\delta_{\mathbf{r},\mathbf{R}}\delta_{\mathbf{r}_{2},\mathbf{R}}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}^{(f,\xi_{2}\xi_{2})}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):

\displaystyle\sum_{\mu\nu,\xi\xi^{\prime}}\sum_{\mu_{2}\nu_{2},\xi_{2}\xi_{2}}\bigg[\gamma^{2}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau):+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(i\partial_{r_{x}^{\prime}}+\xi^{\prime}\partial_{r_{y}^{\prime}}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi-\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau):+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(-i\partial_{r_{x}}+\xi\partial_{r_{y}}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,-\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau):\bigg]

\displaystyle\langle:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{r},\mathbf{r}^{\prime},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime\prime},\xi_{2}\xi_{2})}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):\rangle_{0,con}d\tau d\tau_{2}

As we derived in Sec.
S11
, Eq.
S583
, we represent
\langle:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{r},\mathbf{r}^{\prime},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime\prime},\xi_{2}\xi_{2})}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):\rangle_{0,con}
with single-particle Green’s function and find

\displaystyle-\langle S_{K}S_{J}\rangle_{0,con}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{-\beta/2}^{\beta/2}\int_{-\beta/2}^{\beta/2}\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{R}_{2},\mathbf{r},\mathbf{r}^{\prime}}\frac{J}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\sum_{\mu\nu,\xi}\bigg[\gamma^{2}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\tau):+(v_{\star}^{\prime})^{2}(-i\partial_{r_{x}}+\xi\partial_{r_{y}})(i\partial_{r_{x}^{\prime}}+\xi^{\prime}\partial_{r_{y}^{\prime}}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,-\xi-\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\tau):

\displaystyle+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(i\partial_{r_{x}^{\prime}}+\xi^{\prime}\partial_{r_{y}^{\prime}}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi-\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\tau):+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(-i\partial_{r_{x}}+\xi\partial_{r_{y}}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,-\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\tau):\bigg]:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):

\displaystyle(-1)g^{*}_{2,\xi}(\mathbf{r}^{\prime}-\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau-\tau_{2})g_{2,\xi}(\mathbf{r}-\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau-\tau_{2})d\tau d\tau_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{-\beta/2}^{\beta/2}\int_{-\beta/2}^{\beta/2}\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{R}_{2},\mathbf{r},\mathbf{r}^{\prime}}(-J)\bigg(-\frac{1}{D_{1,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{1}{D_{2,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg)\sum_{\mu\nu,\xi}\bigg[

\displaystyle\gamma^{2}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):g^{*}_{2,\xi}(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau-\tau_{2})g_{2,\xi}(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau-\tau_{2})

\displaystyle+(v_{\star}^{\prime})\gamma:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi-\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):[(i\partial_{R_{x}}+\xi\partial_{R_{y}})g^{*}_{2,\xi}(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau-\tau_{2})][g_{2,\xi}(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau-\tau_{2})]

\displaystyle+(v_{\star}^{\prime})\gamma:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,-\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):[g^{*}_{2,\xi}(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau-\tau_{2})][(-i\partial_{R_{x}}+\xi\partial_{R_{y}})g_{2,\xi}(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau-\tau_{2})\bigg]d\tau d\tau_{2}

where the Green’s function is defined in Sec.
S9
, Eq.
S546
and Eq.
S548
.

Using the analytical expression of single-particle Green’s function derived in
Sec.
S9
, Eq.
S557
and Eq.
S558
at zero temperature and infinite momentum cutoff, we have

\displaystyle-\langle S_{K}S_{J}\rangle_{0,con}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{R}_{2}}(-J)\bigg(-\frac{1}{D_{1,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{1}{D_{2,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg)\sum_{\mu\nu,\xi}\bigg[

\displaystyle\gamma^{2}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):\frac{\pi^{2}|\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2}|^{2}}{A_{MBZ}^{2}(|v_{\star}(\tau-\tau_{2})|^{2}+|\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2}|^{2})^{3}}

\displaystyle+(v_{\star}^{\prime})\gamma:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi-\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):\frac{-3\pi^{2}|\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2}|^{2}}{A_{MBZ}^{2}(|v_{\star}(\tau-\tau_{2})|^{2}+|\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2}|^{2})^{4}}\bigg(\xi(R_{y}-R_{2,y})+i(R_{x}-R_{2,x})\bigg)

\displaystyle+(v_{\star}^{\prime})\gamma:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,-\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):\frac{-3\pi^{2}|\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2}|^{2}}{A_{MBZ}^{2}(|v_{\star}(\tau-\tau_{2})|^{2}+|\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2}|^{2})^{4}}\bigg(\xi(R_{y}-R_{2,y})-i(R_{x}-R_{2,x}\bigg)\bigg]d\tau d\tau_{2}

(S223)

S5.3
-\frac{1}{2}\langle S_{K}^{2}\rangle_{0,con}

We now calculate
-\frac{1}{2}\langle S_{K}^{2}\rangle_{0,con}
:

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\langle S_{K}^{2}\rangle_{0,con}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{-\beta/2}^{\beta/2}\int_{-\beta/2}^{\beta/2}\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{r},\mathbf{r}^{\prime}}\sum_{\mathbf{R}_{2},\mathbf{r}_{2},\mathbf{r}^{\prime}_{2}}\frac{1}{2D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}\delta_{\mathbf{r},\mathbf{R}}\delta_{\mathbf{r}^{\prime},\mathbf{R}}\delta_{\mathbf{r}_{2},\mathbf{R}_{2}}\delta_{\mathbf{r}^{\prime}_{2},\mathbf{R}_{2}}

\displaystyle\sum_{\mu\nu,\xi\xi^{\prime}}\bigg[\gamma^{2}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau):+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(i\partial_{r_{x}^{\prime}}+\xi^{\prime}\partial_{r_{y}^{\prime}}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi-\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau):+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(-i\partial_{r_{x}}+\xi\partial_{r_{y}}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,-\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau):\bigg]

\displaystyle\sum_{\mu_{2}\nu_{2},\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime}_{2}}\bigg[\gamma^{2}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}^{(f,\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime}_{2})}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(i\partial_{r_{2,x}^{\prime}}+\xi_{2}^{\prime}\partial_{r_{2,y}^{\prime}}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}^{(f,\xi_{2}-\xi_{2}^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(-i\partial_{r_{2,x}}+\xi_{2}\partial_{r_{2,y}}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}^{(f,-\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime}_{2})}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):\bigg]

\displaystyle\langle:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},\xi)}(\mathbf{r},\mathbf{r}^{\prime},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime}_{2},\xi_{2})}(\mathbf{r}_{2},\mathbf{r}^{\prime}_{2},\tau_{2}):\rangle_{0,con}d\tau d\tau_{2}

We rewrite the above equations with single-particle Green’s function as derived in
Sec.
S11
, Eq.
S576

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\langle S_{K}^{2}\rangle_{0,con}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{-\beta/2}^{\beta/2}\int_{-\beta/2}^{\beta/2}\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{r},\mathbf{r}^{\prime}}\sum_{\mathbf{R}_{2},\mathbf{r}_{2},\mathbf{r}^{\prime}_{2}}\sum_{\xi,\xi^{\prime}}\frac{1}{2D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}\delta_{\mathbf{r},\mathbf{R}}\delta_{\mathbf{r}^{\prime},\mathbf{R}}\delta_{\mathbf{r}_{2},\mathbf{R}_{2}}\delta_{\mathbf{r}^{\prime}_{2},\mathbf{R}_{2}}

\displaystyle\sum_{\mu\nu}\bigg[\gamma^{2}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau):+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(i\partial_{r_{x}^{\prime}}+\xi^{\prime}\partial_{r_{y}^{\prime}}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi-\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau):+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(-i\partial_{r_{x}}+\xi\partial_{r_{y}}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,-\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau):\bigg]

\displaystyle\bigg[\gamma^{2}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(i\partial_{r_{2,x}^{\prime}}+\xi\partial_{r_{2,y}^{\prime}}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi^{\prime}-\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(-i\partial_{r_{2,x}}+\xi^{\prime}\partial_{r_{2,y}}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}^{(f,-\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):\bigg]

\displaystyle(-1)g_{0}(\mathbf{r}_{2}^{\prime}-\mathbf{r},\tau_{2}-\tau)g_{0}(\mathbf{r}^{\prime}-\mathbf{r}_{2},\tau-\tau_{2})d\tau d\tau_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{-\beta/2}^{\beta/2}\int_{-\beta/2}^{\beta/2}\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{r},\mathbf{r}^{\prime}}\sum_{\mathbf{R}_{2},\mathbf{r}_{2},\mathbf{r}^{\prime}_{2}}\sum_{\xi,\xi^{\prime}}\frac{1}{2D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}\sum_{\mu\nu}

\displaystyle\Bigg[:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):\gamma^{4}g_{0}(\mathbf{R}_{2}-\mathbf{R},\tau_{2}-\tau)g_{0}(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau-\tau_{2})

\displaystyle+:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,-\xi^{\prime}-\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):2\gamma^{2}(v_{\star}^{\prime})^{2}[(i\partial_{x}+\xi\partial_{y})g_{0}(\mathbf{R}_{2}-\mathbf{R},\tau_{2}-\tau)][(i\partial_{x}-\xi^{\prime}\partial_{y})g_{0}(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau-\tau_{2})]

\displaystyle+:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi^{\prime}-\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):2\gamma^{3}v_{\star}^{\prime}(i\partial_{R_{x}}+\xi\partial_{R_{y}})g_{0}(\mathbf{R}_{2}-\mathbf{R},\tau_{2}-\tau)]g_{0}(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau-\tau_{2})

\displaystyle+:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,-\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):2\gamma^{3}v_{\star}^{\prime}g_{0}(\mathbf{R}_{2}-\mathbf{R},\tau_{2}-\tau)(i\partial_{R_{x}}-\xi\partial_{R_{y}})g_{0}(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau-\tau_{2})]\bigg]d\tau d\tau_{2}

(S224)

where the Green’s function is defined in Sec.
S9
, Eq.
S546
and Eq.
S548
.

Here, we mention that we expand Kondo interaction in powers of
\mathbf{k}
(note that the hybridization matrix has been expanded in powers of
\mathbf{k}
in Eq.
S198
). Thus,
\gamma^{4},\gamma^{3}v_{\star}^{\prime}
correspond to zeroth and linear-order terms and are kept. In addition, we notice that
\gamma^{2}(v_{\star}^{\prime})^{2}
term in Eq.
S224
provide leading-order contributions in the interaction channel
:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,-\xi^{\prime}-\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):
. In other words, even if we expand to Kondo interaction to higher orders in
\mathbf{k}
, the current
\gamma^{2}(v_{\star}^{\prime})^{2}
term still gives the leading order contributions and should be kept.

At zero temperature, we utilize the analytical expression of single-particle Green’s function derived in
Sec.
S9
, Eq.
S557
and obtain

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\langle S_{K}^{2}\rangle_{0,con}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{R}_{2}}\sum_{\xi,\xi^{\prime}}\frac{1}{2D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}\sum_{\mu\nu}\Bigg[-:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):\gamma^{4}\frac{\pi^{2}}{A_{MBZ}^{2}}\frac{v_{\star}^{2}|\tau-\tau_{2}|^{2}}{\bigg(|v_{\star}|^{2}\tau^{2}+|\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2}|^{2}\bigg)^{3}}

\displaystyle-:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,-\xi^{\prime}-\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):\frac{9\gamma^{2}(v_{\star}^{\prime})^{2}\pi^{2}|v_{\star}(\tau-\tau_{2})|^{2}}{A_{MBZ}^{2}\bigg(v_{\star}^{2}\tau^{2}+|\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2}|^{2}\bigg)^{5}}(i(R_{x}-R_{2,x})+\xi(R_{y}-R_{2,y}))

(S225)

\displaystyle(-i(R_{x}-R_{2,x})+\xi^{\prime}(R_{y}-R_{2,y}))

\displaystyle+:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi^{\prime}-\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):\frac{6\gamma^{3}v_{\star}^{\prime}\pi^{2}|v_{\star}(\tau-\tau)|^{2}\bigg(i(R_{2,x}-R_{x})+\xi(R_{2,y}-R_{y})\bigg)}{A_{MBZ}^{2}\bigg(v_{\star}^{2}\tau^{2}+|\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2}|^{2}\bigg)^{4}}

\displaystyle+:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,-\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):\frac{6\gamma^{3}v_{\star}^{\prime}\pi^{2}|v_{\star}(\tau-\tau)|^{2}\bigg(-i(R_{2,x}-R_{x})+\xi(R_{2,y}-R_{y})\bigg)}{A_{MBZ}^{2}\bigg(v_{\star}^{2}\tau^{2}+|\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2}|^{2}\bigg)^{4}}\bigg]d\tau d\tau_{2}

(S226)

S5.4
-\frac{1}{2}\langle S_{J}^{2}\rangle_{0,con}

Finally, we calculate
-\frac{1}{2}\langle S_{J}^{2}\rangle_{0,con}
.

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\langle S_{J}^{2}\rangle_{0,con}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{J^{2}}{2}\int_{-\beta/2}^{\beta/2}\int_{-\beta/2}^{\beta/2}\sum_{\mu\nu\mu_{2}\nu_{2},\xi\xi_{2},\mathbf{R},\mathbf{R}_{2}}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}^{(f,\xi_{2}\xi_{2})}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):\langle::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime\prime},\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\mathbf{R},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}^{(c^{\prime\prime},\xi_{2}\xi_{2})}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\mathbf{R},\tau_{2}):d\tau d\tau_{2}

Expressing in terms of single-particle Green’s function as we derived in
Sec.
S11
, Eq.
S577
, we have

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\langle S_{J}^{2}\rangle_{0,con}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{J^{2}}{2}\int_{-\beta/2}^{\beta/2}\int_{-\beta/2}^{\beta/2}\sum_{\mu\nu,\xi,\mathbf{R},\mathbf{R}_{2}}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):(-1)g_{0}(\mathbf{R}_{2}-\mathbf{R},\tau_{2}-\tau)g_{0}(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau-\tau_{2})d\tau d\tau_{2}

where the Green’s function is defined in Sec.
S9
, Eq.
S546
and Eq.
S548
.
Using the analytical expression of single-particle Green’s function at zero temperature and infinite momentum cutoff as derived in

Sec.
S9
, Eq.
S557
, we have

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\langle S_{J}^{2}\rangle_{0,con}=-\frac{J^{2}}{2}\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\int_{-\beta/2}^{\beta/2}\sum_{\mu\nu,\xi,\mathbf{R},\mathbf{R}_{2}}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):\frac{\pi^{2}|v_{\star}(\tau-\tau_{2})|^{2}}{A_{MBZ}^{2}\bigg[(v_{\star}(\tau-\tau_{2}))^{2}+|\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2}|^{2}\bigg]^{3}}d\tau d\tau_{2}

(S227)

S5.5
RKKY interactions

We sum over all three terms in Eq.
S223
, Eq.
S226
, Eq.
S227
, and find the analytical formula of
S_{RKKY}=-\langle S_{K}S_{J}\rangle_{0,con}-\frac{1}{2}\langle S_{K}^{2}\rangle_{0,con}-\frac{1}{2}\langle S_{J}^{2}\rangle_{0,con}
:

\displaystyle S_{RKKY}=\int\int\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{R}_{2},\mu\nu}\bigg[\sum_{\xi\xi^{\prime}}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):\chi_{0}(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau-\tau_{2})

\displaystyle+\sum_{\xi}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):\chi_{1}(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau-\tau_{2})+\sum_{\xi}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,-\xi-\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):\chi_{2}(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau-\tau_{2})

\displaystyle+\sum_{\xi}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,-\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):\chi_{3,\xi}(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau-\tau_{2})+:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi-\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):\chi_{4,\xi}(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau-\tau_{2})

\displaystyle+\sum_{\xi}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi-\xi)}(\mathbf{R},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi-\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2}):\chi_{5,\xi}(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau-\tau_{2})\bigg]d\tau d\tau_{2}

(S228)

where

\displaystyle\chi_{0}(\mathbf{R},\tau)=

\displaystyle-\frac{\gamma^{4}}{2D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}\frac{\pi^{2}|v_{\star}\tau|^{2}}{A_{MBZ}^{2}\bigg(|v_{\star}\tau|^{2}+|\mathbf{R}|^{2}\bigg)^{3}}

\displaystyle\chi_{1}(\mathbf{R},\tau)=

\displaystyle\frac{-J\gamma^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\frac{\pi^{2}|\mathbf{R}|^{2}}{A_{MBZ}^{2}(|v_{\star}\tau|^{2}+|\mathbf{R}|^{2})^{3}}-\frac{J^{2}}{2}\frac{\pi^{2}|v_{\star}\tau|^{2}}{A_{MBZ}^{2}\bigg[|v_{\star}\tau|^{2}+|\mathbf{R}|^{2}\bigg]^{3}}

\displaystyle\chi_{2}(\mathbf{R},\tau)=

\displaystyle-\frac{9\gamma^{2}(v_{\star}^{\prime})^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}\frac{\pi^{2}|v_{\star}\tau|^{2}|\mathbf{R}|^{2}}{A_{MBZ}^{2}\bigg(|v_{\star}\tau|^{2}+|\mathbf{R}|^{2}\bigg)^{5}}

\displaystyle\chi_{3,\xi}(\mathbf{R},\tau)=

\displaystyle\frac{-3Jv_{\star}^{\prime}\gamma}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\frac{\pi^{2}|\mathbf{R}|^{2}}{A_{MBZ}^{2}(|v_{\star}\tau|^{2}+|\mathbf{R}|^{2})^{4}}\bigg(-\xi R_{y}+iR_{x}\bigg)+\frac{3\gamma^{3}v_{\star}^{\prime}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}\frac{\pi^{2}|v_{\star}\tau|^{2}\bigg(-\xi R_{y}+iR_{x}\bigg)}{A_{MBZ}^{2}\bigg(v_{\star}^{2}\tau^{2}+|\mathbf{R}|^{2}\bigg)^{4}}

\displaystyle\chi_{4,\xi}(\mathbf{R},\tau)=

\displaystyle\gamma\frac{3Jv_{\star}^{\prime}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\frac{\pi^{2}|\mathbf{R}|^{2}}{A_{MBZ}^{2}(|v_{\star}\tau|^{2}+|\mathbf{R}|^{2})^{4}}\bigg(\xi R_{y}+iR_{x}\bigg)+\frac{3\gamma^{3}v_{\star}^{\prime}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}\frac{\pi^{2}|v_{\star}(\tau-\tau)|^{2}\bigg(-\xi R_{y}-iR_{x}\bigg)}{A_{MBZ}^{2}\bigg(|v_{\star}\tau|^{2}+|\mathbf{R}|^{2}\bigg)^{4}}

\displaystyle\chi_{5,\xi}(\mathbf{R},\tau)=

\displaystyle-\frac{9\gamma^{2}(v_{\star}^{\prime})^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}A}\frac{\pi^{2}|v_{\star}\tau|^{2}}{A_{MBZ}^{2}\bigg(v_{\star}^{2}\tau^{2}+|\mathbf{R}|^{2}\bigg)^{5}}\bigg[[R_{x}^{2}-R_{y}^{2}]-2i\xi R_{x}R_{y}]\bigg]

(S229)

The corresponding RKKY interactions can be derived by taking the zero-frequency contribution of
\chi
in Eq.
S229
, which leads to the following RKKY Hamiltonian

\displaystyle H_{RKKY}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{R}_{2},\mu\nu}\sum_{\xi\xi^{\prime}}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R})::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2}):J^{RKKY}_{0}(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2})

\displaystyle+\sum_{\xi}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R})::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2}):J^{RKKY}_{1}(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2})+\sum_{\xi}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R})::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,-\xi-\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2}):J^{RKKY}_{2}(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2})

\displaystyle+\sum_{\xi}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R})::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,-\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2}):J^{RKKY}_{3,\xi}(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2})+:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R}):{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi-\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2}):J^{RKKY}_{4,\xi}(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2})

\displaystyle+\sum_{\xi}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi-\xi)}(\mathbf{R})::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi-\xi)}(\mathbf{R}_{2}):J^{RKKY}_{5,\xi}(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2})

(S230)

where the RKKY interactions are defined as

\displaystyle J_{0}^{RKKY}=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\chi_{0}(\mathbf{R},\tau))d\tau,\quad J_{1}^{RKKY}=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\chi_{1}(\mathbf{R},\tau))d\tau,\quad J_{2}^{RKKY}=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\chi_{2}(\mathbf{R},\tau))d\tau

\displaystyle J_{3,\xi}^{RKKY}=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\chi_{3,\xi}(\mathbf{R},\tau))d\tau,\quad J_{4,\xi}^{RKKY}=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\chi_{4,\xi}(\mathbf{R},\tau))d\tau,\quad J_{5,\xi}^{RKKY}=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\chi_{5,\xi}(\mathbf{R},\tau))d\tau

(S231)

The analytical expressions of RKKY interactions are given below

\displaystyle J^{RKKY}_{0}(\mathbf{R})=\frac{-\pi^{3}}{|v_{\star}|A_{MBZ}^{2}}\bigg[\frac{\gamma^{4}}{16D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}\bigg]\frac{1}{|\mathbf{R}|^{3}}

\displaystyle J^{RKKY}_{1}(\mathbf{R})=-\frac{\pi^{3}}{8A_{MBZ}^{2}|v_{\star}|}\bigg[\frac{3J\gamma^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{J^{2}}{2}\bigg]\frac{1}{|\mathbf{R}|^{3}}

\displaystyle J^{RKKY}_{2}(\mathbf{R})=-\frac{\pi^{3}}{A_{MBZ}^{2}|v_{\star}|}\frac{45\gamma^{2}(v_{\star}^{\prime})^{2}}{128D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}\frac{1}{\mathbf{R}^{5}}

\displaystyle J^{RKKY}_{3,\xi}(\mathbf{R})=-\frac{3\pi^{3}}{16A_{MBZ}^{2}|v_{\star}|}\bigg[\frac{5v_{\star}^{\prime}\gamma J}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{v_{\star}^{\prime}\gamma^{3}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}\bigg]\frac{(\xi R_{y}-iR_{x})}{|\mathbf{R}|^{5}}

\displaystyle J^{RKKY}_{4,\xi}(\mathbf{R})=-\frac{3\pi^{3}}{16A_{MBZ}^{2}|v_{\star}|}\bigg[\frac{5v_{\star}^{\prime}\gamma J}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{v_{\star}^{\prime}\gamma^{3}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}\bigg]\frac{(\xi R_{y}+iR_{x})}{|\mathbf{R}|^{5}}

\displaystyle J^{RKKY}_{5,\xi}(\mathbf{R})=-\frac{\pi^{3}}{A_{MBZ}^{2}|v_{\star}|}\frac{45\gamma^{2}(v_{\star}^{\prime})^{2}}{128D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}\frac{[R_{x}^{2}-R_{y}^{2}]-2i\xi R_{x}R_{y}}{|\mathbf{R}|^{7}}

(S232)

We also comment that RKKY interactions diverge at
R\rightarrow 0
. During the derivation of RKKY interactions, when we perform momentum integral, we have set the cutoff of momentum integral to infinity to obtain an analytical expression. This introduces a short-distance divergence. The divergence can be cured by introducing a short-distance cutoff (
a_{M}
). In practice, we can replace
|\mathbf{R}|
in the denominators of Eq.
S232
by
\sqrt{|\mathbf{R}|^{2}+a_{M}^{2}}
.

Finally, we provide the Fourier transformation of
J^{RKKY}_{0/1/2/3,\xi/4,\xi}(\mathbf{k})=\sum_{\mathbf{R}}J^{RKKY}_{0/1/2/3,\xi/4,\xi}(\mathbf{R})e^{-i\mathbf{k}\cdot\mathbf{R}}
using the Fourier transformation derived in Sec.
S12
, Eq.
S585
. During the Fourier transformation, we also introduce a short-distance cutoff
a_{M}
(moiré lattice constant)

RKKY interactions in the momentum space now take the form of

\displaystyle J^{RKKY}_{0}(\mathbf{k})=

\displaystyle\frac{-\pi^{3}}{|v_{\star}|a_{M}A_{MBZ}}\frac{\gamma^{4}}{64D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}(I_{0}(ka_{M})-L_{0}(ka_{M}))

\displaystyle J^{RKKY}_{1}(\mathbf{k})=

\displaystyle-\frac{\pi^{3}}{32A_{MBZ}a_{M}|v_{\star}|}\bigg[\frac{3J\gamma^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{J^{2}}{2}\bigg](I_{0}(ka_{M})-L_{0}(ka_{M}))

\displaystyle J^{RKKY}_{2}(\mathbf{k})=

\displaystyle-\frac{\pi^{2}}{A_{MBZ}|v_{\star}|a_{M}^{3}}\frac{45\gamma^{2}(v_{\star}^{\prime})^{2}}{3072D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}\frac{1}{ka_{M}}\bigg[3\pi(-2+k^{2}a_{M}^{2})L_{1}(ka_{M})+qa_{M}\bigg(4qa_{M}+3\pi I_{0}(qa_{M})

\displaystyle-3\pi qa_{M}I_{1}(qa_{M})-3\pi L_{2}(qa_{M})\bigg)\bigg]

\displaystyle J^{RKKY}_{3,\xi}(\mathbf{k})=

\displaystyle-\frac{3\pi^{2}}{32A_{MBZ}^{2}|v_{\star}|a_{M}^{2}}\bigg[\frac{5v_{\star}^{\prime}\gamma J}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{v_{\star}^{\prime}\gamma^{3}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}\bigg]\frac{1-e^{-ka_{M}}(1+ka_{M})}{k^{2}a_{M}^{2}}(-i\xi k_{y}-k_{x})

\displaystyle J^{RKKY}_{4,\xi}(\mathbf{k})=

\displaystyle-\frac{3\pi^{2}}{32A_{MBZ}^{2}|v_{\star}|a_{M}^{2}}\bigg[\frac{5v_{\star}^{\prime}\gamma J}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{v_{\star}^{\prime}\gamma^{3}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}\bigg]\frac{1-e^{-ka_{M}}(1+ka_{M})}{k^{2}a_{M}^{2}}(-i\xi k_{y}+k_{x})

\displaystyle J^{RKKY}_{5,\xi}(\mathbf{k})=

\displaystyle\frac{\pi^{2}}{A_{MBZ}^{2}|v_{\star}|a_{M}}\frac{45\gamma^{2}(v_{\star}^{\prime})^{2}}{256D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}ka_{M}\bigg[-\frac{1}{30}+\frac{\pi}{4k^{3}a_{M}^{3}}\bigg(I_{2}(ka_{M})+ka_{M}I_{3}(ka_{M})\bigg)

\displaystyle-\frac{\pi}{4k^{3}a_{M}^{3}}\bigg(L_{2}(ka_{M})+ka_{M}L_{3}(ka_{M})\bigg)\bigg](k_{x}^{2}-k_{y}^{2}+2i\xi k_{x}k_{y})

(S233)

where
I_{n}(x)
is the modified Bessel function of the second kind
Abramowitz et al. 1988
,
L_{n}(x)
is the modified Struve function
Abramowitz et al. 1988
.

We also provide the long-wavelength behavior of RKKY by expanding in powers of
k
to second order

\displaystyle J^{RKKY}_{0}(\mathbf{k})\approx\frac{-\pi^{3}}{|v_{\star}|A_{MBZ}a_{M}}\bigg[\frac{\gamma^{4}}{64D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}\bigg](1-\frac{2}{\pi}a_{M}k+\frac{1}{4}a_{M}^{2}k^{2})

\displaystyle J^{RKKY}_{1}(\mathbf{k})\approx-\bigg(\frac{3\pi^{3}}{4A_{MBZ}|v_{\star}|}\frac{J\gamma^{2}}{2D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{\pi^{3}}{16|v_{\star}|A_{MBZ}}J^{2}\bigg)\frac{1}{4a_{M}}(1-\frac{2}{\pi}a_{M}k+\frac{1}{4}a_{M}^{2}k^{2})

\displaystyle J^{RKKY}_{2}(\mathbf{k})\approx-\frac{\pi^{3}}{8A_{MBZ}|v_{\star}|a_{M}^{3}}\bigg[\frac{45\gamma^{2}(v_{\star}^{\prime})^{2}}{128D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}\bigg](1-\frac{1}{4}(ka_{M})^{2})

\displaystyle J^{RKKY}_{3,\xi}(\mathbf{k})\approx\frac{\pi^{3}}{A_{MBZ}|v_{\star}|a_{M}}\bigg[\frac{15v_{\star}^{\prime}\gamma J}{16D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{3v_{\star}^{\prime}\gamma^{3}}{16D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}\bigg](\frac{1}{2}-\frac{ka_{M}}{3})(i\xi k_{y}+k_{x})

\displaystyle J^{RKKY}_{4,\xi}(\mathbf{k})\approx\frac{\pi^{3}}{A_{MBZ}|v_{\star}|a_{M}}\bigg[\frac{15v_{\star}^{\prime}\gamma J}{16D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{3v_{\star}^{\prime}\gamma^{3}}{16D^{2}_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg](\frac{1}{2}-\frac{ka_{M}}{3})(i\xi k_{y}-k_{x})

\displaystyle J^{RKKY}_{5,\xi}(\mathbf{k})\approx\frac{\pi^{3}}{A_{MBZ}|v_{\star}|a_{M}}\frac{45\gamma^{2}(v_{\star}^{\prime})^{2}}{8192D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}(k_{x}^{2}-k_{y}^{2}+2i\xi k_{x}k_{y})

(S234)

S6
Ground state of
f
-moments

In this section, we solve the RKKY Hamiltonian (Eq.
S230
) and find the corresponding ground state at
\nu=0,-1,-2
,
M=0
. We will consider both
v_{\star}^{\prime}=0
where the system has a
U(4)\times U(4)
symmetry, and
v_{\star}^{\prime}\neq 0
where the system only has a flat
U(4)
symmetry.

S6.1
Ground state at
v_{\star}^{\prime}=0,M=0

Based on the RKKY Hamiltonian we derived in Eq.
S110
, we calculate the ground state. We first consider the case of
v_{\star}^{\prime}=0
where we have
U(4)\times U(4)
symmetry. The RKKY interactions at
v_{\star}^{\prime}=0
are
