S322
can be written as

\displaystyle\sum_{\xi_{2}^{\prime}}M(\mathbf{q})_{\xi_{2},\xi_{2}^{\prime}}u^{I}_{21,-\xi_{2}^{\prime}}(\mathbf{q})=E_{\mathbf{q}}^{I}u^{I}_{21,-\xi_{2}}(\mathbf{q})

(S324)

There are two excitation modes with dispersions

\displaystyle E^{1,2}_{\mathbf{q}}=

\displaystyle 2[J_{0}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q})-J_{0}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q}_{2}=0)+J_{1}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q})-J_{1}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q}_{2}=0)]-J^{RKKY}_{2}(\mathbf{q}=0)

\displaystyle\pm\sqrt{(J^{RKKY}_{2}(\mathbf{q}=0))^{2}+|J_{3,+}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q})|^{2}}

(S325)

At
\mathbf{q}=0
, two excitation modes are

\displaystyle E_{\mathbf{q}=0}^{1}=-2J_{2}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q}=0)\quad,\quad E_{\mathbf{q}=0}^{2}=0

(S326)

with
E_{\mathbf{q}=0}^{2}
gives the Goldstone modes.

S7.3.3
Half-empty sector

We next discuss the half-empty sector with
(i,j)\in\{(3,2),(4,2)\}
.
Combining Eq.
S308
, Eq.
and also the constraints
S318
, we construct following eigenequations (where
i=3,4
) with eigenvalue
E_{\mathbf{q}}^{I}

\displaystyle[{\hat{H}}_{RKKY},O_{i2,\mathbf{q}}^{I}]|\psi_{0}\rangle=\sum_{\mathbf{R}}\sum_{\xi_{1},\xi_{1}^{\prime}}u^{I}_{i2,\xi_{1}^{\prime}+}(\mathbf{q})M(\mathbf{q})_{\xi_{1},\xi_{1}^{\prime}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},i}^{f,\xi_{1},{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},2}^{f,+}\frac{e^{i\mathbf{q}\cdot\mathbf{R}}}{N_{M}}|\psi_{0}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E_{\mathbf{q}}^{I}\sum_{\mathbf{R}}u^{I}_{i2,\xi_{1}+}(\mathbf{q})\psi_{\mathbf{R},i}^{f,\xi_{2},{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},2}^{f,+}\frac{e^{i\mathbf{q}\cdot\mathbf{R}}}{N_{M}}|\psi_{0}\rangle

(S327)

where the
2\times 2
matrix
M(\mathbf{q})_{\xi_{1},\xi_{1}^{\prime}}
is defined as

\displaystyle M(\mathbf{q})_{+,+}=

\displaystyle 2[J_{0}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q})-J_{0}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q}_{2}=0)+J_{1}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q})-J_{1}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q}_{2}=0)]

\displaystyle M(\mathbf{q})_{-,-}=

\displaystyle 2[J_{0}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q})-J^{RKKY}_{0}(\mathbf{q}_{2}=0)+J_{1}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q})-J_{1}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q}_{2}=0)-J_{2}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q}_{2}=0)]

\displaystyle M(\mathbf{q})_{+,-}=

\displaystyle J_{4+}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q})\quad,\quad M(\mathbf{q})_{-,+}=J_{3+}^{RKKY}(-\mathbf{q})

(S328)

Equivalently, Eq.
S327
can be written as

\displaystyle\sum_{\xi_{1}^{\prime}}M(\mathbf{q})_{\xi_{1},\xi_{1}^{\prime}}u^{I}_{I2,\xi_{1}^{\prime}+}(\mathbf{q})=E_{\mathbf{q}}^{I}u^{I}_{i2,\xi_{1}+}(\mathbf{q})

(S329)

The two eigenvalues of
M(\mathbf{q})_{\xi_{1},\xi_{1}^{\prime}}
are

\displaystyle E^{1}_{\mathbf{q}}=

\displaystyle 2[J_{0}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q})-J_{0}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q}_{2}=0)+J_{1}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q})-J_{1}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q}_{2}=0)]-J_{2}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q}_{2}=0)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{J_{2}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q}_{2}=0)^{2}+|J_{3+}^{RKKY}(-\mathbf{q})|^{2}}

\displaystyle E^{2}_{\mathbf{q}}=

\displaystyle 2[J_{0}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q})-J_{0}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q}_{2}=0)+J_{1}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q})-J_{1}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q}_{2}=0)]-J_{2}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q}_{2}=0)

\displaystyle-\sqrt{J_{2}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q}_{2}=0)^{2}+|J_{3+}^{RKKY}(-\mathbf{q})|^{2}}

(S330)

At
\mathbf{q}=0
, we have

\displaystyle E_{\mathbf{q}=0}^{1}=-2J_{2}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q}_{2}=0)\quad,\quad E_{\mathbf{q}=0}^{2}=0

(S331)

where
E_{\mathbf{q}}^{2}
corresponds to the Goldstone mode

S7.3.4
Half-half sector

We next discuss the half-empty sector with
(i,i)=(2,2)
.
Combining Eq.
S308
, Eq.
and also the constraints
S318
, we have

\displaystyle[{\hat{H}}_{RKKY},O_{\mathbf{q},22}^{I}]|\psi_{0}\rangle

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{R}}\frac{e^{i\mathbf{q}\cdot\mathbf{R}}}{N_{M}}\bigg[-J(\mathbf{q}_{2}=0,+,-,-,+)u_{22,-+}^{I}(\mathbf{q})\psi_{\mathbf{R},2}^{f,-,{\dagger}}\psi_{f,\mathbf{R},2}^{+}-J(\mathbf{q}_{2}=0,+,-,-,+)u_{22,-+}\psi_{\mathbf{R},2}^{f,-,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},2}^{f,+}

\displaystyle+J(\mathbf{q}_{2}=0,-,-,-,-)u_{22,-+}^{I}(\mathbf{q})\psi_{\mathbf{R},2}^{f,-,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},2}^{f,+}+J(-\mathbf{q},+,+,-,-)u_{22,-+}^{I}(\mathbf{q})(-1)\psi_{\mathbf{R},2}^{f,-,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},2}^{f,+}

\displaystyle-J(\mathbf{q},-,-,+,+)u_{22,-+}^{I}(\mathbf{q})\psi_{\mathbf{R},2}^{f,-,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},2}^{f,+}-J(\mathbf{q}_{2}=0,+,+,+,+)u_{22,-+}^{I}(\mathbf{q})(-1)\psi_{\mathbf{R},2}^{f,-,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},2}^{f,+}

\displaystyle+J(\mathbf{R}_{2}=0,+,-,-,+)u_{22,-+}^{I}(\mathbf{q})\psi_{\mathbf{R},2}^{f,-,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},2}^{f,+}+J(\mathbf{R}_{2}=0,-,+,+,-)u_{22,-+}^{I}(\mathbf{q})\psi_{f,\mathbf{R},2}^{f,-,{\dagger}}\psi_{f,\mathbf{R},2}^{f,+}\bigg]|\psi_{0}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\bigg[J_{2}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q}_{2}=0)-J_{2}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{R}=0)-2J_{1}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q}_{2}=0)+J_{1}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{R}=0)+J_{0}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q})-J_{0}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q}=0)\bigg]O_{\mathbf{q},22}^{I}|\psi_{0}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E_{\mathbf{q}}^{I}O_{\mathbf{q},22}^{I}|\psi_{0}\rangle

where the dispersion is

\displaystyle E_{\mathbf{q}}^{I}=2\bigg[J_{2}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q}_{2}=0)-J_{2}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{R}=0)-J_{1}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q}_{2}=0)+J_{1}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{R}=0)+J_{0}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q})-J_{0}^{RKKY}(\mathbf{q}=0)\bigg]

(S332)

S7.3.5
Number of Goldstone modes

We now count the number of Goldstone modes of ground states in Eq.
S310
. According to Eq.
S321
, there are one Goldstone modes for each
(i,j)\in\{(3,1),(4,1)\}
choice in half-empty sector. Then, there are in total two Goldstone modes in the full-empty sector. According to Eq.
S326
, there are one Goldstone modes for each
(i,j)\in\{(2,1)\}
choices in the full-half sector. Then, there is one Goldstone mode in the full-half sector.
According to Eq.
S331
, there are one Goldstone modes for each
(i,j)\in\{(3,2),(4,2)\}
choices in the half-empty sector. Then, there are in total two Goldstone modes in the half-empty sector. Therefore, we have 5 Goldstone modes in total, which is consistent with Ref.
Bernevig et al. 2021c
.

S7.4
Discussion

First, we mention that our spectrum is calculated from RKKY Hamiltonian (Eq.
S110
). Thus the polarization of conduction electrons is ignored and the RKKY interactions are long-range (power-law decay). Consequently, we observe a linear dispersion of the Goldstone modes. Second, we derive the RKKY interaction in the momentum space by performing Fourier transformation integral and introducing a short distance cutoff (as described below Eq.
S234
and also in Sec.
S12
). Therefore, the short-distance information (large momentum) is lost in the spectrum. In the next section (Sec.
S8
, we will include the polarization of conduction electrons and produce the excitation spectrum that will more accurately describe the fluctuations of
f
-moments.

S8
Effective theory of
f
-moments

After finding the ground state from RKKY interactions, we now derive the effective theory of
f
-moments in the nonchiral-flat limit (
v_{\star}^{\prime}=0,M\neq 0)
and at integer filling
\nu=\nu_{f}=0,-1,-2
.

We first define the coherent state
|u\rangle
of
f
-moments as

\displaystyle|u\rangle=\prod_{\mathbf{R}}|u(\mathbf{R})\rangle_{\mathbf{R}}

(S333)

\displaystyle|u(\mathbf{R})\rangle_{\mathbf{R}}=\hat{R}[u(\mathbf{R})]|\psi_{0}\rangle_{\mathbf{R}}\,.

(S334)

|u(\mathbf{R})\rangle_{\mathbf{R}}
is the coherent state of the
f
-moment at site
\mathbf{R}

where
|\psi_{0}\rangle_{\mathbf{R}}
is a basis state that corresponds to the ground state given in Eq.
S265
or Eq.
S266
. Without loss of generality, at each filling, we let

\displaystyle\nu_{f}=0\quad:\quad

\displaystyle|\psi_{0}\rangle_{\mathbf{R}}=\psi_{\mathbf{R},1}^{f,+,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},1}^{f,-,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},2}^{f,+,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},2}^{f,-,{\dagger}}|0\rangle

\displaystyle\nu_{f}=-1\quad:\quad

\displaystyle|\psi_{0}\rangle_{\mathbf{R}}=\psi_{\mathbf{R},2}^{f,+,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},1}^{f,+,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},1}^{f,-,{\dagger}}|0\rangle

\displaystyle\nu_{f}=-2\quad:\quad

\displaystyle|\psi_{0}\rangle_{\mathbf{R}}=\psi_{\mathbf{R},1}^{f,+,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},1}^{f,-,{\dagger}}|0\rangle

(S335)

The corresponding ground states are then

\displaystyle|\psi_{0}\rangle=\prod_{\mathbf{R}}|\psi_{0}\rangle_{\mathbf{R}}

(S336)

\hat{R}[u(\mathbf{R})]
is a
SU(8)
rotation defined as

\displaystyle\hat{R}[u(\mathbf{R})]=\prod_{\mathbf{R}}\exp\bigg(-i\sum_{ij,\xi\xi^{\prime}}u_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}(\mathbf{R})\psi_{\mathbf{R},i}^{f,\xi,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},j}^{f,\xi^{\prime}}\bigg)\,.

(S337)

(Here we do not include the
U(1)
charge rotation. Because we fix the filling of
f
to be integer, and the
U(1)
charge is a good quantum number). If we treat
i\xi
as row indices and
j\xi^{\prime}
as column indices, then
u(\mathbf{R})
can be understood as a
8\times 8
matric. Since
\hat{R}_{\mathbf{R}}[u(\mathbf{R})]
generates a
SU(8)
rotation of
f
-fermions,
u(\mathbf{R})
is a traceless Hermitian matrix. To observe the nature of the transformation, we act the transformation operator on the
f
electrons which gives

\displaystyle\bigg(\hat{R}^{\dagger}_{\mathbf{R}}[u(\mathbf{R})]\bigg)\psi_{\mathbf{R},i}^{f,\xi}\bigg(\hat{R}_{\mathbf{R}}[u(\mathbf{R})]\bigg)=[e^{-iu(\mathbf{R})}]_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},j}^{f,\xi^{\prime}}

(S338)

where
\exp(-iu(\mathbf{R}))
is a matrix exponential. We let

\displaystyle R_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}(\mathbf{R})=[e^{-iu(\mathbf{R})}]_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}

(S339)

then

\displaystyle\bigg(\hat{R}^{\dagger}_{\mathbf{R}}[u(\mathbf{R})]\bigg)\psi_{\mathbf{R},i}^{f,\xi}\bigg(\hat{R}_{\mathbf{R}}[u(\mathbf{R})]\bigg)=R_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}(\mathbf{R})\psi_{\mathbf{R},j}^{f,\xi^{\prime}}

(S340)

We now derive the path integral of the following Hamiltonian

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}={\hat{H}}_{c}^{\prime}+{\hat{H}}_{int}\,.

(S341)

{\hat{H}}_{c}^{\prime}
contains the fermion bilinear terms and
{\hat{H}}_{int}
denotes the interactions between
c
-electrons and
f
-moments.

{\hat{H}}_{c^{\prime}}
takes the form of

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}_{c}^{\prime}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{k},i}\bigg[v_{\star}(k_{x}+i\xi k_{y})\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{\xi,c^{\prime},{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{\xi,c^{\prime\prime}}+\text{h.c.}\bigg]+(-\mu+W\nu_{f}+\frac{V_{0}}{\Omega_{0}}\nu_{c})\sum_{\mathbf{k},i}[\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{\xi,c^{\prime},{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{\xi,c^{\prime}}+\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{\xi,c^{\prime\prime},{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{\xi,c^{\prime\prime}}]

\displaystyle-\sum_{\mathbf{k},\xi,i}\bigg(\frac{1}{D_{1,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{1}{D_{2,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg)e^{-\lambda^{2}|\mathbf{k}|^{2}}\bigg[\frac{\gamma^{2}}{2}\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{\xi,c^{\prime},{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{\xi,c^{\prime}}+v_{\star}^{\prime}\gamma(k_{x}-i\xi k_{y})\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},-\xi}\bigg]

where the contributions from
{\hat{H}}_{W}
(Eq.
S7
),
{\hat{H}}_{V}
(Eq.
S8
), as well as the one-body scattering term from SW transformation
{\hat{H}}_{cc}
(Eq.
S203
), have been included, and
{\hat{H}}_{V}
has been treated by mean-field approximation. In addition, we have dropped the constant terms.
The interaction term (Eq.
S202
) is

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}_{int}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\xi,\xi^{\prime}}\sum_{\mu\nu}\frac{e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot\mathbf{R})}F(|\mathbf{k})|F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime})}{N_{M}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}}\bigg[\gamma^{2}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R}):

\displaystyle+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi-\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R}):(k_{x}^{\prime}-i\xi^{\prime}k_{y}^{\prime})+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,-\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R}):(k_{x}+i\xi k_{y})\bigg]:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k}):

\displaystyle-J\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\mu\nu,\xi}e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot\mathbf{R}}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{R})::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime\prime},\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k}):

(S342)

We now derive the path integral formula of the partition function corresponding to the Hamiltonian
{\hat{H}}={\hat{H}}_{c}^{\prime}+{\hat{H}}_{int}
. The partition function can be written as

\displaystyle Z=\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\prod_{n=1}^{N}\text{Tr}[e^{-\Delta\tau{\hat{H}}}],\quad\text{where }\Delta\tau=\beta/N

(S343)

For each time slice
\tau_{n}=n\Delta\tau
, we insert the identity operator (with Haar measure
Altland and Simons 2010
) and have

\displaystyle Z=

\displaystyle\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\int D[u(\mathbf{R},\tau_{n}),c_{\mathbf{k},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}(\tau_{n}),c_{\mathbf{k},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}(\tau_{n})]\text{Tr}\bigg[\prod_{n=1}^{N}e^{-\Delta\tau H}\frac{|c(\tau_{n})\rangle|u(\tau_{n})\rangle\langle u(\tau_{n})|\langle c(\tau_{n})|}{\bigg|\langle u(\tau_{n})|\langle c(\tau_{n})|\bigg|}\bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\lim_{N\rightarrow\infty}\int D[u(\mathbf{R},\tau_{n}),c_{\mathbf{k},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}(\tau_{n}),c_{\mathbf{k},\alpha\eta s}^{\dagger}(\tau_{n})]\text{Tr}\bigg[\prod_{n=1}^{N}\langle\frac{\langle u(\tau_{n})\langle c(\tau_{n+1})|e^{-\Delta\tau H}|c(\tau_{n+1})\rangle|u(\tau_{n+1})\rangle}{{\sqrt{\bigg|\langle u(\tau_{n}+\Delta\tau)|\langle c(\tau_{n}+\Delta\tau)\bigg|\bigg|\langle u(\tau_{n})|\langle c(\tau_{n})\bigg|}}}\bigg]

(S344)

where
|u(\tau_{n})\rangle=\prod_{\mathbf{R}}|u(\mathbf{R},\tau_{n})\rangle,|c(\tau_{n})\rangle
are coherent states of
c
and
u
fields, respectively, at time slice
\tau_{n}=n\beta/N
. For each time slice and large
N
(small
\Delta\tau
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{\langle u(\tau_{n}+\Delta\tau)|\langle c(\tau_{n}+\Delta\tau)|e^{-\Delta\tau{\hat{H}}}|c(\tau_{n})\rangle|u(\tau_{n})\rangle}{\sqrt{\bigg|\langle u(\tau_{n}+\Delta\tau)|\langle c(\tau_{n}+\Delta\tau)\bigg|\bigg|\langle u(\tau_{n})|\langle c(\tau_{n})\bigg|}}

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle\frac{\langle c(\tau_{n}+\Delta\tau)||c(\tau_{n})\rangle}{\sqrt{\bigg||\langle c(\tau_{n}+\Delta\tau)\bigg|\bigg||\langle c(\tau_{n})\bigg|}}\frac{\langle u(\tau_{n}+\Delta\tau)||u(\tau_{n})\rangle}{\sqrt{\bigg|\langle u(\tau_{n}+\Delta\tau)\bigg|\bigg|\langle u(\tau_{n})\bigg|}}-\Delta\tau\frac{\langle u(\tau_{n}+\Delta\tau)|\langle c(\tau_{n}+\Delta\tau)|{\hat{H}}|c(\tau_{n})\rangle|u(\tau_{n})\rangle}{\sqrt{\bigg|\langle u(\tau_{n}+\Delta\tau)|\langle c(\tau_{n}+\Delta\tau)\bigg|\bigg|\langle u(\tau_{n})|\langle c(\tau_{n})\bigg|}}

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bigg(1-\sum_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}[c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}^{\dagger}(\tau_{n}+\Delta\tau)-c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}^{\dagger}(\tau_{n})]c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}(\tau)\bigg)\bigg\{1-\bigg[\frac{\langle u(\tau_{n}+\Delta\tau)|u(\tau_{n})\rangle}{\sqrt{\bigg|\langle u(\tau_{n}+\Delta\tau)\bigg|\bigg|\langle u(\tau_{n})\bigg|}}-1\bigg]\bigg\}-H(\tau_{n})\Delta\tau}

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle 1-\sum_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}^{\dagger}(\tau_{n})\partial_{\tau}c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}(\tau_{n})\Delta\tau+\bigg[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\frac{\langle u(\tau_{n}+\Delta\tau)|u(\tau_{n})\rangle}{\sqrt{\bigg|\langle u(\tau_{n}+\Delta\tau)\bigg|\bigg|\langle u(\tau_{n})\bigg|}}}-1\bigg]-H(\tau_{n})\Delta\tau

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle\exp\bigg[-\sum_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}^{\dagger}(\tau_{n})\partial_{\tau}c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}(\tau_{n})\Delta\tau+\bigg[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\frac{\langle u(\tau_{n}+\Delta\tau)|u(\tau_{n})\rangle}{\sqrt{\bigg|\langle u(\tau_{n}+\Delta\tau)\bigg|\bigg|\langle u(\tau_{n})\bigg|}}}-1\bigg]-H(\tau_{n})\Delta\tau\bigg]

(S345)

where
H(\tau_{n})
is the original Hamiltonian
{\hat{H}}
where each
c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}
is replaced by
c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}(\tau_{n})
, and each
{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R})
is replaced by
\Sigma_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau_{n})=\langle u(\tau_{n})|:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R}):|u(\tau)\rangle
. And we use the fact that
|u(\tau_{n})\rangle
is normalized with
||u(\tau_{n})\rangle|=1
.

We then define the action as

\displaystyle S=\int_{0}^{\beta}\sum_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}^{\dagger}(\tau)\partial_{\tau}c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}(\tau)d\tau-\lim_{\Delta\tau\rightarrow 0}\sum_{n}\bigg[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\frac{\langle u(\tau_{n}+\Delta\tau)|u(\tau_{n})\rangle}{\sqrt{\bigg|\langle u(\tau_{n}+\Delta\tau)\bigg|\bigg|\langle u(\tau_{n})\bigg|}}}-1\bigg]+\int_{0}^{\beta}H(\tau)d\tau\,.

(S346)

We next evaluate each term in the action. The first term is the dynamical term of
c
electrons. The second one corresponds to the Berry phase term of
f
-moments. We note that

\displaystyle\langle u|\tilde{u}\rangle\approx

\displaystyle\prod_{\mathbf{R}}\langle\psi_{0}|\bigg\{1+i\sum_{ij,\xi\xi^{\prime}}u_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}(\mathbf{R})\psi_{\mathbf{R},i}^{\xi,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},j}^{\xi^{\prime}}-\frac{1}{2}\bigg[\sum_{ij,\xi\xi^{\prime}}u_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}(\mathbf{R})\psi_{\mathbf{R},i}^{\xi,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},j}^{\xi^{\prime}}\bigg]^{2}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\bigg\{1-i\sum_{i_{2}j_{2},\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime}_{2}}\tilde{u}_{i_{2}\xi_{2},j_{2}\xi_{2}^{\prime}}(\mathbf{R})\psi_{\mathbf{R},i_{2}}^{\xi_{2},{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},j_{2}}^{\xi^{\prime}_{2}}-\frac{1}{2}\bigg[\sum_{i_{2}j_{2},\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime}_{2}}\tilde{u}_{i_{2}\xi_{2},j_{2}\xi_{2}^{\prime}}(\mathbf{R})\psi_{\mathbf{R},i_{2}}^{\xi_{2},{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},j_{2}}^{\xi^{\prime}_{2}}\bigg]^{2}\bigg\}|\psi_{0}\rangle

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle\prod_{\mathbf{R}}\bigg[1+\sum_{i\xi}iu_{i\xi,i\xi}n(\xi,i)(\mathbf{R})-i\tilde{u}_{i\xi,i\xi}(\mathbf{R})n(\xi,i)

\displaystyle+\sum_{i,\xi,j,\xi^{\prime},i_{2},\xi_{2},j_{2},\xi_{2}^{\prime}}[\delta_{i,j}\delta_{\xi,\xi^{\prime}}\delta_{i_{2},j_{2}}\delta_{\xi_{2},\xi_{2}^{\prime}}n(\xi,i)n(\xi_{2},i_{2})+\delta_{i,j_{2}}\delta_{\xi,\xi_{2}^{\prime}}\delta_{j,i_{2}}\delta_{\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}}\delta_{j,i_{2}}n(\xi,i)(1-n(\xi^{\prime},j))]

\displaystyle[u_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}(\mathbf{R})\tilde{u}_{i_{2}\xi_{2},j_{2}\xi_{2}^{\prime}}(\mathbf{R})-\frac{1}{2}u_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}(\mathbf{R}){u}_{i_{2}\xi_{2},j_{2}\xi_{2}^{\prime}}(\mathbf{R})-\frac{1}{2}\tilde{u}_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}(\mathbf{R})\tilde{u}_{i_{2}\xi_{2},j_{2}\xi_{2}^{\prime}}(\mathbf{R})]\bigg]

(S347)

where we use the fact that

\langle\psi_{0}|\psi_{\mathbf{R},i}^{\xi,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},j}^{\xi^{\prime}}|\psi_{0}\rangle\propto\delta_{i,j}\delta_{\xi,\xi^{\prime}}
, with
|\psi_{0}\rangle
the ground state given in Eq.
S265
or Eq.
S266
. In addition, we let
n(\xi,i)=\langle\psi_{0}|\psi_{\mathbf{R},i}^{\xi,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},i}^{\xi}|\psi_{0}\rangle
.
We aim to rewrite the above equation in a more compact form. We introduce the following matrix

\displaystyle\Lambda_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}=\frac{1}{2}\langle\psi_{0}|:\psi_{\mathbf{R},i}^{f,\xi,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},j}^{f,\xi^{\prime}}:|\psi_{0}\rangle=\delta_{i,j}\delta_{\xi,\xi^{\prime}}\frac{1}{2}(n(\xi,i)-\frac{1}{2})

(S348)

For
|\psi_{0}\rangle
defined in Eq.
S335
(which are ground states at
v_{\star}^{\prime}\neq 0,M=0
), the values of
\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi}
are

\displaystyle\nu_{f}=0\quad:\quad

\displaystyle\Lambda_{1+,1+}=\Lambda_{2+,2+}=\Lambda_{1-,1-}=\Lambda_{2-,2-}=\frac{1}{4}\quad,\quad\Lambda_{3+,3+}=\Lambda_{4+,4+}=\Lambda_{3-,3-}=\Lambda_{4-,4-}=-\frac{1}{4}

\displaystyle\nu_{f}=-1\quad:\quad

\displaystyle\Lambda_{1+,1+}=\Lambda_{2+,2+}=\Lambda_{1-,1-}=\frac{1}{4}\quad,\quad\Lambda_{2-,2-}=\Lambda_{3+,3+}=\Lambda_{4+,4+}=\Lambda_{3-,3-}=\Lambda_{4-,4-}=\frac{1}{4}

\displaystyle\nu_{f}=-2\quad:\quad

\displaystyle\Lambda_{1+,1+}=\Lambda_{1-,1-}=\frac{1}{4}\quad,\quad\Lambda_{2+,2+}=\Lambda_{2-,2-}=\Lambda_{3+,3+}=\Lambda_{4+,4+}=\Lambda_{3-,3-}=\Lambda_{4-,4-}=\frac{1}{4}

(S349)

Here we comment that no matter what types of ferromagnetic order/ground state we considered, we can always make a basis transformation to make
\Lambda
a diagonal matrix. To observe this, we assume
\Lambda
is an arbitrary Hermitian matrix. We perform an eigendecomposition
\Lambda=V\tilde{\Lambda}V^{\dagger}
where
\tilde{\Lambda}
is a diagonal matrix that characterizes the eigenvalues of
\Lambda
and
V
is the matrix formed by eigenvectors of
\Lambda
. Clearly,
V
can be understood as a
SU(8)
rotation and we can make a basis change accordingly
\psi^{f}\rightarrow\tilde{\psi}^{f}
, where
\tilde{\psi}^{f,\xi}_{\mathbf{R},i}=\sum_{j,\xi^{\prime}}\psi^{f,\xi^{\prime}}_{\mathbf{R},j}V_{j\xi^{\prime},i\xi}
, such that
\frac{1}{2}\langle\psi_{0}|:\tilde{\psi}_{\mathbf{R},i}^{f,\xi,{\dagger}}\tilde{\psi}_{\mathbf{R},j}^{f,\xi^{\prime}}:|\psi_{0}\rangle=\tilde{\Lambda}
which is a diagonal matrix. Then in the new basis, we have a new model with a diagonal
\Lambda
matrix. However, there are two situations. In the first case, the
SU(8)
rotation characterized by
V
is also a symmetry transformation of the flat
U(4)
group. Since the Hamiltonian is invariant under a flat
U(4)
group, so the Hamiltonian is invariant under the basis change which is just a flat
U(4)
transformation (Note that we also need to perform the same
V
-transformation on
c
-electron). Then our effective theory remains the same compared to the effective theory we built for the ground state in Eq.
S335
. This is also a consequence of the flat
U(4)
symmetry of the system, namely all the ground states (characterized by
\Lambda
) that are connected by flat
U(4)
transformation giving rise to the same effective theory. In the second case, the
SU(8)
rotation characterized by
V
does not belong to the flat
U(4)
group. Then we have different types of ground state compared to the ground state in Eq.
S335
, and we have a different effective theory.

Then we have

\displaystyle\langle u|\tilde{u}\rangle=

\displaystyle\prod_{\mathbf{R}}1+2i\text{Tr}[(u-\tilde{u})\Lambda]-2\bigg[\text{Tr}[(u-\tilde{u})\Lambda]\bigg]^{2}+2\text{Tr}\bigg[[(u-\tilde{u})\Lambda]^{2}\bigg]-\frac{1}{8}\text{Tr}\bigg[(u-\tilde{u})^{2}\bigg]-\text{Tr}[u\Lambda\tilde{u}]+\text{Tr}[\tilde{u}\Lambda u]

(S350)

Using Eq.
S350
, the Berry phase term of
f
-moments becomes

\displaystyle\frac{\langle u(\tau_{n}+\Delta\tau)|u(\tau_{n})\rangle}{\sqrt{\bigg|\langle u(\tau_{n}+\Delta\tau)\bigg|\bigg|\langle u(\tau_{n})\bigg|}}\approx\prod_{\mathbf{R}}\bigg[1+2i\text{Tr}[\Lambda\partial_{\tau}u(\mathbf{R},\tau)]\Delta\tau-\text{Tr}[(\Lambda u(\mathbf{R},\tau_{n})-u(\mathbf{R},\tau_{n})\Lambda)\partial_{\tau}u(\mathbf{R},\tau_{n})]\Delta\tau\bigg]

(S351)

Finally, to the formula of
H(\tau)
(Eq.
S345
), we represent the
U(8)
moments in the path integral with
u
,

\displaystyle\Sigma_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau_{n})=\langle u(\tau_{n})|:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R}):|u(\tau_{n})\rangle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i,j}T_{ij}^{\mu\nu}\langle\psi_{0}|\hat{R}^{\dagger}[u(\mathbf{R}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\tau})]:\psi_{\mathbf{R},i}^{\xi,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},j}^{\xi^{\prime}}:\hat{R}[u(\mathbf{R}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0},\tau})]|\psi_{0}\rangle

Using Eq.
S339
, Eq.
S340
and Eq.
S348
, we can now express
f
-moments with
u
fields

\displaystyle\Sigma_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau)=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i,j}T_{ij}^{\mu\nu}R_{j\xi^{\prime},j_{2}\xi_{2}^{\prime}}(\mathbf{R},\tau_{n})R^{*}_{i\xi,i_{2}\xi_{2}}(\mathbf{R},\tau_{n})\langle\psi_{0}|:\psi_{\mathbf{R},i_{2}}^{f,\xi_{2},{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},j_{2}}^{f,\xi^{\prime}_{2}}:|\psi_{0}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i,j,i_{2},j_{2},\xi_{2},\xi_{2}^{\prime}}T_{ij}^{\mu\nu}R_{j\xi^{\prime},j_{2}\xi_{2}^{\prime}}(\mathbf{R},\tau)R^{*}_{i\xi,i_{2}\xi_{2}}(\mathbf{R},\tau)\Lambda_{i_{2}\xi_{2},j_{2}\xi^{\prime}_{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{ij}T_{ij}^{\mu\nu}[R^{\dagger}(\mathbf{R},\tau)\Lambda R(\mathbf{R},\tau)]_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}=\sum_{ij}T_{ij}^{\mu\nu}[e^{iu(\mathbf{R},\tau)}\Lambda e^{-iu(\mathbf{R},\tau)}]_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}

(S352)

where we use
R(\mathbf{R},\tau)=\exp[-iu(\mathbf{R},\tau)]
(Eq.
S339
). We next expand in powers of
u
, which gives

\displaystyle\Sigma_{\mu\nu}^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{R},\tau)=

\displaystyle\sum_{ij}T_{ij}^{\mu\nu}[R^{\dagger}(\mathbf{R},\tau)\Lambda R(\mathbf{R},\tau)]_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle\sum_{ij}T_{ij}^{\mu\nu}\bigg[\Lambda+iu(\mathbf{R},\tau)\Lambda-i\Lambda u(\mathbf{R},\tau)-\frac{u(\mathbf{R},\tau)u(\mathbf{R},\tau)\Lambda+\Lambda u(\mathbf{R},\tau)u(\mathbf{R},\tau)-2u(\mathbf{R},\tau)\Lambda u(\mathbf{R},\tau)}{2}\bigg]_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}

(S353)

We can introduce the following two matrices

\displaystyle A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=[iu(\mathbf{R},\tau)\Lambda-i\Lambda u(\mathbf{R},\tau)]_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}=i[u(\mathbf{R},\tau),\Lambda]_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}

\displaystyle B(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{i}{2}\bigg[u(\mathbf{R},\tau)A(\mathbf{R},\tau)-A(\mathbf{R},\tau)u(\mathbf{R},\tau)\bigg]_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}=\frac{i}{2}[u(\mathbf{R},\tau),A(\mathbf{R},\tau)]_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}\,.

(S354)

We also let

\displaystyle[\delta\Sigma(\mathbf{R},\tau)]_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}=A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}+B(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}

(S355)

such that

\displaystyle\Sigma^{(f,\xi\xi^{\prime})}_{\mu\nu}(\mathbf{R},\tau)\approx\sum_{ij}T^{\mu\nu}_{ij}\bigg(\Lambda_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}+[\delta\Sigma(\mathbf{R},\tau)]_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}\bigg)

(S356)

Using Eq.
S353
, we can rewrite the interaction term (Eq.
S342
) as

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}_{int}

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{R},\xi,\xi^{\prime},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}\sum_{\mu\nu}\frac{\gamma^{2}e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot\mathbf{R}}F(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)}{N_{M}D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}[\sum_{i,j}\Lambda_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}T^{\mu\nu}_{ij}]:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k}):

\displaystyle-J\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mu\nu,\xi,\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}\frac{e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot\mathbf{R}}}{N_{M}}[\sum_{ij}\Lambda_{i\xi,j\xi}T^{\mu\nu}_{ij}]:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime\prime},\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k}):

\displaystyle+\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\xi,\xi^{\prime}}\frac{e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot\mathbf{R}}F(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)}{N_{M}}\bigg[\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}T^{\mu\nu}_{ij}\Lambda_{\xi i,-\xi^{\prime}j}(k_{x}^{\prime}-i\xi^{\prime}k_{y}^{\prime})+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}T^{\mu\nu}_{ij}\Lambda_{-\xi i,\xi^{\prime}j}(k_{x}+i\xi k_{y})\bigg]:\Sigma_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k}):

\displaystyle+\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\xi,\xi^{\prime},\mu\nu,i,j}\frac{e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot\mathbf{R}}F(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)T^{\mu\nu}_{ij}}{N_{M}D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg[\gamma^{2}[\delta\Sigma(\mathbf{R})]_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}:[\delta\Sigma(\mathbf{R})]_{i\xi,j-\xi^{\prime}}:(k_{x}^{\prime}-i\xi^{\prime}k_{y}^{\prime})

\displaystyle+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}[\delta\Sigma(\mathbf{R})]_{i-\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}(k_{x}+i\xi k_{y})\bigg]

\displaystyle:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k}):-J\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\mu\nu,\xi,i,j}\frac{e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot\mathbf{R}}}{N_{M}}T^{\mu\nu}_{ij}[\delta\Sigma(\mathbf{R})]_{i\xi,j\xi}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime\prime},\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k}):

where the first two lines describe the polarization of conduction electrons due to the ferromagnetic ordering of
f
-moments and the last three lines describe the interactions between conduction electrons and the fluctuations of
f
-moments.
F(|\mathbf{k}|)
(Eq.
S189
) is the damping factor.

Now we are able to write down the full action. Combining Eq.
S341
, Eq.
S346
, Eq.
S351
and Eq.
S8
, we have

\displaystyle S=

\displaystyle S_{u}+S_{c,order}+S_{int}

\displaystyle S_{u}=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{\beta}\sum_{\mathbf{R}}\bigg[2i\text{Tr}[\Lambda\partial_{\tau}u(\mathbf{R},\tau)]-\text{Tr}[{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}[\Lambda,u(\mathbf{R},\tau)]}\partial_{\tau}u(\mathbf{R},\tau)]\bigg]d\tau

\displaystyle S_{c,order}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\beta}\bigg[\sum_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}^{\dagger}(\tau)\partial_{\tau}c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}+H_{c,order}(\tau)\bigg]d\tau

\displaystyle S_{int}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\beta}\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\xi,\xi^{\prime}}\frac{e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot\mathbf{R}}F(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)}{N_{M}D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\sum_{\mu\nu,i,j}T^{\mu\nu}_{ij}\bigg[\gamma^{2}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j-\xi^{\prime}}(k_{x}^{\prime}-i\xi^{\prime}k_{y}^{\prime})

\displaystyle+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i-\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}(k_{x}+i\xi k_{y})\bigg]:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k},\tau):

\displaystyle-J\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\mu\nu,\xi,i,j}\frac{e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot\mathbf{R}}}{N_{M}}T^{\mu\nu}_{ij}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime\prime},\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k},\tau):d\tau

\displaystyle S_{int,2}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\beta}\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\xi,\xi^{\prime}}\frac{e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot\mathbf{R}}F(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)}{N_{M}D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\sum_{\mu\nu,i,j}T^{\mu\nu}_{ij}\bigg[\gamma^{2}B(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}B(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j-\xi^{\prime}}(k_{x}^{\prime}-i\xi^{\prime}k_{y}])

\displaystyle+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}B(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i-\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}(k_{x}+i\xi k_{y})\bigg]:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau):

\displaystyle-J\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\mu\nu,\xi,i,j}\frac{e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot\mathbf{R}}}{N_{M}}T^{\mu\nu}_{ij}B(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime\prime},\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k},\tau):d\tau

(S357)

where
S_{u}
describes the Berry phase of the
f
moments,
S_{c,order}
contains all the fermion bilinear and is characterized by the Hamiltonian
{\hat{H}}_{c,order}
.
{\hat{H}}_{c,order}
takes the form of

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}_{c,order}=

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{k},i}\bigg[v_{\star}(k_{x}+i\xi k_{y})\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{\xi,c^{\prime},{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{\xi,c^{\prime\prime}}+\text{h.c.}\bigg]+(-\mu+W+\frac{V_{0}}{\Omega_{0}}\nu_{c})\nu_{f}\sum_{\mathbf{k},i}[\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{\xi,c^{\prime},{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{\xi,c^{\prime}}+\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{\xi,c^{\prime\prime},{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{\xi,c^{\prime\prime}}]

\displaystyle-\sum_{\mathbf{k},\xi,i}\bigg(\frac{1}{D_{1,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{1}{D_{2,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg)|F(|\mathbf{k}|)|^{2}\bigg[\frac{\gamma^{2}}{2}\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{\xi,c^{\prime},{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{\xi,c^{\prime}}+v_{\star}^{\prime}\gamma(k_{x}-i\xi k_{y})\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},-\xi}\bigg]

\displaystyle+\sum_{\mathbf{k},\xi,i}\frac{2\gamma^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi}\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi}-\sum_{\mathbf{k},\xi,i}2J\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi}\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime\prime},\xi,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime\prime},\xi}+\sum_{\mathbf{k},\xi,i}\frac{2\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}(\Lambda_{i-\xi,i-\xi}

\displaystyle+\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi})(k_{x}-ik_{y}\xi)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{\xi,c^{\prime},{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{-\xi,c^{\prime}}

(S358)

where we rewrite the Hamiltonian with
\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime}},\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime\prime}}
basis and drop the constant term.

In Eq.
S358
, The first line contains the contribution from
{\hat{H}}_{c}
,
{\hat{H}}_{W}
and
{\hat{H}}_{V}
(mean-field level). The second line comes from
{\hat{H}}_{cc}
(Eq.
S203
). The third line describes the polarization of conduction electrons due to the long-range order of the
f
-moments. In addition, we utilize the fact that
\Lambda_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}
is a diagonal matrix.

We also rewrite the
{\hat{H}}_{c,order}
in a more compact form

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}_{c,order}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{k},i}\begin{bmatrix}\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{\xi=+,c^{\prime},{\dagger}}&\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{\xi=+,c^{\prime\prime},{\dagger}}&\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{\xi=-,c^{\prime},{\dagger}}&\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{\xi=-,c^{\prime\prime},{\dagger}}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}E_{0,\mathbf{k}}^{+,i}+E_{3,\mathbf{k}}^{+,i}&v_{\star}(k_{x}+ik_{y})&v_{\mathbf{k}}^{i}(k_{x}-ik_{y})&0\\
v_{\star}(k_{x}-ik_{y})&E_{0,\mathbf{k}}^{+,i}-E_{3,\mathbf{k}}^{+,i}&0&0\\
v_{\mathbf{k}}^{i}(k_{x}+ik_{y})&0&E_{0,\mathbf{k}}^{-,i}+E_{3,\mathbf{k}}^{-,i}&v_{\star}(k_{x}-ik_{y})\\
0&0&v_{\star}(k_{x}+ik_{y})&E_{0,\mathbf{k}}^{-,i}-E_{3,\mathbf{k}}^{-,i}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{\xi=+,c^{\prime}}\\
\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{\xi=+,c^{\prime\prime}}\\
\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{\xi=-,c^{\prime}}\\
\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{\xi=-,c^{\prime\prime}}\end{bmatrix}

(S359)

where

\displaystyle E_{0,\mathbf{k}}^{\xi,i}

\displaystyle=-\mu+W+\frac{V_{0}}{\Omega_{0}}\nu_{c}-\bigg(\frac{1}{D_{1,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{1}{D_{2,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg)\frac{e^{-\lambda^{2}|\mathbf{k}|^{2}}\gamma^{2}}{4}+\frac{\gamma^{2}\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}-J\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi}

\displaystyle E_{3,\mathbf{k}}^{\xi,i}

\displaystyle=-\bigg(\frac{1}{D_{1,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{1}{D_{2,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg)\frac{e^{-\lambda^{2}|\mathbf{k}|^{2}}\gamma^{2}}{4}+\frac{\gamma^{2}\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+J\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi}

\displaystyle v_{\mathbf{k}}^{i}

\displaystyle=-\sum_{\mathbf{k},\xi,i}\bigg(\frac{1}{D_{1,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}+\frac{1}{D_{2,\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg)e^{-\lambda^{2}|\mathbf{k}|^{2}}v_{\star}^{\prime}+\frac{2\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}(\Lambda_{i-,i-}+\Lambda_{i+,i+})

(S360)

S_{int}
and
S_{int,2}
contain the interaction between
u
and
c
. We separate
S_{int}
into two parts
S_{K}^{\prime},S_{J}^{\prime}

\displaystyle S_{int}=

\displaystyle S_{K}^{\prime}+S_{J}^{\prime}

\displaystyle S_{K}^{\prime}=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\beta}\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\xi,\xi^{\prime}}\frac{e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot\mathbf{R}}F(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)}{N_{M}D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\sum_{\mu\nu,i,j}T^{\mu\nu}_{ij}\bigg[\gamma^{2}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j-\xi^{\prime}}(k_{x}^{\prime}-i\xi^{\prime}k_{y}])

\displaystyle+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i-\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}(k_{x}+i\xi k_{y})\bigg]:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k},\tau):

\displaystyle S_{J}^{\prime}=

\displaystyle-J\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\mu\nu,\xi,i,j}\frac{e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot\mathbf{R}}}{N_{M}}T^{\mu\nu}_{ij}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime\prime},\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k},\tau):d\tau

(S361)

We now integrate out conduction electrons to derive the effective action of the
f
-moments described by
u(\mathbf{R},\tau)
. The partition function can be written as

\displaystyle Z=

\displaystyle\int D[c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}^{\dagger}(\tau),c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}(\tau),u(\mathbf{R},\tau)]\exp\bigg[-S_{u}-S_{c,order}-S_{int}-S_{int,2}\bigg]

\displaystyle\propto

\displaystyle\int D[u(\mathbf{R},\tau)]e^{-S_{u}}\langle e^{-S_{int}-S_{int,2}}\rangle_{0}

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle\int D[u(\mathbf{R},\tau)]e^{-S_{u}}\exp\bigg[-\langle S_{int}\rangle_{0}-\langle S_{int,2}\rangle_{0}+\frac{1}{2}[\langle S_{int}^{2}\rangle_{0}-(\langle S_{int}\rangle_{0})^{2}]\bigg]

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle\int D[u(\mathbf{R},\tau)]\exp\bigg[-{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\bigg[S_{u}+\langle S_{int}\rangle_{0}+\langle S_{int,2}\rangle_{0}-\frac{1}{2}[\langle S_{int}^{2}\rangle_{0}-(\langle S_{int}\rangle_{0})^{2}]\bigg]\bigg]}

(S362)

where we integrate out
c
-electrons in the second line and introduce

\displaystyle\langle O\rangle_{0}=\frac{1}{Z_{0}}\int D[c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}^{\dagger}(\tau),c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}(\tau)]Oe^{-S_{c,order}}\quad,\quad Z_{0}=\int D[c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}^{\dagger}(\tau),c_{\mathbf{k},a\eta s}(\tau)]e^{-S_{c,order}}\,.

(S363)

S_{int}
(Eq.
S8
) is linear in
u(\mathbf{R},\tau)
, so we keep its first-order and second-order contributions.
S_{int,2}
(Eq.
S8
) contains bilinear term of
u(\mathbf{R},\tau)
, so we only keep its first-order contribution. Overall, we keep the zeroth order, first order and second-order terms of
u(\mathbf{R},\tau)
in the effective action.
From Eq.
S362
.
We then define the effective action of
f
-moments as

\displaystyle S_{eff}=

\displaystyle S_{u}+\langle S_{int}\rangle_{0}{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}+}\langle S_{int,2}\rangle_{0}-\frac{1}{2}\bigg[\langle S_{int}S_{int}\rangle_{0,con}\bigg]

(S364)

where

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\langle T_{\tau}S_{int}S_{int}\rangle_{0,con}=-\frac{1}{2}\langle T_{\tau}S_{K}^{\prime}S_{K}^{\prime}\rangle_{0,con}-\frac{1}{2}\langle T_{\tau}S_{J}^{\prime}S_{J}^{\prime}\rangle_{0,con}-\langle S_{J}^{\prime}S_{K}\rangle_{0,con}

(S365)

S8.1
Single-particle Green’s function of conduction electrons

In order to find the effective theory of
f
-moments, it is useful to first consider the following single-particle Green’s function of conduction electrons

\displaystyle g^{\xi\xi^{\prime},i}_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}(\mathbf{k},\tau)=-\langle T_{\tau}\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},{\dagger}}(0)\rangle_{0}\quad,\quad g^{\xi\xi^{\prime},i}_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}(\mathbf{R},\tau)=-\langle T_{\tau}\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime\prime},\xi}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime\prime},\xi^{\prime},{\dagger}}(0)\rangle_{0}

\displaystyle g^{\xi\xi^{\prime},i}_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}(\mathbf{k},\tau)=-\langle T_{\tau}\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime\prime},\xi^{\prime},{\dagger}}(0)\rangle_{0}\quad,\quad g^{\xi\xi^{\prime},i}_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime}}(\mathbf{k},\tau)=-\langle T_{\tau}\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime\prime},\xi}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},{\dagger}}(0)\rangle_{0}

(S366)

where the expectation value is calculated with respect to the Hamiltonian
{\hat{H}}_{c,order}
(Eq.
S359
).
Using Wick’s theorem we have

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}:\Sigma_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k},\tau)::\Sigma_{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}^{(c^{\prime},\xi_{2}\xi_{2}^{\prime})}(\mathbf{k}_{2}^{\prime},\mathbf{k}_{2}-\mathbf{k}_{2}^{\prime},0):\rangle=-\sum_{ij}\delta_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}_{2}}\delta_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}_{2}^{\prime}}T^{\mu\nu}_{ij}T^{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}_{ji}g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},-\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi\xi_{2},j}(\mathbf{k},\tau)/4

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}:\Sigma_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime\prime},\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k},\tau)::\Sigma_{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}^{(c^{\prime\prime},\xi_{2}\xi_{2}^{\prime})}(\mathbf{k}_{2}^{\prime},\mathbf{k}_{2}-\mathbf{k}_{2}^{\prime},0):\rangle=-\sum_{ij}\delta_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}_{2}}\delta_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}_{2}^{\prime}}T^{\mu\nu}_{ij}T^{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}_{ji}g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}^{\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},-\tau)g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}^{\xi\xi_{2},j}(\mathbf{k},\tau)/4

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}:\Sigma_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime}\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k},\tau)::\Sigma_{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}^{(c^{\prime\prime},\xi_{2}\xi_{2}^{\prime})}(\mathbf{k}_{2}^{\prime},\mathbf{k}_{2}-\mathbf{k}_{2}^{\prime},0):\rangle=-\sum_{ij}\delta_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}_{2}}\delta_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}_{2}^{\prime}}T^{\mu\nu}_{ij}T^{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}_{ji}g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},-\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}^{\xi\xi_{2},j}(\mathbf{k},\tau)/4

(S367)

Now we are in the position to calculate each term in the effective action.

S8.2
\langle S_{int}\rangle_{0}

We have

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}S_{int}\rangle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\beta}\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\xi,\xi^{\prime}}\frac{e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot\mathbf{R}}F(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)}{N_{M}D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\sum_{\mu\nu,i,j}T^{\mu\nu}_{ij}\bigg[\gamma^{2}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j-\xi^{\prime}}(k_{x}^{\prime}-i\xi^{\prime}k_{y}^{\prime})

\displaystyle+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i-\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}(k_{x}+i\xi k_{y})\bigg]\langle T_{\tau}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k},\tau):\rangle_{0}

\displaystyle-J\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\mu\nu,\xi,i,j}\frac{e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot\mathbf{R}}}{N_{M}}T^{\mu\nu}_{ij}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi}\langle T_{\tau}:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime\prime},\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k},\tau):\rangle_{0}d\tau

(S368)

where
F(|\mathbf{k}|)
(Eq.
S189
) is the damping factor.
For the given Hamiltonian of conduction electrons (Eq.
S359
), we have

\displaystyle\langle:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k},\tau):\rangle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{s,t}T^{\mu\nu}_{st}\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},s}^{c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},t}^{c^{\prime},\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}=\frac{\delta_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}}{2}\sum_{s}T^{\mu\nu}_{ss}\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},s}^{c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},s}^{c^{\prime},\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}

\displaystyle\langle:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime\prime},\xi^{\prime},\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau):\rangle=\frac{1}{2}\sum_{s,t}T^{\mu\nu}_{st}\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},s}^{c^{\prime\prime},\xi^{\prime},{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},s}^{c^{\prime\prime},\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}=\frac{\delta_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}}{2}\sum_{s}T^{\mu\nu}_{ss}\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},s}^{c^{\prime\prime},\xi^{\prime},{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},s}^{c^{\prime\prime},\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}

(S369)

Combining above equation with Eq.
S368
, we find

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}S_{int}\rangle_{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\beta}\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\xi,\xi^{\prime}}\frac{|F(|\mathbf{k}|)|^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}N_{M}}\sum_{i}2\bigg[\gamma^{2}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,i\xi^{\prime}}+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,i-\xi^{\prime}}(k_{x}-i\xi^{\prime}k_{y})+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i-\xi,i\xi^{\prime}}(k_{x}+i\xi k_{y})\bigg]

\displaystyle\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}-J\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\xi,i}\frac{2}{N_{M}}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,i\xi}\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime\prime},\xi,{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime\prime},\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}d\tau

(S370)

According to the definition of
A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}
(Eq.
S354
), we have

\displaystyle A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,i\xi}=iu(\mathbf{R},\tau)(\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi}-\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi})=0

(S371)

Then only
A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,i-\xi}(\mathbf{R},\tau)
can give non-zero contribution

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}S_{int}\rangle_{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\beta}\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\xi}\frac{|F(|\mathbf{k}|)|^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}N_{M}}\sum_{i}2\bigg[\gamma^{2}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,i-\xi}\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},-\xi,{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}

\displaystyle+\bigg(\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,i-\xi}(k_{x}-i\xi k_{y})+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i-\xi,i\xi}(k_{x}+i\xi k_{y})\bigg)\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi,{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}\bigg]

(S372)

In terms of Green’s function

\displaystyle\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},-\xi,{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}=-g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime},i}^{\xi,-\xi}(\mathbf{k},-0^{+})

\displaystyle\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi,{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}=-g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime},i}^{\xi,\xi}(\mathbf{k},-0^{+})-1/2

where the
-1/2
comes from the normal ordering and minus sign comes from the fermion anti-commutation relation.
By direct solving the
{\hat{H}}_{c,order}
, we find
g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi,-\xi,i}(\mathbf{k},\tau)=(k_{x}-i\xi k_{y})f_{4}(|\mathbf{k}|,\tau,i)
(see Sec.
S13
, Eq.
S588
) where
f_{4}(|\mathbf{k}|,\tau,i)
is a function that only depends on
|\mathbf{k}|
. Then the following
\mathbf{k}
summation vanishes due to the
(k_{x}\pm i\xi k_{y})
contribution

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{k}}\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},-\xi,{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}=\sum_{\mathbf{k}}-(k_{x}-i\xi k_{y})f_{4}(|\mathbf{k}|,0^{+},i)=0

(S374)

As for
g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi,\xi,i}(\mathbf{k},\tau)
, by direct solving
{\hat{H}}_{c,order}
, we find it only depends on
|\mathbf{k}|
(see Sec.
S13
, Eq.
S588
). Then the following
\mathbf{k}
summation vanishes due to the
(k_{x}\pm i\xi k_{y})
term

\displaystyle\sum_{\mathbf{k}}(k_{x}\pm i\xi k_{y})\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi,{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}=\sum_{\mathbf{k}}(k_{x}\pm i\xi k_{y})[-g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi,\xi}(\mathbf{k},-0^{+})-1/2]=0

(S375)

From Eq.
S374
and Eq.
S375
, all
\mathbf{k}
summation in Eq.
S372
goes to zero and then

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}S_{int}\rangle_{0}=0

(S376)

S8.3
\langle S_{int,2}\rangle_{0}

Expanding
\Sigma_{\mu\nu}^{c^{\prime}/c^{\prime\prime},\xi,\xi^{\prime})}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau)
in
S_{int,2}
, we have

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}S_{int,2}\rangle_{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}\xi,\xi^{\prime}}\sum_{\mu\nu,i,j}\frac{e^{-i(\mathbf{k}-\mathbf{k}^{\prime})\cdot\mathbf{R}}F(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)}{N_{M}}\sum_{lm}T^{\mu\nu}_{lm}\frac{T_{ij}^{\mu\nu}}{2}\bigg[\frac{\gamma^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}B(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{l\xi,m\xi^{\prime}}\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},j}^{c^{\prime},\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}

\displaystyle-J\delta_{\xi,\xi^{\prime}}B(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi}\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime\prime},\xi,{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},j}^{c^{\prime\prime},\xi}(\tau)\rangle_{0}+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}B(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{l\xi,m\xi^{\prime}}(k_{x}^{\prime}+i\xi^{\prime}k_{y})\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},-\xi^{\prime},{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},j}^{c^{\prime},\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}

\displaystyle+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}B(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{l\xi,m\xi^{\prime}}(k_{x}-i\xi k_{y})\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime},j}^{c^{\prime\prime},-\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}\bigg]d\tau

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\xi,\xi^{\prime},i}B(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,i\xi^{\prime}}\frac{|F(|\mathbf{k}|)|^{2}}{N_{M}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}}\bigg[\frac{2\gamma^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}-2J^{2}\delta_{\xi,\xi^{\prime}}\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime\prime},\xi,{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime\prime},\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}

\displaystyle+\frac{2\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}(k_{x}+i\xi^{\prime}k_{y})\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},-\xi^{\prime},{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}+\frac{2\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}(k_{x}-i\xi k_{y})\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},-\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}\bigg]d\tau

(S377)

where
F(|\mathbf{k}|)
(Eq.
S189
) is the damping factor.

Using Eq.
S374
and Eq.
S375
, we find

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}S_{int,2}\rangle_{0}=

\displaystyle\int\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\xi,i}B(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,i\xi}\frac{|F(|\mathbf{k}|)|^{2}}{N_{M}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}}\bigg[\frac{2\gamma^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi,{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}-2J^{2}\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime\prime},\xi,{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime\prime},\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}

\displaystyle+\frac{2\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}(k_{x}+i\xi k_{y})\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},-\xi,{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}+\frac{2\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}(k_{x}-i\xi k_{y})\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi,{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},-\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}\bigg]d\tau

(S378)

We then have

\displaystyle\langle T_{\tau}S_{int,2}\rangle_{0}=\int\sum_{\mathbf{R},\xi,i}B(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,i\xi}N_{i\xi}d\tau

(S379)

where we have defined
N_{i\xi}
as

\displaystyle N_{i\xi}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}}\bigg[\frac{2|F(|\mathbf{k}|)|^{2}\gamma^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi,{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}-2J\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime\prime},\xi,{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime\prime},\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}

\displaystyle+\frac{2\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}|F(|\mathbf{k}|)|^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}(k_{x}+i\xi k_{y})\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},-\xi,{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}+\frac{2\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}|F(|\mathbf{k}|)|^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}(k_{x}-i\xi k_{y})\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi,{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},-\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}\bigg]

(S380)

S8.4
-\frac{1}{2}\langle S_{K}^{\prime}S_{K}^{\prime}\rangle_{0,con}

From Eq.
S362
, we obtain

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\langle S_{K}^{\prime}S_{K}^{\prime}\rangle_{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\xi,\xi^{\prime}}\sum_{\mathbf{R}_{2},\mathbf{k}_{2},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}_{2},\xi_{2},\xi^{\prime}_{2}}\frac{e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot\mathbf{R}}F(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)}{N_{M}D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\frac{e^{-i(\mathbf{k}_{2}-\mathbf{k}_{2}^{\prime})\cdot\mathbf{R}_{2}}F(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)}{N_{M}D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\sum_{\mu\nu,i,j}T^{\mu\nu}_{ij}\sum_{\mu_{2}\nu_{2},i_{2},j_{2}}T^{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}_{i_{2}j_{2}}

\displaystyle\bigg[\gamma^{2}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j-\xi^{\prime}}(k_{x}^{\prime}-i\xi^{\prime}k_{y}^{\prime})+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i-\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}(k_{x}+i\xi k_{y})\bigg]

\displaystyle\bigg[\gamma^{2}A(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2})_{i_{2}\xi_{2},j_{2}\xi_{2}^{\prime}}+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}A(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2})_{i_{2}\xi_{2},j_{2}-\xi^{\prime}_{2}}(k_{2,x}-i\xi_{2}^{\prime}k_{2,y})+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}A(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2})_{i_{2}-\xi_{2},j_{2}\xi^{\prime}_{2}}(k_{2,x}^{\prime}+i\xi_{2}k_{2,y}^{\prime})\bigg]

\displaystyle\langle:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime}_{2},\xi_{2})}(\mathbf{k}_{2}^{\prime},\mathbf{k}_{2}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k}_{2},\tau_{2}):\rangle_{0,con}d\tau d\tau_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\xi,\xi^{\prime}}\sum_{\mathbf{R}_{2},\xi_{2},\xi^{\prime}_{2}}\frac{e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2})}|F(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)|^{2}}{N_{M}D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\frac{1}{N_{M}D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\sum_{\mu\nu,i,j}T^{\mu\nu}_{ij}\sum_{\mu_{2}\nu_{2},i_{2},j_{2}}T^{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}_{i_{2}j_{2}}\sum_{s,t}T^{\mu\nu}_{st}T^{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}_{ts}

\displaystyle\bigg[\gamma^{2}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j-\xi^{\prime}}(k_{x}^{\prime}-i\xi^{\prime}k_{y}^{\prime})+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i-\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}(k_{x}+i\xi k_{y})\bigg]

\displaystyle\bigg[\gamma^{2}A(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2})_{i_{2}\xi_{2},j_{2}\xi_{2}^{\prime}}+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}A(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2})_{i_{2}\xi_{2},j_{2}-\xi^{\prime}_{2}}(k_{x}-i\xi_{2}^{\prime}k_{y})+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}A(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2})_{i_{2}-\xi_{2},j_{2}\xi^{\prime}_{2}}(k_{x}^{\prime}+i\xi_{2}k_{y}^{\prime})\bigg]

\displaystyle(-1)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime},s}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau_{2}-\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi\xi_{2}^{\prime},t}(\mathbf{k},\tau-\tau_{2})/4d\tau d\tau_{2}

(S381)

where we use Eq.
S367
in the final step
and the factor
1/4
comes from the two
1/2
factors in the definition of
\Sigma_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime}\xi)}
and
\Sigma_{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}^{(c^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime},\xi_{2})}
(Eq.
S20
).
Using
\sum_{\mu\nu}T^{\mu\nu}_{ij}T^{\mu\nu}_{st}=4\delta_{i,t}\delta_{i,s}
, we find

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\langle S_{K}^{\prime}S_{K}^{\prime}\rangle_{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\int_{0}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{R}_{2},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\xi,\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2},\xi_{2}^{\prime},i,j}\frac{e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2})}|F(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}|)|^{2}}{(N_{M}D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}})^{2}}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}A_{j\xi_{2},i\xi_{2}^{\prime}}(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2})

\displaystyle\bigg\{\gamma^{4}g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau_{2}-\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},\tau-\tau_{2})

\displaystyle+\gamma^{3}v_{\star}^{\prime}\bigg[(k_{x}+i\xi_{2}^{\prime}k_{y})g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau_{2}-\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi-\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},\tau-\tau_{2})+(k_{x}^{\prime}-i\xi_{2}k_{y}’)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{-\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau_{2}-\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},\tau-\tau_{2})

\displaystyle+(k_{x}^{\prime}+i\xi^{\prime}k_{y}^{\prime})g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}-\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau_{2}-\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},\tau-\tau_{2})+(k_{x}-i\xi k_{y})g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau_{2}-\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{-\xi\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},\tau-\tau_{2})\bigg]

\displaystyle+\gamma^{2}(v_{\star}^{\prime})^{2}\bigg[(k_{x}^{\prime}+i\xi^{\prime}k_{y}^{\prime})(k_{x}+i\xi_{2}^{\prime}k_{y})g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}-\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau_{2}-\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi-\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},\tau-\tau_{2})

\displaystyle+(k_{x}-i\xi k_{y})(k_{x}^{\prime}-i\xi_{2}k_{y}^{\prime})g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{-\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau_{2}-\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{-\xi\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},\tau-\tau_{2})

\displaystyle+(k_{x}^{\prime}+i\xi^{\prime}k_{y})(k_{x}^{\prime}-i\xi_{2}k_{y}^{\prime})g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{-\xi_{2}-\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau_{2}-\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},\tau-\tau_{2})

\displaystyle+(k_{x}-i\xi k_{y})(k_{x}+i\xi_{2}^{\prime}k_{y})g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau_{2}-\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{-\xi-\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},\tau-\tau_{2})\bigg]\bigg\}d\tau d\tau_{2}

(S382)

Here, we comment that, all terms that are at the second order of
S_{K}^{\prime}
(
\gamma^{4},\gamma^{2}(v_{\star}^{\prime})^{2},\gamma^{3}v_{\star}^{\prime}
terms) need to be kept in order to recover the Goldstone mode in the ordered ground state. This is because all terms we kept in the
{\hat{H}}_{K}
, (
\gamma^{2},\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}
terms) introduce a polarization effect to the conduction electrons. This can be observed from Eq.
S359
and Eq.
S360
where both
\gamma^{2}
and
\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}
terms affect the single-particle Hamiltonian of conduction electrons. Therefore, when we derive the effective action,
all the second-order terms induced by
\gamma^{2},\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}
terms in
{\hat{H}}_{K}
, that are
\gamma^{4},\gamma^{2}(v_{\star}^{\prime})^{2},\gamma^{3}v_{\star}^{\prime}
terms, need to be kept, in order to be consistent with the single-particle Hamiltonian of conduction electrons.

S8.5
-\frac{1}{2}\langle S_{J}^{\prime}S_{J}^{\prime}\rangle_{0,con}

Using Eq.
S367
, we find

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\langle S_{J}^{\prime}S_{J}^{\prime}\rangle_{0,con}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}\frac{J^{2}}{2N_{M}^{2}}\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{R}_{2},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\mathbf{k}_{2},\mathbf{k}_{2}^{\prime}}\sum_{\mu\nu,\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}\sum_{\xi,\xi_{2}}\sum_{ij,i_{2}j_{2}}\frac{e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot\mathbf{R}-i(\mathbf{k}_{2}-\mathbf{k}_{2}^{\prime})\cdot\mathbf{R}_{2}}}{N_{M}^{2}}T^{\mu\nu}_{ij}T^{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}_{i_{2}j_{2}}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi}A(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2})_{i_{2}\xi_{2},j_{2}\xi_{2}}

\displaystyle\langle:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime\prime},\xi\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}^{(c^{\prime\prime},\xi_{2}\xi_{2})}(\mathbf{k}_{2}^{\prime},\mathbf{k}_{2}-\mathbf{k}_{2}^{\prime},\tau_{2}):\rangle_{0,con}d\tau d\tau_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}\frac{J^{2}}{2}\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{R}_{2},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}\sum_{\mu\nu,\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}\sum_{\xi,\xi_{2}}\sum_{ij,i_{2}j_{2}}e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2})}T^{\mu\nu}_{ij}T^{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}_{i_{2}j_{2}}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi}A(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2})_{i_{2}\xi_{2},j_{2}\xi_{2}}

\displaystyle(-1)\sum_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}}T^{\mu\nu}_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}}T^{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}_{j^{\prime}i^{\prime}}g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}^{\xi_{2}\xi,i^{\prime}}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},-\tau+\tau_{2})g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}^{\xi\xi_{2},j^{\prime}}(\mathbf{k},\tau-\tau_{2})/4d\tau d\tau_{2}

(S383)

Using
\sum_{\mu\nu}T^{\mu\nu}_{ij}T^{\mu\nu}_{st}=4\delta_{i,t}\delta_{i,s}
, we find

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\langle S_{J}^{\prime}S_{J}^{\prime}\rangle_{0,con}=\int_{0}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}\frac{2J^{2}e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2})}}{N_{M}^{2}}\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{R}_{2}}\sum_{\xi,\xi_{2},i,j}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi}A(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2})_{j\xi_{2},i\xi_{2}}g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}^{\xi_{2}\xi,j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},-\tau+\tau_{2})g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}^{\xi\xi_{2},i}(\mathbf{k},\tau-\tau_{2})d\tau d\tau_{2}

(S384)

S8.6
-\langle S_{J}^{\prime}S_{K}^{\prime}\rangle_{0,con}

Using Eq.
S367
, we find

\displaystyle-\langle S_{J}^{\prime}S_{K}^{\prime}\rangle_{0,con}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}J\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\mathbf{k}_{2},\mathbf{k}_{2}^{\prime},\xi,\xi^{\prime}}\frac{e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot\mathbf{R}-i(\mathbf{k}_{2}-\mathbf{k}_{2}^{\prime})\cdot\mathbf{R}_{2}}F(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)}{N_{M}^{2}D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\sum_{\mu\nu,i,j}T^{\mu\nu}_{ij}\sum_{\mathbf{R}_{2},\mu_{2}\nu_{2},i,j,\xi_{2}}T_{i_{2}j_{2}}^{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}\sum_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}}T_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}}^{\mu\nu}T_{j^{\prime}i^{\prime}}^{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}

\displaystyle\bigg[\gamma^{2}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j-\xi^{\prime}}(k_{x}^{\prime}-i\xi k_{y}^{\prime})+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i-\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}(k_{x}+i\xi k_{y})\bigg]A(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2})_{i_{2}\xi_{2},j_{2}\xi_{2}}

\displaystyle\langle:{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu\nu}^{(c^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},\xi)}(\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k},\tau)::{\hat{\Sigma}}_{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}^{(c^{\prime\prime},\xi_{2},\xi_{2})}(\mathbf{k}_{2}^{\prime},\mathbf{k}_{2}-\mathbf{k}_{2}^{\prime},\tau_{2}):\rangle_{0,con}d\tau d\tau_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}J\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\xi,\xi^{\prime}}\frac{e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2})}F(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)}{N_{M}^{2}D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\sum_{\mu\nu,i,j}T^{\mu\nu}_{ij}\sum_{\mathbf{R}_{2},\mu_{2}\nu_{2},i,j,\xi_{2}}T_{i_{2}j_{2}}^{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}\sum_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}}T_{i^{\prime}j^{\prime}}^{\mu\nu}T_{j^{\prime}i^{\prime}}^{\mu_{2}\nu_{2}}

\displaystyle\bigg[\gamma^{2}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j-\xi^{\prime}}(k_{x}^{\prime}-i\xi^{\prime}k_{y}^{\prime})+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i-\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}(k_{x}+i\xi k_{y})\bigg]

\displaystyle A(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2})_{i_{2}\xi_{2},j_{2}\xi_{2}}(-1)g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime},i^{\prime}}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},-\tau+\tau_{2})g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}^{\xi\xi_{2},j^{\prime}}(\mathbf{k},\tau-\tau_{2})/4d\tau d\tau_{2}

(S385)

Using
\sum_{\mu\nu}T^{\mu\nu}_{ij}T^{\mu\nu}_{st}=4\delta_{i,t}\delta_{i,s}
, we find

\displaystyle-\langle S_{J}^{\prime}S_{K}^{\prime}\rangle_{0,con}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-4\int_{0}^{\beta}\int_{0}^{\beta}J\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{R}_{2},\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\xi,\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}}\frac{e^{-i(\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k})\cdot(\mathbf{R}-\mathbf{R}_{2})}F(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}|)}{N_{M}^{2}D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}A(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2})_{j\xi_{2},i\xi_{2}}\bigg[

\displaystyle\gamma^{2}g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},-\tau+\tau_{2})g^{\xi\xi_{2},i}_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}(\mathbf{k},\tau-\tau_{2})+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(k_{x}^{\prime}+i\xi^{\prime}k_{y}^{\prime})g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}-\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},-\tau+\tau_{2})g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}^{\xi\xi_{2},i}(\mathbf{k},\tau-\tau_{2})

\displaystyle+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(k_{x}-i\xi k_{y})g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},-\tau+\tau_{2})g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}^{-\xi\xi_{2},i}(\mathbf{k},\tau-\tau_{2})\bigg]d\tau d\tau_{2}

(S386)

S8.7
Effective action

Combining Eq.
S364
, Eq.
S379
, Eq.
S382
, Eq.
S384
and Eq.
S386
, we find the following effective action of the
f
-moments.

\displaystyle S_{eff}=

\displaystyle S_{u}+\int\sum_{\xi,i,\mathbf{R}}B(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,i\xi}N_{i\xi}d\tau

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\pi}\int\int\sum_{\mathbf{R},\mathbf{R}_{2},\mathbf{q},\xi,\xi^{\prime}}A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}A(\mathbf{R}_{2},\tau_{2})_{j\xi_{2},i\xi_{2}^{\prime}}\frac{e^{i\mathbf{q}\cdot(\mathbf{R}_{2}-\mathbf{R})-i\omega(\tau_{2}-\tau)}}{N_{M}}\chi_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}}^{A}(\mathbf{q},i\omega,i,j)d\tau d\tau_{2}

(S387)

where we consider zero temperature and define

\displaystyle\chi^{A}_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}}(\mathbf{q},i\omega,i,j)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int\sum_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}\frac{\delta_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k},\mathbf{q}}e^{i\omega\tau}}{N_{M}}\bigg\{\frac{2\gamma^{4}[F(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)]^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\gamma^{3}v_{\star}^{\prime}[F(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)]^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}\bigg[(k_{x}+i\xi_{2}^{\prime}k_{y})g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi-\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)+(k_{x}^{\prime}-i\xi_{2}k_{y}’)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{-\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)

\displaystyle+(k_{x}^{\prime}+i\xi^{\prime}k_{y}^{\prime})g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}-\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)+(k_{x}-i\xi k_{y})g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{-\xi\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{2\gamma^{2}(v_{\star}^{\prime})^{2}[F(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)]^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}\bigg[(k_{x}^{\prime}+i\xi^{\prime}k_{y}^{\prime})(k_{x}+i\xi_{2}^{\prime}k_{y})g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}-\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi-\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)

\displaystyle+(k_{x}-i\xi k_{y})(k_{x}^{\prime}-i\xi_{2}k_{y}^{\prime})g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{-\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{-\xi\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)+(k_{x}^{\prime}+i\xi^{\prime}k_{y})(k_{x}^{\prime}-i\xi_{2}k_{y}^{\prime})g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{-\xi_{2}-\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)

\displaystyle+(k_{x}-i\xi k_{y})(k_{x}+i\xi_{2}^{\prime}k_{y})g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{-\xi-\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)\bigg]+2J^{2}\delta_{\xi,\xi^{\prime}}\delta_{\xi_{2}^{\prime},\xi_{2}}g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}^{\xi_{2}\xi,j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau)g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}^{\xi\xi_{2},i}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)

\displaystyle-\frac{4\delta_{\xi_{2},\xi_{2}^{\prime}}JF(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg[\gamma^{2}g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau)g^{\xi\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)

\displaystyle+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(k_{x}^{\prime}+i\xi^{\prime}k_{y}^{\prime})g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}-\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}^{\xi\xi_{2},i}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(k_{x}-i\xi k_{y})g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}^{-\xi\xi_{2},i}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)\bigg]\bigg\}d\tau

(S388)

where
\omega
denotes the Matsubara frequency (
w\in 2\pi/\beta\mathbb{Z}
at finite temperature and can be treated as a continuous variable at zero temperature).

We next rewrite
A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}},B(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,i\xi}
with
u(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}

\displaystyle A(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}=i(\Lambda_{j\xi^{\prime},j\xi^{\prime}}-\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi})u(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}\quad,\quad B(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,i\xi}=u(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}u(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{j\xi^{\prime},i\xi}\bigg(\Lambda_{j\xi^{\prime},j\xi^{\prime}}-\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi}\bigg)

(S389)

and and introduce the Fourier transformation of
u(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}

\displaystyle u(\mathbf{q},i\omega)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}=\int\sum_{\mathbf{R}}e^{-i\mathbf{q}\cdot\mathbf{R}+i\omega\tau}u(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}d\tau

Then

\displaystyle S_{eff}=

\displaystyle-\int\sum_{\mathbf{R}}2i\sum_{i\xi}\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi}\partial_{\tau}u(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,i\xi}+\frac{1}{2\pi N_{M}}\int\sum_{\mathbf{q}}\sum_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}i\omega(\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi}-\Lambda_{j\xi^{\prime},j\xi^{\prime}})u(\mathbf{q},i\omega)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}u(-\mathbf{q},-i\omega)_{j\xi^{\prime},i\xi}d\omega

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\pi N_{M}}\int\sum_{\mathbf{q}}\sum_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}N_{i\xi}\bigg(\Lambda_{j\xi^{\prime},j\xi^{\prime}}-\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi}\bigg)u(\mathbf{q},i\omega)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}u(-\mathbf{q},-i\omega)_{j\xi^{\prime},i\xi}d\omega

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\pi N_{M}}\int\sum_{\mathbf{q}}\sum_{i,j,\xi,\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2},\xi_{2}^{\prime}}\bigg(\Lambda_{j\xi^{\prime},j\xi^{\prime}}-\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi}\bigg)\bigg(\Lambda_{j\xi_{2},j\xi_{2}}-\Lambda_{i\xi_{2}^{\prime},i\xi_{2}^{\prime}}\bigg)\chi^{A}_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}}(\mathbf{q},i\omega,i,j)

\displaystyle u(\mathbf{q},i\omega)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}u(-\mathbf{q},-i\omega)_{j\xi_{2},i\xi_{2}^{\prime}}d\omega

(S390)

We next to separate
u(\mathbf{R},\tau)
(or
u(\mathbf{q},i\omega)
) into two-parts, the diagonal components
u(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,i\xi}
and the off-diagonal components
u(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}
with
i\xi\neq j\xi^{\prime}
. Correspondingly, we can separate the effective action into two parts
S_{eff}=S_{eff,diag}+S_{eff,off}
, where
S_{eff,diag}
(
S_{eff,off}
) describes the behaviors of diagonal (off-diagonal) components of
u(\mathbf{R},\tau)
.
S_{eff,diag}
can be written as

\displaystyle S_{eff,diag}=-\int\sum_{\mathbf{R}}2i\sum_{i\xi}\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi}\partial_{\tau}u(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,i\xi}d\tau

(S391)

where we write the action in the position and imaginary-time spaces to illustrate its behaviors.
Clearly,
S_{eff,diag}
is a total derivative and only matters when
u(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,i\xi}
develops topologically non-trivial configurations which give a non-zero winding number
Q_{i\xi}(\mathbf{R})\neq 0
where
Q_{i\xi}(\mathbf{R})=\int\partial_{\tau}u(\mathbf{R},\tau)_{i\xi,i\xi}d\tau
. Then,
S_{eff,diag}=-2i\sum_{\mathbf{R},i\xi}\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi}Q_{i\xi}(\mathbf{R})
is just a phase factor. Therefore, we will focus on the off-diagonal components of
u
.

The off-diagonal components give

\displaystyle S_{eff,off}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi N_{M}}\int\sum_{\mathbf{q}}\sum_{i,j,\xi,\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2},\xi_{2}^{\prime}}u(\mathbf{q},i\omega)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}u(-\mathbf{q},-i\omega)_{j\xi_{2},i\xi_{2}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\bigg\{-i\omega(\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi}-\Lambda_{j\xi^{\prime},j\xi^{\prime}})\delta_{\xi_{2},\xi^{\prime}}\delta_{\xi_{2}^{\prime},\xi}+N_{i\xi}(\Lambda_{j\xi^{\prime},j\xi^{\prime}}-\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi})\delta_{\xi_{2},\xi^{\prime}}\delta_{\xi_{2}^{\prime},\xi}

\displaystyle+\bigg(\Lambda_{j\xi^{\prime},j\xi^{\prime}}-\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi}\bigg)\bigg(\Lambda_{j\xi_{2},j\xi_{2}}-\Lambda_{i\xi_{2}^{\prime},i\xi_{2}^{\prime}}\bigg)\chi^{A}_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}}(\mathbf{q},i\omega,i,j)\bigg\}d\omega

(S392)

It is worth mentioning that not all the off-diagonal terms appear in the effective action
S_{eff,off}
. To observe that, we first introduce the following two sets of indices:

\displaystyle S_{fill}=\{i\xi|\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi}=1/4\}\quad,\quad S_{emp}=\{i\xi|\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi}=-1/4\}

(S393)

where
S_{fill}
(
S_{emp}
) denotes the set of flavors, that are filled with one (zero)
f
electron. For the ground states in Eq.
S335
, we have

\displaystyle\nu_{f}=0

\displaystyle:\quad S_{fill}=\{1+,1-,2+,2-\},\quad S_{emp}=\{3+,3-,4+,4-\}

\displaystyle\nu_{f}=-1

\displaystyle:\quad S_{fill}=\{1+,1-,2+\},\quad S_{emp}=\{2-,3+,3-,4+,4-\}

\displaystyle\nu_{f}=-2

\displaystyle:\quad S_{fill}=\{1+,1-\},\quad S_{emp}=\{2+,2-,3+,3-,4+,4-\}

and

\displaystyle\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi}-\Lambda_{j\xi^{\prime},j\xi^{\prime}}=0\quad,\quad i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}\in S_{fill}

\displaystyle\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi}-\Lambda_{j\xi^{\prime},j\xi^{\prime}}=0\quad,\quad i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}\in S_{emp}

\displaystyle\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi}-\Lambda_{j\xi^{\prime},j\xi^{\prime}}=1/2\quad,\quad i\xi\in S_{fill}\quad,\quad j\xi^{\prime}\in S_{emp}

\displaystyle\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi}-\Lambda_{j\xi^{\prime},j\xi^{\prime}}=-1/2\quad,\quad i\xi\in S_{emp}\quad,\quad j\xi^{\prime}\in S_{fill}\,.

(S395)

All terms in Eq.
S392
that contain
u(\mathbf{q},i\omega)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}
will have a prefactor
\Lambda_{i\xi,i\xi}-\Lambda_{j\xi^{\prime},j\xi^{\prime}}
. Then, only
u(\mathbf{q},i\omega)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}
with
i\xi\in S_{fill},j\xi^{\prime}\in S_{emp}
or
i\xi\in S_{emp},j\xi^{\prime}\in S_{fill}
produces non-zero contribution. This allows us to write the effective action as

\displaystyle S_{eff,off}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi N_{M}}\int\sum_{\mathbf{q}}\sum_{i\xi,i\xi_{2}^{\prime}\in S_{fill},j\xi^{\prime},j\xi_{2}\in S_{emp}}u(\mathbf{q},i\omega)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}u(-\mathbf{q},-i\omega)_{j\xi_{2},i\xi_{2}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\bigg\{i\omega\delta_{\xi_{2},\xi^{\prime}}\delta_{\xi_{2}^{\prime},\xi}-\frac{N_{i\xi}-N_{j\xi^{\prime}}}{2}\delta_{\xi_{2},\xi^{\prime}}\delta_{\xi_{2}^{\prime},\xi}+\frac{1}{4}\bigg(\chi^{A}_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}}(\mathbf{q},i\omega=0,i,j)+\chi^{A}_{\xi_{2}\xi_{2}^{\prime},\xi^{\prime}\xi}(-\mathbf{q},i\omega=0,j,i)\bigg)\bigg\}d\omega

(S396)

where we also take the low-frequency approximation of the
\chi^{A}
by letting
\chi^{A}_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}}(\mathbf{q},i\omega,i,j)\approx\chi^{A}_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}}(\mathbf{q},i\omega=0,i,j)
. For what follows, we will focus on the
S_{eff,off}
which describes the spin fluctuations of the system. Here, we also provide the formula of
N_{i\xi},\chi_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}}^{A}(\mathbf{q},i\omega,i,j)

\displaystyle N_{i\xi}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N_{M}}\sum_{\mathbf{k}}\bigg[\frac{2\gamma^{2}[F(|\mathbf{k}|)]^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi,{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}-2J\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime\prime},\xi,{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime\prime},\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}

\displaystyle+\frac{2\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}[F(|\mathbf{k}|)]^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}(k_{x}+i\xi k_{y})\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},-\xi,{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}+\frac{2\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}[F(|\mathbf{k}|)]^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}(k_{x}-i\xi k_{y})\langle:\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},\xi,{\dagger}}(\tau)\psi_{\mathbf{k},i}^{c^{\prime},-\xi}(\tau):\rangle_{0}\bigg]

\displaystyle\chi^{A}_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}}(\mathbf{q},i\omega=0,i,j)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int\sum_{\mathbf{k},\mathbf{k}^{\prime}}\frac{\delta_{\mathbf{k}^{\prime}-\mathbf{k},\mathbf{q}}}{N_{M}}\bigg\{\frac{2\gamma^{4}[F(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)]^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\gamma^{3}v_{\star}^{\prime}[F(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)]^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}\bigg[(k_{x}+i\xi_{2}^{\prime}k_{y})g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi-\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)+(k_{x}^{\prime}-i\xi_{2}k_{y}’)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{-\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)

\displaystyle+(k_{x}^{\prime}+i\xi^{\prime}k_{y}^{\prime})g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}-\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)+(k_{x}-i\xi k_{y})g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{-\xi\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)\bigg]

\displaystyle+\frac{2\gamma^{2}(v_{\star}^{\prime})^{2}[F(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)]^{2}}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}^{2}}\bigg[(k_{x}^{\prime}+i\xi^{\prime}k_{y}^{\prime})(k_{x}+i\xi_{2}^{\prime}k_{y})g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}-\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi-\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)

\displaystyle+(k_{x}-i\xi k_{y})(k_{x}^{\prime}-i\xi_{2}k_{y}^{\prime})g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{-\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{-\xi\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)+(k_{x}^{\prime}+i\xi^{\prime}k_{y})(k_{x}^{\prime}-i\xi_{2}k_{y}^{\prime})g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{-\xi_{2}-\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)

\displaystyle+(k_{x}-i\xi k_{y})(k_{x}+i\xi_{2}^{\prime}k_{y})g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{-\xi-\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)\bigg]+2J^{2}\delta_{\xi,\xi^{\prime}}\delta_{\xi_{2}^{\prime},\xi_{2}}g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}^{\xi_{2}\xi,j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau)g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}^{\xi\xi_{2},i}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)

\displaystyle-\frac{4\delta_{\xi_{2},\xi_{2}^{\prime}}JF(|\mathbf{k}|)F(|\mathbf{k}^{\prime}|)}{D_{\nu_{c},\nu_{f}}}\bigg[\gamma^{2}g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau)g^{\xi\xi_{2}^{\prime},i}_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)

\displaystyle+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(k_{x}^{\prime}+i\xi^{\prime}k_{y}^{\prime})g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}-\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}^{\xi\xi_{2},i}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)+\gamma v_{\star}^{\prime}(k_{x}-i\xi k_{y})g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi_{2}\xi^{\prime},j}(\mathbf{k}^{\prime},\tau)g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}^{-\xi\xi_{2},i}(\mathbf{k},-\tau)\bigg]\bigg\}d\tau

(S397)

We next introduce

\displaystyle F^{ij}_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}}(\mathbf{q})=\frac{N_{i\xi}-N_{j\xi^{\prime}}}{2}\delta_{\xi_{2},\xi^{\prime}}\delta_{\xi_{2}^{\prime},\xi}-\frac{1}{4}\bigg(\chi^{A}_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}}(\mathbf{q},i\omega=0,i,j)+\chi^{A}_{\xi_{2}\xi_{2}^{\prime},\xi^{\prime}\xi}(-\mathbf{q},i\omega=0,j,i)\bigg)

(S398)

Then Eq.
S396
can be written as

\displaystyle S_{eff,off}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi N_{M}}\int\sum_{\mathbf{q}}\sum_{i\xi,i\xi_{2}^{\prime}\in S_{fill},j\xi^{\prime},j\xi_{2}\in S_{emp}}u(\mathbf{q},i\omega)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}u(-\mathbf{q},-i\omega)_{j\xi_{2},i\xi_{2}^{\prime}}\bigg\{i\omega\delta_{\xi_{2},\xi^{\prime}}\delta_{\xi_{2}^{\prime},\xi}-F_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}}^{ij}(\mathbf{q})\bigg)\bigg\}d\omega

(S399)

We mention that the excitation spectrum is obtained by numerically evaluating Eq.
S397
and Eq.
S398
, and then fining the eigenvalues of
F_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}}^{ij}(\mathbf{q})
.
We now prove
F_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}}^{ij}(\mathbf{q})
, as a matrix with row and column indices
\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}
, is a Hermitian matrix, in other words

\displaystyle F_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}}^{ij}(\mathbf{q})=[F_{\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2},\xi\xi^{\prime}}(\mathbf{q})]^{*}

(S400)

To prove Eq.
S400
, we first consider
N_{i\xi}
(Eq.
S397
)

\displaystyle N_{i\xi}^{*}=
