S8.13
\nu_{f}=-1

We now consider
\nu_{f}=-1
.
We first discuss the symmetry of
\nu_{f}=-1
ground state.
It turns out, the remaining discrete symmetry of the ground state (Eq.
S335
) at
\nu_{f}=-1
is
C_{3z}
, because one of the valley-spin flavors at
\nu_{f}=-1
is filled with only one electron. In addition, the original flat
U(4)
symmetry is broken to
U(1)\times U(1)\times U(2)
symmetry.

We next discuss the fluctuations at
\nu_{f}=-1
. From Eq.
S334
and Eq.
S337
, we find

\displaystyle|u(\mathbf{R})\rangle_{\mathbf{R}}=\prod_{\mathbf{R}}\exp\bigg(-i\sum_{ij,\xi\xi^{\prime}}u_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}(\mathbf{R})\psi_{\mathbf{R},i}^{f,\xi,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},j}^{f,\xi^{\prime}}\bigg)|\psi_{0}\rangle\approx\bigg[1-i\sum_{ij,\xi\xi^{\prime}}u_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}(\mathbf{R})\psi_{f,\mathbf{R},i}^{\xi,{\dagger}}\psi_{\mathbf{R},j}^{f,\xi^{\prime}}\bigg]|\psi_{0}\rangle

(S491)

Therefore at the leading-order,
u_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}(\mathbf{R})
(
i\xi\neq j\xi^{\prime}
) describe the procedure of moving one
f
-electron from
j\xi^{\prime}
flavor at site
\mathbf{R}
to
i\xi
flavor at the same site. This allows us to classify the
u
fields into four groups. Taking the ground state in Eq.
S335
at
\nu_{f}=-1
, four groups of
u_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}(\mathbf{R})
are
(1) full-empty sector with
i=3,4,j=1
or
i=1,j=3,4
; (2) half-empty sector with
i=2,j=3,4
or
i=3,4,j=2
; (3) full-half sector with
i=1,j=2
or
j=2,i=1
; (4) half-half sector with
i=2,j=2
. Group (1) describes the fluctuations between fully filled and empty spin-valley flavors.
Group (2) describes the fluctuations between half-filled and empty spin-valley flavors. Group (3) describes the fluctuations between fully filled and empty spin-valley flavors. Group (4) describes the fluctuations between half-filled and half-filled spin-valley flavors.
Correspondingly, we can separate the
S_{eff,off}
into four parts:

\displaystyle S_{eff,off}=S_{eff,off,fe}+S_{eff,off,he}+S_{eff,off,fh}+S_{eff,off,hh}

(S492)

where
S_{eff,off,fe},S_{eff,off,he},S_{eff,off,fh},S_{eff,off,hh}
describe the effective theory of full-empty sector, half-empty sector, full-half sector, and half-half sector respectively.

S8.13.1
Full-empty sector

For the full-empty sector, we have action (Eq.
S399
)

\displaystyle S_{eff,off,fe}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi N_{M}}\int\sum_{\mathbf{q}}\sum_{i\in\{1\},j\in\{3,4\}}\sum_{\xi,\xi_{2}^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}}u(\mathbf{q},i\omega)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}u(-\mathbf{q},-i\omega)_{j\xi_{2},i\xi_{2}^{\prime}}[i\omega\delta_{\xi_{2},\xi^{\prime}}\delta_{\xi_{2}^{\prime},\xi}-F^{ij}_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}}(\mathbf{q})]d\omega

(S493)

where

i=1,j\in\{3,4\}
.
i=1
flavor is filled with two
f
electrons and
j=3,4
are empty (we take the ground sate in Eq.
S335
).
Utilizing the
U(1)\times U(1)\times U(2)
, symmetry, we have

\displaystyle\chi_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}}^{A}(\mathbf{q},i\omega=0,i=1,j)=\chi_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}}^{A}(\mathbf{q},i\omega=0,i=1,j^{\prime})\quad,\quad j,j^{\prime}\in\{3,4\}

\displaystyle N_{1\xi}-N_{j\xi^{\prime}}=N_{1\xi}-N_{j^{\prime}\xi^{\prime}}\quad,\quad j,j^{\prime}\in\{3,4\}

(S494)

Therefore
F^{ij}_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}^{\prime}}
take the same value for
i=1,j\in\{3,4\}
.
We thus define

\displaystyle H^{\nu_{f}=-1,fe}(\mathbf{q})_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}}=F^{ij}_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}^{\prime}}(\mathbf{q})\quad,\quad i=1,j\in\{3,4\}

(S495)

The effective theory can be written as

\displaystyle S_{eff,off,fe}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi N_{M}}\int\sum_{\mathbf{q}}\sum_{i\in\{1\},j\in\{3,4\}}\sum_{\xi,\xi_{2}^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}}u(\mathbf{q},i\omega)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}u(-\mathbf{q},-i\omega)_{j\xi_{2},i\xi_{2}^{\prime}}\bigg\{i\omega\delta_{\xi_{2},\xi^{\prime}}\delta_{\xi_{2}^{\prime},\xi}-H^{\nu_{f}=-1,fe}(\mathbf{q})_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}}\bigg\}d\omega

(S496)

The excitation spectrum can be derived by finding the eigenvalues of
H^{\nu_{f}=-1,fe}(\mathbf{q})
.

Here we comment that
{\hat{H}}_{c,order}
has a larger symmetry group than the symmetry group of the ground state. Even though, the ground state does not have
C_{2z}T
and
C_{2x}
symmetries,
{\hat{H}}_{c,order}
has a special type of
C_{2z}T
and
C_{2x}
symmetry, which we call
(C_{2z}T)^{\prime}
and
(C_{2x})^{\prime}
. For the ground state in Eq.
S335
,
(C_{2z}T)^{\prime}
(
(C_{2x})^{\prime}
) are defined as
C_{2z}T
(
C_{2x}
) transformations that only act on the valley-spin flavors
i=1,3,4
(Note that,
C_{2z}T
and
C_{2x}
will not flip valley and spin indices).
Then
{\hat{H}}_{c,order}
satisfies
(C_{2z}T)^{\prime}
and
(C_{2x})^{\prime}
symmetries, so is the
H^{\nu_{f}=-1,fe}(\mathbf{q})_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}}
. We comment that
(C_{2z}T)^{\prime},(C_{2x})^{\prime}
are not real symmetries of the system.

Consequently, we have

\displaystyle g_{aa^{\prime}}^{\xi\xi^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},\tau,)=(g_{aa^{\prime}}^{-\xi-\xi^{\prime},i}(C_{2z}T\mathbf{k},\tau))^{*}\quad,\quad a,a^{\prime}\in\{c^{\prime},c^{\prime\prime}\}

\displaystyle g_{aa^{\prime}}^{\xi\xi^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},\tau)=g_{aa^{\prime}}^{-\xi-\xi^{\prime},i}(C_{2x}\mathbf{k},\tau)\quad,\quad a,a^{\prime}\in\{c^{\prime},c^{\prime\prime}\}

\displaystyle g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi\xi^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},\tau)=g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi\xi^{\prime},i}(C_{3z}\mathbf{k},\tau)e^{i\frac{2\pi}{3}(\xi^{\prime}-\xi)}\quad,\quad g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}^{\xi\xi^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},\tau)=g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime\prime},i}^{\xi\xi^{\prime}}(C_{3z}\mathbf{k},\tau)

\displaystyle g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}^{\xi\xi^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},\tau)=g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}^{\xi\xi^{\prime},i}(C_{3z}\mathbf{k},\tau)e^{i\frac{2\pi}{3}(-\xi)}\quad,\quad g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi\xi^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},\tau)=g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi\xi^{\prime},i}(C_{3z}\mathbf{k},\tau)e^{i\frac{2\pi}{3}(\xi^{\prime})}\,.

(S497)

where
i=1,3,4
.
Thus, using Eq.
S495
, Eq.
S397
and Eq.
S497
, we find

\displaystyle H^{\nu_{f}=-1,fe}(\mathbf{q})_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}}=(H^{\nu_{f}}(C_{2z}T\mathbf{q})_{-\xi-\xi^{\prime},-\xi_{2}^{\prime}-\xi_{2}})^{*}

\displaystyle H^{\nu_{f}=-1,fe}(\mathbf{q})_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}\xi_{2}^{\prime}}=H^{\nu_{f}=-1,fe}(C_{2x}\mathbf{q})_{-\xi-\xi^{\prime},-\xi_{2}^{\prime}-\xi_{2}}

\displaystyle H^{\nu_{f}=-1,fe}(\mathbf{q})_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}\xi_{2}^{\prime}}=H^{\nu_{f}=-1,fe}(C_{3z}\mathbf{q})_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}}e^{i\frac{2\pi}{3}(\xi^{\prime}-\xi_{2}+\xi_{2}^{\prime}-\xi)}

(S498)

In addition from Eq.
S438
(with
i=1,j=3,\xi=\xi^{\prime}=+
)

\displaystyle H^{\nu_{f}=-1,fe}(\mathbf{q}=0)_{++,++}+H^{\nu_{f}=-1,fe}(\mathbf{q}=0)_{+-,--}=0

(S499)

Clearly,
H^{\nu_{f}=-1,fe}(\mathbf{q})
satisfy the same constrain as
{\hat{H}}^{\nu_{f}=0}(\mathbf{q})
(Eq.
S453
). Consequently,
H^{\nu_{f}=-1,fe}(\mathbf{q})
has the same long-wavelength behavior as shown in Eq.
S464
.

S8.13.2
Full-half sector

For the full-half sector, we take
i=1,j=2
where
i=1
flavor is filled with two
f
electrons, and
j=2
flavor is filled with one
f
electrons (Eq.
S335
).

\displaystyle S_{eff,off,fh}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi N_{M}}\int\sum_{\mathbf{q}}\sum_{\xi,\xi^{\prime}}u(\mathbf{q},i\omega)_{1\xi,2-}u(-\mathbf{q},-i\omega)_{2-,1\xi^{\prime}}\bigg\{i\omega\delta_{\xi,\xi^{\prime}}-F_{\xi-,\xi^{\prime}-}^{12}(\mathbf{q})\bigg\}d\omega

(S500)

We thus introduce the following matrix

\displaystyle H^{\nu_{f}=-1,fh}(\mathbf{q})_{\xi,\xi^{\prime}}=F_{\xi-,\xi^{\prime}-}^{12}(\mathbf{q})

(S501)

which is a
2\times 2
matrix.
The effective action can be written as

\displaystyle S_{eff,off,fh}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi N_{M}}\int\sum_{\mathbf{q}}\sum_{\xi,\xi^{\prime}}u(\mathbf{q},i\omega)_{1\xi,2-}u(-\mathbf{q},-i\omega)_{2-,1\xi^{\prime}}\bigg\{i\omega\delta_{\xi,\xi^{\prime}}-H^{\nu_{f}=-1,fh}(\mathbf{q})_{\xi,\xi^{\prime}}\bigg\}d\omega

(S502)

Ground state at
\nu_{f}=-3
(Eq.
S335
) only has
C_{3z}
symmetry. We use Eq.
S440
, Eq.
S443
and find

\displaystyle g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi\xi^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},\tau)=g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi\xi^{\prime},i}(C_{3z}\mathbf{k},\tau)e^{i\frac{2\pi}{3}(\xi^{\prime}-\xi)}\quad,\quad g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}^{\xi\xi^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},\tau)=g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}^{\xi\xi^{\prime},i}(C_{3z}\mathbf{k},\tau)

\displaystyle g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}^{\xi\xi^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},\tau)=g_{c^{\prime}c^{\prime\prime}}^{\xi\xi^{\prime},i}(C_{3z}\mathbf{k},\tau)e^{i\frac{2\pi}{3}(-\xi)}\quad,\quad g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi\xi^{\prime},i}(\mathbf{k},\tau)=g_{c^{\prime\prime}c^{\prime}}^{\xi\xi^{\prime},i}(C_{3z}\mathbf{k},\tau)e^{i\frac{2\pi}{3}(\xi^{\prime})}\,.

(S503)

Then combining Eq.
S503
and Eq.
S397
, we have

\displaystyle\chi^{A}_{\xi-,\xi^{\prime}-}(\mathbf{q},i\omega=0,2,1)=\chi^{A}_{\xi-,\xi^{\prime}-}(C_{3z}\mathbf{q},i\omega=0,2,1)e^{i2\pi/3(\xi^{\prime}-\xi)}

(S504)

Thus using Eq.
S397
, Eq.
S501
and Eq.
S518

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}^{\nu_{f}=-1,fh}(\mathbf{q})_{\xi,\xi^{\prime}}={\hat{H}}^{\nu_{f}=-1,fh}(C_{3z}\mathbf{q})_{\xi,\xi^{\prime}}e^{i2\pi/3(\xi^{\prime}-\xi)}

(S505)

In the long-wavelength limit, we assume
H_{1}^{\nu_{f}=1,fh}(\mathbf{q})
takes the form of

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}_{1}^{\nu_{f}=1,fh}(\mathbf{q})_{\xi\xi^{\prime}}=\begin{bmatrix}C_{0,0}^{fh}+C_{\mu,1,0}^{fh}q_{\mu}+C_{\mu\nu,2,0}^{fh}q_{\mu}q_{\nu}&C_{0,1}^{fh}+C_{\mu,1,1}^{fh}q_{\mu}+C_{\mu\nu,2,1}^{fh}q_{\mu}q_{\nu}\\
C_{0,3}^{fh}+C_{\mu,1,3}^{fh}q_{\mu}+C_{\mu\nu,2,3}^{fh}q_{\mu}q_{\nu}&C_{0,4}^{fh}+C_{\mu,1,4}^{fh}q_{\mu}+C_{\mu\nu,2,4}^{fh}q_{\mu}q_{\nu}\end{bmatrix}_{\xi\xi^{\prime}}

(S506)

Combining Eq.
S506
and Eq.
S505
we find

\displaystyle C_{0,1}^{fh}=e^{-i4\pi/3}C_{0,1}^{fh},\quad C_{0,3}^{fh}=e^{i4\pi/3}C_{0,3}^{fh}

\displaystyle C_{x,1,0}^{fh}=-\frac{1}{2}C_{x,1,0}^{fh}+\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}C_{y,1,0}^{fh},\quad C_{y,1,0}^{fh}=-\frac{-\sqrt{3}}{2}C_{x,1,0}^{fh}-\frac{1}{2}C_{y,1,0}^{fh}

\displaystyle C_{x,1,4}^{fh}=-\frac{1}{2}C_{x,1,4}^{fh}+\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}C_{y,1,4}^{fh},\quad C_{y,1,4}^{fh}=-\frac{-\sqrt{3}}{2}C_{x,1,4}^{fh}-\frac{1}{2}C_{y,1,4}^{fh}

\displaystyle C_{x,1,1}^{fh}=(-\frac{1}{2}C_{x,1,1}^{fh}+\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}C_{y,1,1}^{fh})e^{-i4\pi/3},\quad C_{y,1,1}^{fh}=(-\frac{-\sqrt{3}}{2}C_{x,1,1}^{fh}-\frac{1}{2}C_{y,1,1}^{fh})e^{-i4\pi/3}

\displaystyle C_{x,1,3}^{fh}=(-\frac{1}{2}C_{x,1,3}^{fh}+\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}C_{y,1,3}^{fh})e^{i4\pi/3},\quad C_{y,1,3}^{fh}=(-\frac{-\sqrt{3}}{2}C_{x,1,3}^{fh}-\frac{1}{2}C_{y,1,3}^{fh})e^{i4\pi/3}

\displaystyle C_{xx,2,0/4}^{fh}=\frac{C_{xx,2,0/4}^{fh}+3C_{yy,2,0/4}^{fh}-\sqrt{3}C_{xy,2,0/4}^{fh}-\sqrt{3}C_{yx,2,0/4}^{fh}}{4}

\displaystyle C_{yy,2,0/4}^{fh}=\frac{3C_{xx,2,0/4}^{fh}+C_{yyv}^{fh}+\sqrt{3}C_{xy,2,0/4}^{fh}+\sqrt{3}C_{yx,2,0/4}^{fh}}{4}

\displaystyle C_{xy,2,0/4}^{fh}=\frac{\sqrt{3}C_{xx,2,0/4}^{fh}-\sqrt{3}C_{yy,2,0/4}^{fh}+C_{xy,2,0/4}^{fh}-3C_{yx,2,0/4}^{fh}}{4}

\displaystyle C_{yx,2,0/4}^{fh}=\frac{\sqrt{3}C_{xx,2,0/4}^{fh}-\sqrt{3}C_{yy,2,0/4}^{fh}-3C_{xy,2,0/4}^{fh}+C_{yx,2,0/4}^{fh}}{4}

\displaystyle C_{xx,2,1}^{fh}=\frac{C_{xx,2,1}^{fh}+3C_{yy,2,1}^{fh}-\sqrt{3}C_{xy,2,1}^{fh}-\sqrt{3}C_{yx,2,1}^{fh}}{4}e^{-i4\pi/3}

\displaystyle C_{yy,2,1}^{fh}=\frac{3C_{xx,2,1}^{fh}+C_{yy,n,0}^{fh}+\sqrt{3}C_{xy,2,1}^{fh}+\sqrt{3}C_{yx,2,1}^{fh}}{4}e^{-i4\pi/3},

\displaystyle C_{xy,2,1}^{fh}=\frac{\sqrt{3}C_{xx,2,1}^{fh}-\sqrt{3}C_{yy,2,1}^{fh}+C_{xy,2,1}^{fh}-3C_{yx,2,1}^{fh}}{4}e^{-i4\pi/3}

\displaystyle C_{yx,2,1}^{fh}=\frac{\sqrt{3}C_{xx,2,1}^{fh}-\sqrt{3}C_{yy,2,1}^{fh}-3C_{xy,2,1}^{fh}+C_{yx,2,1}^{fh}}{4}e^{-i4\pi/3}

\displaystyle C_{xx,2,3}^{fh}=\frac{C_{xx,2,3}^{fh}+3C_{yy,2,1}^{fh}-\sqrt{3}C_{xy,2,1}^{fh}-\sqrt{3}C_{yx,2,3}^{fh}}{4}e^{i4\pi/3}

\displaystyle C_{yy,2,3}^{fh}=\frac{3C_{xx,2,1}^{fh}+C_{yy,n,0}^{fh}+\sqrt{3}C_{xy,2,3}^{fh}+\sqrt{3}C_{yx,2,3}^{fh}}{4}e^{i4\pi/3},

\displaystyle C_{xy,2,3}^{fh}=\frac{\sqrt{3}C_{xx,2,3}^{fh}-\sqrt{3}C_{yy,2,3}^{fh}+C_{xy,2,3}^{fh}-3C_{yx,2,3}^{fh}}{4}e^{i4\pi/3},

\displaystyle C_{yx,2,3}^{fh}=\frac{\sqrt{3}C_{xx,2,3}^{fh}-\sqrt{3}C_{yy,2,3}^{fh}-3C_{xy,2,3}^{fh}+C_{yx,2,3}^{fh}}{4}e^{i4\pi/3}

(S507)

We also utilize the fact that
H_{1}^{\nu_{f}=1,fh}(\mathbf{q})
is a Hermitian matrix (from Eq.
S501
and Eq.
S400
). Then we find the non-vanishing components are

\displaystyle C_{0,0}^{fh},\quad C_{xx/yy,2,0}^{fh},\quad C_{0,4}^{fh},\quad C_{xx/yy,2,4}^{fh},\quad C_{\mu,1,3},\quad C_{\mu,1,1}^{fh},\quad C_{\mu,1,3}^{fh}

(S508)

with

\displaystyle C_{0,0}^{fh}\in\mathbb{R},\quad C_{0,4}^{fh}\in\mathbb{R},\quad C_{\mu\mu,2,0}^{fh}\in\mathbb{R},\quad C_{\mu\mu,2,4}^{fh}\in\mathbb{R}

\displaystyle C_{xx,2,0}^{fh}=C_{yy,2,4}^{fh},\quad C_{xx,2,0}^{fh}=C_{yy,2,4}^{fh}

\displaystyle C_{y,1,1}^{fh}=-iC_{x,1,1}^{fh}=-C_{y,1,3}^{fh}=-iC_{x,1,3}^{fh}

(S509)

Thus we have

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}^{\nu_{f}=1,fh}(\mathbf{q})_{\xi\xi^{\prime}}=\begin{bmatrix}C_{0,0}^{fh}+C_{xx,2,0}^{fh}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}&C_{x,1,1}^{fh}(q_{x}-iq_{y})\\
C_{x,1,1}^{fh}(q_{x}+iq_{y})&C_{0,4}^{fh}+C_{xx,2,4}^{fh}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}\end{bmatrix}_{\xi\xi^{\prime}}

(S510)

We next use Eq.
S438
(we take
i=1,j=2,\xi_{2}=+,\xi_{2}^{\prime}=-
) and Eq.
S496
, and find

\displaystyle H^{\nu_{f}=1,fh}(\mathbf{q}=0)_{--}=0\Rightarrow C_{0,4}^{fh}=0

(S511)

Then we have

\displaystyle H^{\nu_{f}=1,fh}(\mathbf{q})_{\xi\xi^{\prime}}=\begin{bmatrix}C_{0,0}^{fh}+C_{xx,2,0}^{fh}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}&C_{x,1,1}^{fh}(q_{x}-iq_{y})\\
C_{x,1,1}^{fh}(q_{x}+iq_{y})&C_{xx,2,4}^{fh}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}\end{bmatrix}_{\xi\xi^{\prime}}

(S512)

where we observe
C^{fh}_{0,4}=0
produces a Goldstone mode. The eigenvalues of Eq.
S512
are

\displaystyle E(\mathbf{q})=\frac{C_{0,0}^{fh}+C_{xx,2,0}^{fh}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}+C_{xx,2,4}^{fh}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}}{2}\pm\sqrt{\bigg(\frac{C_{0,0}^{fh}+C_{xx,2,0}^{fh}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}-C_{xx,2,4}^{fh}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}}{2}\bigg)^{2}+|C_{x,1,1}^{fh}|^{2}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}}

(S513)

S8.13.3
Half-empty sector

For the half-empty sector, we take
i=2,j=3,4
, where
i=2
flavor is filled with two
f
electrons, and
j=3,4
flavors are empty (Eq.
S335
). Then

\displaystyle S_{eff,off,he}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi N_{M}}\int\sum_{\mathbf{q}}\sum_{j\in\{3,4\}}\sum_{\xi,\xi_{2}}u(\mathbf{q},i\omega)_{2+,j\xi}u(-\mathbf{q},-i\omega)_{j\xi_{2},2+}\bigg\{i\omega\delta_{\xi_{2},\xi}-F^{2j}_{+\xi,+\xi_{2}}(\mathbf{q})\bigg\}d\omega,\quad j=3,4

(S514)

The
U(2)
symmetry that act on the valley-spin flavors
i=3,4
indicates

\displaystyle-\frac{N_{2+}-N_{j\xi}}{2}=-\frac{N_{2+}-N_{j^{\prime}\xi}}{2}\quad,\quad j,j^{\prime}\in\{3,4\}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\bigg(\chi^{A}_{+\xi,+\xi_{2}}(\mathbf{q},i\omega=0,i=2,j)+\chi^{A}_{\xi_{2}+,\xi+}(-\mathbf{q},i\omega=0,j,i=2)\bigg)=\frac{1}{4}\bigg(

\displaystyle\chi^{A}_{+\xi,+\xi_{2}}(\mathbf{q},i\omega=0,i=2,j^{\prime})+\chi^{A}_{\xi_{2}+,\xi+}(-\mathbf{q},i\omega=0,j^{\prime},i=2)\bigg)\quad,\quad j,j^{\prime}\in\{3,4\}

(S515)

Therefore,
F^{23}_{+\xi,+\xi_{2}}(\mathbf{q})=F^{24}_{+\xi,+\xi_{2}}(\mathbf{q})
.
We can introduce the following matrix

\displaystyle H^{\nu_{f}=-1,he}(\mathbf{q})_{\xi,\xi_{2}}=F^{2j}_{+\xi,+\xi_{2}}(\mathbf{q})\quad,\quad j\in\{3,4\}

(S516)

which is a
2\times 2
matrix and does not depend on
j
.
The effective action can be written as

\displaystyle S_{eff,off,he}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi N_{M}}\int\sum_{\mathbf{q}}\sum_{j\in\{3,4\}}\sum_{\xi,\xi_{2}}u(\mathbf{q},i\omega)_{2+,j\xi}u(-\mathbf{q},-i\omega)_{j\xi_{2},2+}[i\omega\delta_{\xi,\xi_{2}}-H^{\nu_{f}=-1,he}(\mathbf{q})_{\xi,\xi_{2}}]d\omega

(S517)

Wround state at
\nu_{f}=-3
(Eq.
S335
) has
C_{3z}
symmetry.
We combine Eq.
S503
and Eq.
S397
, and find
(j=3,4)

\displaystyle\chi^{A}_{+\xi,+\xi_{2}}(\mathbf{q},i\omega=0,j,2)=\chi^{A}_{+\xi,+\xi_{2}}(C_{3z}\mathbf{q},i\omega=0,j,2)e^{i2\pi/3(\xi^{\prime}-\xi)}

(S518)

Thus using Eq.
S397
, Eq.
S501
and Eq.
S518

\displaystyle H^{\nu_{f}=-1,he}(\mathbf{q})_{\xi,\xi_{2}}=H^{\nu_{f}=-1,he}(C_{3z}\mathbf{q})_{\xi,\xi_{2}}e^{i2\pi/3(\xi-\xi_{2})}

(S519)

We next study the long-wavelength limit, we assume
H_{1}^{\nu_{f}=1,fh}(\mathbf{q})
takes the form of

\displaystyle H^{\nu_{f}=1,he}(\mathbf{q})_{\xi\xi_{2}}=\begin{bmatrix}C_{0,0}^{fe}+C_{\mu,1,0}^{fe}q_{\mu}+C_{\mu\nu,2,0}^{fh}q_{\mu}q_{\nu}&C_{0,1}^{fe}+C_{\mu,1,1}^{fe}q_{\mu}+C_{\mu\nu,2,1}^{fe}q_{\mu}q_{\nu}\\
C_{0,3}^{fh}+C_{\mu,1,3}^{fe}q_{\mu}+C_{\mu\nu,2,3}^{fe}q_{\mu}q_{\nu}&C_{0,4}^{fh}+C_{\mu,1,4}^{fe}q_{\mu}+C_{\mu\nu,2,4}^{fe}q_{\mu}q_{\nu}\end{bmatrix}_{\xi\xi 2}

(S520)

with row and column indices
\{+,-\}
.
Combining Eq.
S520
and Eq.
S519
we find

\displaystyle C_{0,1}^{he}=e^{-i4\pi/3}C_{0,1}^{he},\quad C_{0,3}^{he}=e^{i4\pi/3}C_{0,3}^{he}

\displaystyle C_{x,1,0}^{he}=-\frac{1}{2}C_{x,1,0}^{he}+\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}C_{y,1,0}^{he},\quad C_{y,1,0}^{he}=-\frac{-\sqrt{3}}{2}C_{x,1,0}^{he}-\frac{1}{2}C_{y,1,0}^{he}

\displaystyle C_{x,1,4}^{he}=-\frac{1}{2}C_{x,1,4}^{he}+\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}C_{y,1,4}^{he},\quad C_{y,1,4}^{he}=-\frac{-\sqrt{3}}{2}C_{x,1,4}^{he}-\frac{1}{2}C_{y,1,4}^{he}

\displaystyle C_{x,1,1}^{he}=(-\frac{1}{2}C_{x,1,1}^{he}+\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}C_{y,1,1}^{he})e^{i4\pi/3},\quad C_{y,1,1}^{he}=(-\frac{-\sqrt{3}}{2}C_{x,1,1}^{he}-\frac{1}{2}C_{y,1,1}^{he})e^{i4\pi/3}

\displaystyle C_{x,1,3}^{he}=(-\frac{1}{2}C_{x,1,3}^{he}+\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}C_{y,1,3}^{he})e^{-i4\pi/3},\quad C_{y,1,3}^{he}=(-\frac{-\sqrt{3}}{2}C_{x,1,3}^{he}-\frac{1}{2}C_{y,1,3}^{he})e^{-i4\pi/3}

\displaystyle C_{xx,2,0/4}^{he}=\frac{C_{xx,2,0/4}^{he}+3C_{yy,2,0/4}^{he}-\sqrt{3}C_{xy,2,0/4}^{he}-\sqrt{3}C_{yx,2,0/4}^{he}}{4}

\displaystyle C_{yy,2,0/4}^{he}=\frac{3C_{xx,2,0/4}^{he}+C_{yyv}^{he}+\sqrt{3}C_{xy,2,0/4}^{he}+\sqrt{3}C_{yx,2,0/4}^{he}}{4}

\displaystyle C_{xy,2,0/4}^{he}=\frac{\sqrt{3}C_{xx,2,0/4}^{he}-\sqrt{3}C_{yy,2,0/4}^{he}+C_{xy,2,0/4}^{he}-3C_{yx,2,0/4}^{he}}{4}

\displaystyle C_{yx,2,0/4}^{he}=\frac{\sqrt{3}C_{xx,2,0/4}^{he}-\sqrt{3}C_{yy,2,0/4}^{he}-3C_{xy,2,0/4}^{he}+C_{yx,2,0/4}^{he}}{4}

\displaystyle C_{xx,2,1}^{he}=\frac{C_{xx,2,1}^{he}+3C_{yy,2,1}^{he}-\sqrt{3}C_{xy,2,1}^{he}-\sqrt{3}C_{yx,2,1}^{he}}{4}e^{i4\pi/3}

\displaystyle C_{yy,2,1}^{he}=\frac{3C_{xx,2,1}^{he}+C_{yy,n,0}^{he}+\sqrt{3}C_{xy,2,1}^{he}+\sqrt{3}C_{yx,2,1}^{he}}{4}e^{i4\pi/3},

\displaystyle C_{xy,2,1}^{he}=\frac{\sqrt{3}C_{xx,2,1}^{he}-\sqrt{3}C_{yy,2,1}^{he}+C_{xy,2,1}^{he}-3C_{yx,2,1}^{he}}{4}e^{i4\pi/3}

\displaystyle C_{yx,2,1}^{he}=\frac{\sqrt{3}C_{xx,2,1}^{he}-\sqrt{3}C_{yy,2,1}^{he}-3C_{xy,2,1}^{he}+C_{yx,2,1}^{he}}{4}e^{-i4\pi/3}

\displaystyle C_{xx,2,3}^{he}=\frac{C_{xx,2,3}^{he}+3C_{yy,2,1}^{he}-\sqrt{3}C_{xy,2,1}^{he}-\sqrt{3}C_{yx,2,3}^{he}}{4}e^{-i4\pi/3}

\displaystyle C_{yy,2,3}^{he}=\frac{3C_{xx,2,1}^{he}+C_{yy,n,0}^{he}+\sqrt{3}C_{xy,2,3}^{he}+\sqrt{3}C_{yx,2,3}^{he}}{4}e^{-i4\pi/3},

\displaystyle C_{xy,2,3}^{he}=\frac{\sqrt{3}C_{xx,2,3}^{he}-\sqrt{3}C_{yy,2,3}^{he}+C_{xy,2,3}^{he}-3C_{yx,2,3}^{he}}{4}e^{-i4\pi/3},

\displaystyle C_{yx,2,3}^{he}=\frac{\sqrt{3}C_{xx,2,3}^{he}-\sqrt{3}C_{yy,2,3}^{he}-3C_{xy,2,3}^{he}+C_{yx,2,3}^{he}}{4}e^{-i4\pi/3}

(S521)

We also utilize the fact that
H^{\nu_{f}=1,he}(\mathbf{q})
is a Hermitian matrix (from Eq.
S519
and Eq.
S400
). Then we find the non-vanishing components are

\displaystyle C_{0,0}^{he},\quad C_{xx/yy,2,0}^{he},\quad C_{0,4}^{he},\quad C_{xx/yy,2,4}^{he},\quad C_{\mu,1,3},\quad C_{\mu,1,1}^{he},\quad C_{\mu,1,3}^{he}

(S522)

with

\displaystyle C_{0,0}^{he}\in\mathbb{R},\quad C_{0,4}^{he}\in\mathbb{R},\quad C_{\mu\mu,2,0}^{he}\in\mathbb{R},\quad C_{\mu\mu,2,4}^{he}\in\mathbb{R}

\displaystyle C_{xx,2,0}^{he}=C_{yy,2,4}^{he},\quad C_{xx,2,0}^{he}=C_{yy,2,4}^{he}

\displaystyle C_{y,1,1}^{he}=iC_{x,1,1}^{he}=-C_{y,1,3}^{he}=iC_{x,1,3}^{he}

(S523)

Thus we have

\displaystyle{\hat{H}}^{\nu_{f}=1,he}(\mathbf{q})_{\xi\xi^{\prime}}=\begin{bmatrix}C_{0,0}^{he}+C_{xx,2,0}^{he}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}&C_{x,1,1}^{he}(q_{x}+iq_{y})\\
C_{x,1,1}^{he}(q_{x}-iq_{y})&C_{0,4}^{he}+C_{xx,2,4}^{he}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}\end{bmatrix}_{\xi\xi^{\prime}}

(S524)

We next use Eq.
S438
(we let
i=2,j=3,\xi_{2}=+,\xi_{2}^{\prime}=+
) and Eq.
S517
, and find

\displaystyle H^{\nu_{f}=1,fh}(\mathbf{q}=0)_{++}=0\Rightarrow C_{0,0}^{fe}=0

(S525)

Then

\displaystyle H^{\nu_{f}=1,he}(\mathbf{q})_{\xi\xi^{\prime}}=\begin{bmatrix}C_{xx,2,0}^{he}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}&C_{x,1,1}^{he}(q_{x}+iq_{y})\\
C_{x,1,1}^{he}(q_{x}-iq_{y})&C_{0,4}^{he}+C_{xx,2,4}^{he}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}\end{bmatrix}_{\xi\xi^{\prime}}

(S526)

where we notice
C_{0,0}^{fe}=0=0
produces a Goldstone mode for each
\mathbf{q}
.
The eigenvalues of Eq.
S526
are

\displaystyle E(\mathbf{q})=\frac{C_{0,4}^{he}+C_{xx,2,4}^{he}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}+C_{xx,2,0}^{he}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}}{2}\pm\sqrt{\bigg(\frac{C_{0,4}^{he}+C_{xx,2,4}^{he}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}-C_{xx,2,0}^{he}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}}{2}\bigg)^{2}+|C_{x,1,1}^{he}|^{2}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}}

(S527)

S8.13.4
Half-half sector

For the half-half sector, we take
i=2,j=2
, where
i=j=2
flavor is filled with one
f
electron (Eq.
S335
). We then have (Eq.
S399
)

\displaystyle S_{eff,off,hh}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi N_{M}}\int\sum_{\mathbf{q}}u(\mathbf{q},i\omega)_{2+,2-}u(-\mathbf{q},-i\omega)_{2-,2+}[i\omega-F_{-+,-+}^{22}(\mathbf{q})]d\omega

(S528)

We introduce
H^{\nu_{f}=-1,hh}(\mathbf{q})

\displaystyle H^{\nu_{f}=-1,hh}(\mathbf{q})=F_{-+,-+}^{22}(\mathbf{q})

(S529)

and have

\displaystyle S_{eff,off,hh}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\pi N_{M}}\int\sum_{\mathbf{q}}u(\mathbf{q},i\omega)_{2+,2-}u(-\mathbf{q},-i\omega)_{2-,2+}[i\omega-H^{\nu_{f}=-1,hh}(\mathbf{q})]d\omega

(S530)

From Eq.
S400
,
F_{-+,-+}^{22}(\mathbf{q})
and also
H^{\nu_{f}=-1,hh}(\mathbf{q})
is real.

We now discuss the effect of
C_{3z}
symmetry. From Eq.
S503
, Eq.
S397
and Eq.
S398
, we have

\displaystyle F_{-+,-+}^{22}(\mathbf{q})=F_{-+,-+}^{22}(C_{3z}\mathbf{q})

\displaystyle H^{\nu_{f}=-1,hh}(\mathbf{q})=H^{\nu_{f}=-1,hh}(C_{3z}\mathbf{q})

(S531)

We perform a small
\mathbf{q}
expansions of
H^{\nu_{f}=-1,hh}(\mathbf{q})

\displaystyle H^{\nu_{f}=-1,hh}(\mathbf{q})=C_{0}^{hh}+\sum_{\mu}C_{\mu,1}^{hh}q_{\mu}+\sum_{\mu\nu}C_{\mu\nu,2}^{hh}q_{\mu}q_{\nu}

(S532)

From Eq.
S531
, we find

\displaystyle C_{x,1}^{hh}=-\frac{1}{2}C_{x,1}^{hh}+\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}C_{y,1}^{hh},\quad C_{y,1}^{hh}=-\frac{-\sqrt{3}}{2}C_{x,1}^{hh}-\frac{1}{2}C_{y,1}^{hh}

\displaystyle C_{xx,2}^{hh}=\frac{C_{xx,2}^{hh}+3C_{yy,2}^{hh}-\sqrt{3}C_{xy,2}^{hh}-\sqrt{3}C_{yx,2}^{hh}}{4},\quad C_{yy,2}^{hh}=\frac{3C_{xx,2}^{hh}+C_{yy,2}^{hh}+\sqrt{3}C_{xy,2}^{hh}+\sqrt{3}C_{yx,2}^{hh}}{4}

\displaystyle C_{xy,2}^{hh}=\frac{\sqrt{3}C_{xx,2}^{hh}-\sqrt{3}C_{yy,2}^{hh}+C_{xy,2}^{hh}-3C_{yx,2}^{hh}}{4},\quad C_{yx,2}^{hh}=\frac{\sqrt{3}C_{xx,2}^{hh}-\sqrt{3}C_{yy,2}^{hh}-3C_{xy,2}^{hh}+C_{yx,2}^{hh}}{4}

The non-zero components are

\displaystyle C_{0}^{hh},C_{xx,2}^{hh},C_{yy,2}^{hh}\,,

(S534)

and we also have

\displaystyle C_{0}^{hh}\in\mathbb{R},\quad C_{xx,2}^{hh}\in\mathbb{R}\quad,C_{yy,2}^{hh}\in\mathbb{R}

\displaystyle C_{xx,2}^{hh}=C_{yy,2}^{hh}\,.

(S535)

Then we have

\displaystyle H^{\nu_{f}=-1,hh}(\mathbf{q})=C_{0}^{hh}+C_{xx,2}^{hh}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}

(S536)

S8.13.5
Effective theory in the long-wavelength limit

Here, we summarize the effective theory in the long-wavelength limit at
\nu_{f}=-1
. From Eq.
S496
, Eq.
S502
, Eq.
S517
and Eq.
S530

\displaystyle S_{eff,off}=S_{eff,off,fe}+S_{eff,off,he}+S_{eff,off,fh}+S_{eff,off,hh}

\displaystyle S_{eff,off,fe}=\frac{1}{2\pi N_{M}}\int\sum_{\mathbf{q}}\sum_{i\in\{1\},j\in\{3,4\}}\sum_{\xi,\xi_{2}^{\prime},\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}}u(\mathbf{q},i\omega)_{i\xi,j\xi^{\prime}}u(-\mathbf{q},-i\omega)_{j\xi_{2},i\xi_{2}^{\prime}}\bigg\{i\omega\delta_{\xi_{2},\xi^{\prime}}\delta_{\xi_{2}^{\prime},\xi}-H^{\nu_{f}=-1,fe}(\mathbf{q})_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}}\bigg\}d\omega

\displaystyle S_{eff,off,fh}=\frac{1}{2\pi N_{M}}\int\sum_{\mathbf{q}}\sum_{\xi,\xi^{\prime}}u(\mathbf{q},i\omega)_{1\xi,2-}u(-\mathbf{q},-i\omega)_{2-,1\xi^{\prime}}\bigg\{i\omega\delta_{\xi,\xi^{\prime}}-H^{\nu_{f}=-1,fh}(\mathbf{q})_{\xi,\xi^{\prime}}\bigg\}d\omega

\displaystyle S_{eff,off,he}=\frac{1}{2\pi N_{M}}\int\sum_{\mathbf{q}}\sum_{j\in\{3,4\}}\sum_{\xi,\xi_{2}}u(\mathbf{q},i\omega)_{2+,j\xi}u(-\mathbf{q},-i\omega)_{j\xi_{2},2+}\bigg\{i\omega\delta_{\xi,\xi_{2}}-H^{\nu_{f}=-1,he}(\mathbf{q})_{\xi,\xi_{2}}\bigg\}d\omega

\displaystyle S_{eff,off,hh}=\frac{1}{2\pi N_{M}}\int\sum_{\mathbf{q}}u(\mathbf{q},i\omega)_{2+,2-}u(-\mathbf{q},-i\omega)_{2-,2+}\bigg\{i\omega-H^{\nu_{f}=-1,hh}(\mathbf{q})\bigg\}d\omega

(S537)

where (from Eq.
S512
, Eq.
S526
and Eq.
S536
)

\displaystyle H^{\nu_{f}=-1,fe}=\begin{bmatrix}C^{fe}_{0,0}+C^{fe}_{2,0}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}&-C^{fe}_{0,0}+C^{fe}_{2,2}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}&C^{fe}_{1,1}(-q_{x}-iq_{y})&C^{fe}_{1,1}(q_{x}-iq_{y})\\
-C^{fe}_{0,0}+C^{fe}_{2,2}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}&C^{fe}_{0,0}+C^{fe}_{2,0}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}&C^{fe}_{1,1}(q_{x}+iq_{y})&C^{fe}_{1,1}(-q_{x}+iq_{y})\\
C^{fe}_{1,1}(-q_{x}+iq_{y})&C^{fe}_{1,1}(q_{x}-iq_{y})&C^{fe}_{0,1}+C^{fe}_{2,1}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}&C^{fe}_{2,3}(q_{x}^{2}-q_{y}^{2}-2iq_{x}q_{y})\\
C^{fe}_{1,1}(q_{x}+iq_{y})&C^{fe}_{1,1}(-q_{x}-iq_{y})&C^{fe}_{2,3}(q_{x}^{2}-q_{y}^{2}+2iq_{x}q_{y})&C^{fe}_{0,1}+C^{fe}_{2,1}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle H^{\nu_{f}=1,fh}(\mathbf{q})_{\xi\xi^{\prime}}=\begin{bmatrix}C_{0,0}^{fh}+C_{xx,2,0}^{fh}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}&C_{x,1,1}^{fh}(q_{x}-iq_{y})\\
C_{x,1,1}^{fh}(q_{x}+iq_{y})&C_{xx,2,4}^{fh}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}\end{bmatrix}_{\xi\xi^{\prime}}

\displaystyle H^{\nu_{f}=1,he}(\mathbf{q})_{\xi\xi^{\prime}}=\begin{bmatrix}C_{xx,2,0}^{he}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}&C_{x,1,1}^{he}(q_{x}+iq_{y})\\
C_{x,1,1}^{he}(q_{x}-iq_{y})&C_{0,4}^{he}+C_{xx,2,4}^{he}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}\end{bmatrix}_{\xi\xi^{\prime}}

\displaystyle H^{\nu_{f}=-1,hh}(\mathbf{q})=C_{0}^{hh}+C_{xx,2}^{hh}|\mathbf{q}|^{2}

(S538)

(We note that
H^{\nu_{f}=-1,fe}
takes the same structure as
H^{\nu_{f}=0,-2}(\mathbf{q})
in Eq.
S464
). We provide the numerical values of the parameters in Tab.
S2
. In Fig.
S6
(a), we also compare the dispersion from effective model
S537
with the dispersion from directly evaluating Eq.
S399
. We note that the effective model (Eq.
S537
) correctly predicts the long wavelength (small
\mathbf{k}
) behaviors. In addition, we observe a soft mode with a tiny gap in the half-half sector, as shown in Fig.
S6
(b). Here we also provide the expression of the gap
\Delta_{hh}(=C_{0}^{hh})
in the half-half sector. Using Eq.
S536
and Eq.
S530

\displaystyle\Delta=C_{0}^{hh}=H^{\nu_{f}=-1,hh}(\mathbf{q}=0)=F_{-+,-+}^{22}(\mathbf{q}=0)

(S539)

Using the expression of
F_{-+,-+}^{22}(\mathbf{q})
(Eq.
S398
), we have

\displaystyle\Delta=\frac{N_{2-}-N_{2+}}{2}-\frac{1}{4}\bigg(\chi^{A}_{-+,-+}(\mathbf{q}=0,i\omega=0,2,2)+\chi^{A}_{+-,+-}(\mathbf{q}=0,i\omega=0,2,2)\bigg)

(S540)

where
N_{i\xi},\chi_{\xi\xi^{\prime},\xi_{2}^{\prime}\xi_{2}}^{A}(\mathbf{q},i\omega,i,j)
are defined in Eq.
S540
. A direct numerical evaluation of Eq.
S540
gives
\Delta\approx 0.1
meV, which indicates a small gap at
\Gamma_{M}
.
